gānaka
dasaveṃ guṇasthāna se girakara jīva nauveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ hī ātā hai, jisase tīsarā bhūyaskāra bandha bhī nahīṃ bana sakatā hai|
ukta kathana kā sārāṃśa yaha hai ki ūpara ke guṇasthāna se patana ekadama na hokara kramaśaḥ gyārahaveṃ, dasaveṃ, nauve ādi meṃ hotā hai, ataḥ patana kī apekṣā eka ko bāṃdhakara sāta kā bandha karanā, eka ko bāṃdha. kara āṭha karmoṃ kā bandha karanā, chaha ko bāṃdhakara āṭha karmoṃ kā bandha karanā yaha tīnoṃ bhūyaskāra baṃdha nahīṃ banate haiṃ|
aba rahā maraṇa kī apekṣā ādi ke do bhūyaskāra baṃdhoṃ kā ho sakanā | so gyāraha sthāna meṃ jā karake bīca gati meṃ hai janma letā hai| aura vahāṃ baha sāta hī karmoṃ kā baṃdha karatā hai, kyoṃki devagati meṃ chaha māsa kī āsu śeṣa rahane para hī āyu kā bandha hotā hai| ataḥ maraṇa kī apekṣā se eka kā bandha karake āṭha kā bandha kara sakanā saṃbhava nahīṃ hai| isaliye yaha bhūyaskāra baṃdha nahīṃ ho sakatā hai| kintu eka ko bāṃdhakara sāta kā baṃdha rūpa bhūyaskāra saṃbhava hai, lekina usake bāre meṃ yaha jñātavya hai ki jo eka ko bāṃdhakara sāta karmī kā bandha karatā hai to bandhasthāna sāta kā hī rahatā hai, isaliye usako judā nahīṃ ginā jātā hai| yadi baṃdhasthāna kā bheda hotā to 1 badāū paḍivāno seṭiṃgao va pasaṃtamoho bā | jaha kuṇai koi kālaṃ vaccai to'ṇuttarasurasu ||

alpatara bandha
bhūyaskāra bandha se nitānta ulaṭā alpatara baṃdha hotā hai| adhika karmoṃ kā baṃdha karake kama karmoṃ ke baṃdha karane ko alpatara baṃdha kahate haiṃ | alpatara baṃdha bhī bhūyaskāra baṃdha kī taraha tīna hī hote haiṃ| ve isa prakāra haiṃ
āyu karma ke baṃdha kāla meṃ āṭha karmoṃ kā baṃdha karake jaba jīva sāta karmoṃ kā baṃdha karatā hai taba pahalā alpatara baṃdha hotā hai| nauveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ sāta karmoṃ kā baṃdha karake dasaveṃ guṇasthāna ke prathama samaya meṃ jaba jīva mohanīya ke binā śeṣa chaha karmoṃ kā baṃdha karatā hai taba dūsarā alpatara baṃdha hotā hai tathā dasarve guṇasthāna meṃ chaha karmoṃ kā baṃdha karake gyārahaveṃ yā bārahaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ eka karma kā baṃdha karatā hai taba tīsarā alpatara baṃdha hotā hai | yahā~ bhī āṭha kā baṃdha karake chaha tathā eka kā baṃdha rūpa tathā sāta kā baṃdha karake eka kā baṃdha rūpa alpatara baṃdha nahīṃ ho sakate haiṃ| kyoṃki apramatta aura anivṛttikaraṇa guṇasthāna se jīva ekadama gyārahaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ nahīṃ jā sakatā hai aura na apramatta se ekadama dasaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ jātā hai| ataḥ alpatara baṃdha bhī tīna hī jānanā cāhie |
bhūyaskāra aura alpatara baṃdhoṃ meṃ itanā antara hai ki guṇasthāna meṃ patana ke samaya bhūyaskāra baṃdha aura ārohaṇa ke samaya alpatara bandha hote haiṃ | lekina guṇasthānoṃ meṃ ārohaṇa aura avarohaṇa krama-krama se

hotā hai, ekadama nahīṃ, ataḥ donoṃ baṃdhoṃ ke tīna-tīna bheda hote haiṃ | anya vikalpoṃ kī hāra nahīṃ hai :

pahale samaya meṃ jitane karmoṃ kā baṃdha kiyā hai, dūsare samaya meṃ bhī utane hī kamoṃ ke baṃdha karane ko avasthita baṃdha kahate haiṃ| arthāt āṭha ko bāṃdha kara āṭha kā, sāta ko bāṃdha kara sāta kā, chaha ko bāṃdha kara chaha kā aura eka ko bāṃdha kara eka kā baṃdha karane ko avasthita baṃdha kahate haiṃ | baṃdhasthāna cāra haiṃ, ataḥ avasthita baṃdha bhī cāra hote haiṃ| avaktavya bandha ___ eka bhī karma ko na bāṃdhakara punaḥ karma baṃdha ko avaktavya baṃdha kahate haiṃ | yaha baṃdha mūla karmoṃ ke baṃdhasthānoṃ meṃ nahīṃ hotā hai | kyoṃki terahaveṃ guṇasthāna taka to barābara karmabaṃdha hotā rahatā hai lekina caudahaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ hī kisī karma kā baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai aura caudahaveṃ muṇasthāna meṃ pahu~cane ke bāda jīva lauṭakara nīce ke guṇasthāna meṃ nahīṃ ātā hai jisase eka bhī karma kā bandha nahīṃ karane se punaḥ karmabaṃdha karane kā abasara hī nahīṃ ātā hai | isīliye mūla kama prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ avaktavya baṃdha bhī nahīṃ hotā hai|'

paṃcama kramaprantha
śabdārtha--egāvalige – ekā di adhika prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hone se, mo-- bhūpaskāra baṃdha, egāīūṇammi --ekāṭi prakṛla ke dvārā hīna badha hona se, appataro- alpata dha, tammaso - utanī prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hone se, avaṭipao--avasthita badha, paḍhamesamaeabandhaka hone ke bāda punarbandha ke pahale samaya meṃ, avattayoavaktavya bandha|
gāthārtha-ekādi adhika prakṛtiyāṃ kā bandha hone se bhūyaskāra bandha hotā hai | ekādi prakṛtiyo ke dvārā hīna baṃdha hone para alpatara bandha aura utanī hī prakṛtiyoṃ kā bandha hoga se avasthita bandha hotā hai tathā nāka hone ke yā punaḥ baṃdha ke pahale samaya meṃ bandha ho, use avaktavya baṃdha kahate haiṃ|
viśeṣārga-gāthā meṃ bhayaskāra, alpatara, avasthita aura avaktavya baṃdha ke lakṣaṇa batalāye haiṃ|
bhavaskāra baṃdha kā lakṣaṇa batalāte hue kahā hai ki--gādahige bhūo'–eka, do ādi adhika prakṛtiyoṃ ke bāṃdhane para bhūvaskāra baṃdha hotā hai | arthāt jaise eka ko vā~dhakara chaha ko bāṃdhanā, chaha ko bāṃdha. kara sāta ko bā~dhanā aura sāta ko bāṃdhakara āṭha ko bāṃdhanā bhūyaskāra baṃdha hai|
lekina alpatara baṃdha bhūvaskāra baṃdha se ulaṭā hai| yānī 'egāīūṇagammi appataro'-eka, do ādi hīna prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karane para alpatara baṃdha hotā hai | arthāt jaise āṭha ko bāṃdhakara sāta ko bāṃdhanā, sāta ko bāṃdhakara chaha ko bāṃdhanā aura chaha ko bāṃdhakara eka ko bāṃdhanā alpatara vandha kahalātā hai |
avasthita baṃdha usa kahate hai .. tammato bāṃdvayao-- jisama pratisamaya samāna prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha ho adhauta pahale samaya meṃ jinane kamī kā bandha kiyā ho, āge ke samayoṃ meṃ bhī utane hī karmoṃ kā bandha

śavaka
**
karanā avasthita bandha kahalātā hai | jaise āṭha karma ko bāṃdhakara āṭha kā, sāta ko bāṃdhakara sāta kā chaha ko bāṃdhakara chaha kā, eka ko bāṃdhakara eka kā bandha karanā avasthita bandha hai aura kisī bhī karma kā bandha na karake punaḥ karma bandha karane para pahale samaya meṃ avaktavya bandha hotā hai- 'paḍhame samae avattavvo |'
bhūmaskāra āṇi baṃdhoṃ viṣayaka viśeṣa spaṣṭīkaraṇa
bhūyaskāra ādi ukta cāra prakāra ke baṃdhoṃ ke saṃbandha meṃ yaha viśeṣa jānanā cāhie ki bhuyaskāra, alpatara aura avaktavya bandha kevala pahale samaya meṃ hī hote haiṃ aura avasthita baṃdha dvitīya ādi samayoṃ meṃ hotā hai | jaise koī chaha karmoṃ kā baṃdha karake sāta kā baṃdha karatā hai to yaha bhūyaskāra baṃdha hai lekina dūsare samaya meṃ yahī bhūmaskāra nahīṃ ho sakatā hai kyoṃki prathama samaya meṃ sāta kā baṃdha karake yadi dūsare samaya meṃ āṭha kā baṃdha karatā hai to bhūyaskāra badala jātā hai aura chaha karma kā baṃdha karatā hai to alpatara ho jātā hai tathā sāta kā karatā hai to avasthita ho jātā hai |
-
ukta kathana kā sārāṃśa yaha hai ki prakṛtisaṃkhyā meṃ parivartana hue binā adhika bā~dhakara kama bāṃdhanā, kama bāṃdhakara adhika bāṃdhanā aura kucha bhī na bāṃdhakara punaḥ bāṃdhanā kevala eka bāra hī saṃbhava hai, jabaki pahalī bāra bāṃdhe hue karmoṃ ke barābara punaḥ utane hī karmoṃ ko bāṃdhanā punaḥ saṃbhava hai | ataḥ eka hī avasthita bandha lagātāra kaī samaya taka ho sakatā hai, kintu śeṣa tīna baṃdhoṃ meṃ yaha saṃbhava nahīṃ hai |
isa prakāra ke bhūyaskāra ādi baṃdhoṃ ke lakṣaṇa aura mūla karmoṃ meṃ unakī hone vālī saṃkhyā batalākara uttara prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ viśeṣa rūpa se kathana karane ke pūrva sāmānya se uttara prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ bhūyaskāra ādi cāroṃ baṃdhoṃ ko spaṣṭa karate haiṃ |
sāmānya se uttara prakṛtiyoṃ ke unatīsa baṃdhasthāna hote haiṃ | jo

śataka
strī, puruṣa, napuṃsaka ina tīna vedoṃ meṃ se eka samaya meṃ eka hī veda kā tathā hāsya-rati va śoka arati meṃ se eka samaya meṃ eka hī yugala kā baṃdha hotā hai| ataḥ mohanīya karma kī aṭṭhāīsa prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se samyagmithyātva samyaktva tathā tīna vedoṃ meṃ se koī do veda aura hāsya- rati, arati śoka, ina donoṃ yugaloṃ meṃ se koī eka yugala ko kama karane se kula chaha prakṛtiyoṃ ko kama kara dene para śeṣa bāīsa prakṛtiyāṃ hī eka samaya meṃ bandha ko prāpta hotī haiṃ| yaha pahalā baṃdhasthāna hai | isa baṃdhasthāna kī bāīsa prakṛtiyā~ isa prakāra haiṃ
mithyātva anantānubaṃdhī kapāya catuṣka, apratyākhyānāvaraṇa kaṣāya catuṣka, pratyākhyānāvaraṇa kaṣāva catuṣka, saṃjbalana kaṣāya catuṣka, eka veda, eka yugala, bhaya aura jugupsā | isa bāīsa prakṛti rūpa baṃdhasthāna kā bandha kevala pahale guṇasthāna meṃ hotā hai |
"
dūsare guṇasthāna meṃ mithyātva ke sivāya śeṣa ikkīsa prakṛtiyoṃ kā, tīsare, cauthe guṇasthāna meṃ anantānubaṃdhī krodha, māna, māyā, lobha ke sivāya śeṣa satraha kā pāṃcaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ apratyākhyānāvaraṇa kaṣāya catuṣka kā baṃdha na hone se śeṣa teraha prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hotā hai | ye kramaśaḥ dūsare, tīsare aura cauthe baṃdhasthāna haiṃ| isake anantara chaṭhe, sātaveṃ aura āṭhaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ pratyākhyānāvaraṇa kaṣāya catuṣka kā bandha na hone ke kāraṇa śeṣa nau prakṛtiyoṃ kā hī bandha hotā hai| āṭhaveṃ guṇasthāna ke anta meṃ hāsya, rati, bhaya aura jugupsā kā bandhaviccheda ho jāne se nauveṃ guṇasthāna ke prathama bhāga meṃ pāṃca prakṛtiyoṃ kā hī baṃdhasthāna hotā hai| dūsare bhāga meṃ veda kā abhāva ho jāne se cāra kā, tīsare bhāga meṃ saṃcalana krodha ke baṃdha kā abhāva ho jāne ke kāraṇa tīna hī prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hotā hai| cauthe bhāga meṃ saṃjvalana māna kā bandha na hone se do prakṛtiyoṃ kā bandhasthāna hai | pāṃcaveṃ bhāga meṃ saṃjvalana māyā kā bhī bandha na hone se kevala eka saṃjvalana lobha kā

hī bandha hotā hai | isake āge bādara kaṣāya kā abhāva ho jāne se saṃjvalana lobha prakṛti kā bhī baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai| isa prakāra mohanīya karma ke dasa bandhasthāna jānanā cāhiye | ina dasa baṃdhasthānoṃ meṃ nī bhūyaskāra, borḍa alpatara, dasa avasthita aura do avakatavya baṃdha hote haiṃ| jinakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa nīce kiyā jātā hai |
mohanīya karma ke bhūyaskāra ādi baṃdha - eka ko bāṃdha kara do kā baṃdha karane para pahalā bhūyaskāra baṃdha aura do ko bāṃdhakara tīna kā baṃdha karane para dūsarā bhūyaskāra baṃdha hotā hai| isī prakāra tīna ko bāṃdha kara cāra kā baṃdha karane para tīsarā, cāra ko bāṃdhakara pāṃca kā bandha karane para cauthā, pāṃca kā baṃdha karake nau kā baṃdha karane para pāṃcavāṃ, nī kā baṃdha karake teraha kā bandha karane para chaṭhā, teraha kā baṃdha karake sannaha kā baṃdha karane para sātavāṃ satraha kā bandha karake ikkīsa kā bandha karane para āṭhavāṃ aura ikkīsa kā bandha karake bāīsa kā bandha karane para nauvāṃ bhūyaskāra bandha hotā hai |
āṭha alpatara baṃdha isa prakāra haiṃ- bāīsa kā baṃdha karake sataha
1 go0 karmakāṃḍa meṃ mohanīya karma ke bhūjākārādi vaṃdhoṃ meṃ kucha antara hai, usameṃ adhika māne gaye haiṃ, jinakā vivaraṇa pariśiṣṭa meṃ diyā gayā hai|
2 mohanīya karma ke āṭha alpatara bandha hote haiṃ| bāīma kā bandha karake ikkīsa kā bandha rūpa alpatara bandha nahīṃ batāne kā kāraṇa yaha hai ki bāīsa kā bandha pahale guṇasthāna meṃ hotā hai aura ikkīsa kā bandha dūsare guṇasthāna meṃ | lekina pahale guṇasthāna se jīva dūsare guṇasthāna meṃ nahīṃ jātā hai| dūsarā guṇasthāna akrānti kī apekṣā se hai, utkrānti kī apekṣā se nahīṃ | yadi jīva pahale guṇasthāna se dūsare guṇasthāna meṃ jā sakatā to ikkīsa kā alpatara bandha bana sakatā thā| lekina mithyādṛṣṭi sāsā

111 nau dhruvabaṃdhinī, basa, bādara, paryāpta, pratyeka, sthira. yā asthira, __ śubha yā aśuma, ādeya. yaśa-kīrti yā ayaśa kīti, devagati, paṃcendriya jāti, vaikriya' śarīra, prathama saṃsthāna, devānupūrvī, vaikriya aṃgopāṃga, susvara, praśasta bihāyogati, ucchvāsa, parāghāta, tīrthakara, ina prakṛti rūpa devagati aura tīrthaṃkara sahita unatīsa kā baṃdhasthāna hotā hai| isa prakāra se unatīsa prakṛtikā vadhasthāna chaha hote haiṃ | ina sthānoṃ kā bandhaka dvīndriya, trīndriya, caturindriya aura paṃcendriya tiyaṃcoṃ meṃ tathā manuṣyagati aura devagati meṃ janma letā hai|
dvīndriya, trīndriya, caturindriya aura paṃcendriya paryāptayuta unatīsa ke cāra bandhasthānoṃ meṃ udyota prakṛti ke milāne se dvīndriya, trīndriya, caturindriya aura paṃcendriya paryāptayuta tīsa ke bāra baṃdhasthāna hote haiṃ | paryāpta manuṣya sahita unatīsa ke bandhasthāna meṃ tīrthaṃkara prajāti ke milāne se manuṣyagati sahita tīsa kā baṃdhasthāna hotā hai| devagati sahita unatīsa ke bandhasthāna meṃ se tīrthaṃkara prakṛti ghaṭākara āhārakadvika ko milāne se devagatiyuta tīsa kā vadhasthāna honā hai| isa prakāra tīsa prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna chaha hote haiṃ|
devagati sahita unatīsa ke baṃdhasthāna meṃ āhārakaddhika ke milāne se devagati sahita ikatīsa kā vandhasthāna hotā hai| eka prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna meṃ kevala eka yaśaḥīti kā hī bandha hotā hai|

hai | yahā~ bhinna bhinna bandhasthānoṃ kī apekṣā se bhayaskāro ke bhedoṃ kī vivakṣā nahīṃ kī hai, yadi vibhinna bandhasthānoṃ kī apekṣā vivakṣā kī jāye to bahuta se bhūskāra ho jāyeṃge| jaise kabhī aṭṭhāīsa kā baṃdha karake ikatīsa kā bandha karatā hai, kabhī unatīsa kā bandha karake ika tīsa kā bandha karatā hai aura kabhī eka kā bandha karake ikatīsa kā bandha karatā hai tathā kabhī teīsa kā bandha karake aṭṭhāīsa kā bandha karatā hai aura kabhī paccīsa kā bandha karake aṭṭhāīsa kā bandha karatā hai | isa prakāra sāta se bhī adhika bahuta se bhūyaskāra ho sakate haiṃ, jo yahā~ iṣṭa nahīṃ haiṃ | ataḥ bhinna-bhinna andhasthānoṃ kī apekṣā se bhūyaskāra ke bheda nahīṃ batāye haiṃ | isa prakāra se bhamaskāra bandha chaha hote haiṃ|
aba sāta alpatara baṃdha batalāte haiṃ | apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna meṃ deva - gati yogya 28, 2, 30 athavā 31 kā bandha karake 1 prakṛtika baṃdhasthāna kā bandha karane para pahalā alpatara baṃdha hotā hai | āhārakadvika aura tīrthaṃkara sahita ikatīsa kā baṃdha karake jo jīva devaloka meṃ utpanna hotā hai, vaha prathama samaya meṃ hī manuṣyagatiyuta tīsa prakṛtiyoṃ kā bandha karatā hai, yaha dūsarā alpatara bandha hai | vahī jīva svarga se cyuta hokara manuṣyagati meṃ janma lekara devagati yogya tīrthaṃkara sahita unatīsa prakṛtiyoṃ kā bandha karatā hai taba tīsarā alpavara baṃdha hotā hai | jaba koī tithaṃca yā manuṣya, tiyaṃcagati ke yogya pūrvokta unatīsa prakṛtiyoṃ kā bandha karake viśuddha pariṇāmoṃ ke kāraṇa devagati yogya aṭṭhāīsa prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karatā hai taba cauthā alpatara baṃdha, aṭṭhāīsa prakṛtika bandhasthāna kā bandha karake saṃkleśa pariṇāmoṃ ke kāraṇa jaba koī jīva ekendriya ke yogya chabbīsa prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karatā hai taba pā~cavāṃ alpatara bandha hotā hai | chabbīsa kā bandha karake paccīsa kā bandha karane para chaṭhā alpatara bandha hotā hai tathā paccīsa kā bandha karake teīsa kā bandha karane para sātavāṃ alpatara bagha hotā hai |

૪
śataka
āṭha bandhasthānoṃ kī apekṣā se āṭha hī avasthita bandha hote haiṃ |
gyārahaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ nāmakarma kī eka bhī prakṛti ko na bā~dhakara vahā~ se cyuta hokara jaba koī jona eka prakṛti kā baṃdha karatā hai taba pahalā avaktavya bandha hotā hai tathā gyārahaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ maraṇa karake koī jīva anuttara devoṃ meṃ janma lekara yadi manuṣyagati yogya tīsa prakṛti kā bandha karatā hai taba dūsarā avaktavya bandha hotā hai aura manuṣyagati yogya unatīsa prakṛti kā bandha karatā hai to tīsarā abaktavya bandha hotā hai | isa prakāra tīna avaktavya bandha hote haiṃ |"
isa prakāra se gāyā ke tīna caraṇoṃ meṃ nāmakarma ke baṃdhasthānoṃ aura unameṃ bhūyaskara ādi baṃdhoṃ kā nirdeśa karake śeṣa karmoṃ ke baṃdhasthānoṃ ko batalāne hetu gāthā ke cauthe caraṇa meṃ saṃketa diyā hai ki 'sesesu ya ṭhāṇamikkiṃ śeṣa pāṃca karmoṃ - jñānāvaraṇa, vedanīya, āyu, gotra, antarāya - meṃ eka-eka hī baṃdhasthāna hotā hai| kyoṃki śānāvaraṇa aura aṃtarāya kī pāṃca-pāṃca prakṛtiyāṃ eka sātha hī baṃdhatī haiṃ aura eka sātha hī rukatī haiṃ| vedanīya, āyu, gotra karma kī uttara prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ bhī eka samaya meṃ eka-eka prakṛti kā hī baṃdha hotā hai | jisase ina karmoṃ meṃ bhūyaskāra ādi baṃdha nahīṃ hote haiṃ| kyoṃki jahāṃ eka hī prakṛti kā baṃdha hotā hai, vahāṃ thoḍa़ī prakṛtiyoṃ ko bāṃdha kara adhika prakṛtiyoṃ ko bāṃdhanā yā adhika prakṛtiyoṃ ko bāṃdhakara thoḍa़ī prakṛtiyoṃ ko bāṃdhanā saṃbhava nahīṃ hotā hai |
———.
1 go0 karmakāṃḍa gā0 165 se 582 taka nānakarma ke bhūvaskāra ādi bandhoṃ kī vistāra se carcā kī hai| usameṃ guṇasthānoṃ kī apekṣā se bhūsaskāra ādibaṃdha batalāye haiṃ aura jitane prakṛtika sthāna ko rodhakara jitane prakṛtika sthānoṃ kī anya saṃbhava hai aura una una sthānoṃ meṃ jitane bhaṃga ho sakate haiṃ, una sabako apekṣā se sūskāra ādi ko batalāyā hai |

yaha eka sāmānya niyama hai kintu vedanīya ke sivāya śeṣa cāra karmoṃ meṃ avaktavya aura avasthita baṃdha hote haiṃ| kyoṃki gyārahaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ jñānāvaraṇa, aṃtarāya aura gotra karma kā baṃdha na karake jaba koī jīva vahāṃ se cyuta hotā hai aura nīce ke muṇasthāna meṃ ākara punaḥ una karmoṃ kā baṃdha karatā hai taba prathama samaya meṃ avaktavya baṃdha hotā hai aura dvitīya ādi samayoṃ meṃ avasthita baṃdha hotā hai tathā vibhāga meṃ jaba āyu karma kā baṃdha hotā hai taba prathama samaya meṃ avaktavya baṃdha hotā hai aura dvitīya ādi samayoṃ meṃ avasthita baṃdha hotā hai| kintu vedanīya karma meṃ kevala avasthita baṃdha hī hotā hai, avaktavya baṃdha nahīṃ | kyoṃki vedanīya karma kā abandha ayogi kevalī guṇasthāna meṃ hotā hai, kintu vahāṃ se girakara jīva ke nīce ke guṇasthāna meṃ nahīṃ āne ke kāraṇa punaḥ vaṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai|

111
jñānāvaraṇa, darśanāvaraṇa, mohanīya aura aṃtarāya kī aṃtamuhūrta, vedanīya kī bāraha muhūrta, āyu kī antarmuhūrta, nāma aura gotra kī āṭha-āṭha muhūrta hai |"
sthitibandha kā mukhya kāraṇa kaṣāya hai | kaṣāyodayajanya saṃkliṣṭa pariṇāmoṃ kī tīvratā hone para utkṛṣṭa sthiti kā bandha hotā hai aura kaṣāya pariṇāmoṃ ke maṃda hone para jaghanya sthiti kā bandha hotā hai tathā madhyama pariṇāmoṃ dvārā ajaghanyotkṛṣṭa ( madhyama ) sthiti kā bandha hotā hai |
yadyapi prakṛtibandha ke paścāta usake svāmī kā varṇana karanā cāhiye thā lekina vaṃghasvāmitva kī ṭīkā meṃ usakā vistāra se varṇana kiye jāne ke kāraṇa punarāvṛtti na karake yahāṃ sthitibandha ko batalāyā hai |
bandha ho jāne para jo karma jitane samaya taka ātmā ke sātha ṭhaharā rahatā hai, vaha usakā sthitibandha kahalātā hai| karma baṃdhane ke bāda hī tatkāla apanā phala denā prārambha nahīṃ kara dete haiṃ aura na eka sātha hī eka samaya meṃ apanā pūrā phala de dete haiṃ | kintu yathāsamaya phala denā prārambha karake apanī śakti ko krama se naṣṭa karate haiṃ | isa baṃdhane ke samaya se lekara nirjīrṇa hone ke samaya taka karmoṃ kī ātmā ke sātha saṃbaddha rahane kī adhikatama aura nyūnatama kālamaryādā ko batalāne ke lie sthitibandha kā kathana kiyā jātā hai| adhikatama

śataka

paṃcama karmagrantha
121
akaṣāya jīvoṃ ko bhī baṃdhatā hai aura śeṣa sāta karma kevala sakaṣāya jīvoṃ ko baṃdhate haiṃ | akapāya jīvoṃ ko jo vedanīya karma kā bandha hotā hai, usakī kevala do samaya kī sthita hotī hai, pahale samaya meṃ usakā bandha hotā hai aura dūsare samaya meṃ usakā vedana hokara nirjarā ho jātī hai | ataḥ karmoṃ kī jaghanya sthiti batalāne ke prasaṃga meṃ vedanīya karma kī jo bāraha muhūrta kī jaghanya sthiti batalāī vaha 'mutta aphasāyaṭhiī' akaṣāya jīvoṃ ko choḍa़kara sakaṣāya jīvoṃ ko samajhanā cāhiye | arthāt sakaṣāya vedanīya karma kī jaghanya sthiti bāraha muharta' hai aura akaṣāya vedanīya kī do muṃhata |
āge uttara prakṛtiyoṃ ke āśraya se karmoṃ ke abādhākāla (anudayakāla) kā kathana kiyā jāyegā | ataḥ usake anusāra mūla prakṛtiyoṃ kā bhī abādhākāla samajhanā cāhiye | vānī jñānāvaraṇa, darśanāvaraṇa, vedanīya aura antarāya kāmakā tīna hajāra varṣa, mohanīya kā sāta hajāra varṣa, nāma tathā gotra karma kā do hajāra varṣa evaṃ āyu karma kā antamuharta aura pūrva koḍa़ī kā tīsarā bhāga | sthiti meṃ se abādhākāla ko kama karane para jo kāla bākī rahe use niṣekākāla (bhogyakāla) jānanā cāhiye | abādhākāla yānī dalikoṃ kī racanā se rahita kāla | jisa samaya jitanī sthiti vālā jo karma ātmā bāṃdhatā hai aura usake bhāga meṃ jitanī karmavargaṇāyeṃ ātī haiṃ, ve vargaṇāyeṃ utane samaya paryanta niyata phala de sakane ke liye apanī racanā karatī haiṃ| prārambha ke kucha sthānoṃ meṃ ve racanā nahīṃ karatī haiṃ | isī ko abādhākāla kahate haiṃ | abādhākāla ke bāda ke pahale sthāna meṃ adhika, dūsare meṃ usase kama, tīsare meṃ dūsare se kama, isa prakāra sthitibandha ke carama samaya taka bhogane ke liye kī gaī karmadalikoṃ kī racanā ko niṣeka kahā jātā hai|
1 uttarādhyayana' meṃ antamuhūrta pramāṇa bhī kahī hai |

abādhākāla kā aisā niyama hai ki jaghanya sthiti bandha meṃ antamuhataṃ kā abādhākāla, samayādhika jaghanya sthitibandha se lekara palyopama ke asaṃkhya bhāgādhika sthiti bāṃdhane ke samaya taka samayādhika antama hūrta tathā usakī apekṣā samayādhika bandha se lekara dūsare palyopama kā asaṃkhyātavāṃ bhāga pūrṇa hone taka do samaya adhika antamuhūrta kā abādhākāla hotā hai| isa prakāra palyopama ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāgādhika baṃdha meṃ samaya-samaya kā abādhākāla baḍha़āte jāne para pūrṇa koḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgaropama ke baṃdha meṃ mau varṣa kā abādhākāla hotā hai| yānī utane kāla ke jitane samaya hote haiṃ, utane sthānoṃ meṃ dālakoṃ kī racanā nahīṃ hotī hai|
isa prakāra se mūla karmoṃ kī utkṛṣṭa aura jaghanya sthiti batalāne ke paścāta aba uttara prakṛtiyoṃ kī utkṛṣṭa spiti kā kathana karate haiṃ| uttara prakṛtiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha

mataka

viśeṣārtha-isa gāthā meṃ tīrthakara aura āhārakadvika āhāraka śarīra aura āhāraka aṃgopāṃga kī utkṛṣṭa va jaghanya sthiti tathā abādhākāla batalāne ke sātha āyukarma ke manuṣya va tiryaṃca āyu ina do bhedoṃ kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti batalāī hai|

pātaka
māmakarma kī sattā kī apekṣā se kahā hai, na ki sāmānya sattā kī apekṣā se | isalie anikācita tīrthaṃkara nāmakarma kī sattā rahane para bhī jīva kā cāra maśinoṃ meṃ jāne se kisī gāya kā misevakahī hai|
ukta kathana kā sārāṃśa yaha hai ki tīthaṃkara nāmakarma kī sthiti aṃtaḥkoḍākoḍa़ī sāgaropama aura tīrthakara ke bhava sa pahale ke tīsare bhava meṃ jo usakā baṃdha honā kahā hai, vaha isa prakāra samajhanā cāhie ki tīsare bhava meṃ udvartana, apavartana ke dvārā usa sthiti ko tīna bhavoṃ ke yogya kara liyā jātā hai| yadyapi tīna bhavoṃ meṃ to koḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgaropama kī sthiti pūrṇa nahīṃ ho sakatī hai ataḥ apavartanakaraṇa ke dvārā usa sthiti kā hāsa kara diyā jātā hai | śāstroṃ meṃ jo tīsare bhaya meṃ tīrthakara prakṛti ke baṃdha kā vidhāna kiyā hai, vaha nikācita tīrthakara prakṛti ke liye samajhanā cāhiye yānī nikācita prakṛti apanā phala avaśya de detī hai, kintu anikācita tīrthaṃkara prakṛti ke liye koī niyama nahī hai| vaha tīsare bhava se pahale bhī baṃdha sakatī hai|
narakāyu aura devāyu kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti pahale batalā āye haiṃ, ataḥ yahāṃ manuṣyāyu aura tiryaṃcāyu ko utkṛṣṭa sthita batāī hai ki 'naratiriyāṇāu pallatiga' manuṣya aura tiryaṃcāyu tīna palya kī hai|' āyukarma kī sthiti ke bāre meṃ yaha viśeṣa jānanā cāhiye ki bhavaḥ sthiti kī apekṣā se utkṛṣṭa aura jaghanya āyu kā pramāṇa batalāyā jātā hai ki koī bhī jīva janma pākara usameṃ jaghanya athavā utkṛṣṭa kitane kāla taka jī sakatā hai |

pacama karmagrantha
136
āmukarma ke sivāya śeṣa sāta karmoṃ kī abādhā kā saṃketa pūrva meṃ kiyā jā cukā hai ki eka koḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgara kī sthiti meṃ sau varṣa abādhākāla hotā hai| lekina yaha anupāta āyukarma kī abādhā sthiti para lāgū nahīṃ hotā hai | isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki anya karmoṃ kā baṃdhato sadā hotā rahatā hai| kintu āyukarma kā baṃdha amukaamuka kāla meṃ hī hotā hai| isalie āyukarma ke abādhākāla kā alaga se saṃketa kiyā gayā hai ki - niruvakamāṇa chamāsā - nirupakrama āyu vāle arthāt jinakī āyu kā apavartana, ghāta nahīṃ hotā aise deva, nāraka aura bhogabhūmija manuṣya, tiryaṃcoṃ ke āyukarma kī abādhā chaha māsa hotī hai tathā śeṣa manuṣya aura tiryaṃcoṃ ke āyukarma kī abādhā apanī-apanī āyu ke tīsare bhāga pramāṇa hai- abāha sesāṇa bhavataṃso |
|
gati ke anusāra āyubaṃdha ke amuka-amuka kāla nimna prakāra haiṃmanuṣyagati aura tiryaṃcagati meṃ jaba bhujyamāna āyu ke do bhāga bīta jāte haiṃ taba parabhava kī āyubaṃdha kā kāla upasthita hotā hai |

isake anusāra darśanāvaraṇa aura vedanīya varga ko utkṛṣṭa sthiti tīsa koḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgara meṃ mithyātva kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti sattara koḍa़: koḍa़ī sāgara kā bhāga dene para jo labdha ātā hai usameṃ patya ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga ko kama kara dene para nidrāpaṃcaka aura asātā vedanīya kī jaghanya sthiti jñāta hotī hai| darśanamohanīya varga kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti sattara koḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgara meṃ mithyātva kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti kā bhāga dene para prāpta labdha eka sāgara meṃ palya kā asaṃkhyātavāṃ bhāga kama karane para mithyātva kī jaghanya sthiti hotī hai| kaṣāyamohanīya varga kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti cālīsa koḍākoḍī sāgara meṃ mithyātva kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti kā bhāga dekara labdha ke sāgara meṃ se palya kā asaṃkhyātavāṃ bhāga kama karane para anantānubaṃdhī krodhādi bāraha kaṣāyoṃ kī jaghanya sthiti jñāta hotī hai| nokaṣāyamohanīya varga kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti bīsa koḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgara meṃ mithyātva kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti kā bhāga dekara labdha sāgara meṃ se palya kā asaṃkhyātavāṃ bhāga kama karane para puruṣa veda ke sivāya śeṣa āṭha nokapāyoṃ kī jaghanya sthiti ātī hai| nāmavarga aura govavarga kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti bīsa koḍākoḍa़ī sāgara meṃ mithyātva kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti kā bhāga dekara labdha meṃ se palya kā asaṃkhyātavāṃ bhāga kama kara dene para vaikriyaṣaṭka, āhārakadvika, tīrthaṃkara, yaśa:kīrti ko choḍa़kara nāmakarma kī śeṣa sattāvana prakṛtiyoṃ aura nīcagotra kī jaghanya sthiti jñāta hotī hai|
yahāṃ para jo 85 prakṛtiyoṃ kī javanya sthiti batalāī hai, usameṃ karmaprakṛti kī vivecanā ke anurūpa palya ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga ko kama karane kā saṃketa isa gāthā meṃ nahīṃ kiyā gayā hai, lekina āge kī gāthā meṃ 'paliyāsaṃkhaṃ sahīṇa lahubaṃdhoṃ pada diyā hai| jisakā artha hai palya ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga ko kama kara dene para ekendriya jīva ko unauna prakṛtiyoṃ kī aghanya sthiti hotī hai | ataḥ karmaprakṛti ke anusāra

ghātaka ke baṃdha yogya nāmakarma kī 58 prakṛtiyoṃ aura donoṃ gotroṃ kī sāgara pramāṇa utkṛṣṭa sthiti ātī hai| ___ ekendriya ke isa utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha meṃ se patya kā asaṃkhyātavāṃ bhāga kama kara dete. para ekendriya jova ke jaghanya sthitibaṃdha kā pramāṇa hogā paliyāsaṃkhasahīṇa lahubaṃdho| arthāt jo vibhinna prakṛ. tiyoṃ kī : sāgara ādi utkṛṣṭa sthitiyāṃ batalāī haiṃ, unameṃ se palya kā asaṃkhyātavā~ bhāga kama kara dene para ekendriya jīva ke lie vahī usa prakṛti ko jaghanya sthiti ho jātī hai| ___ isa prakāra se ekendriya kī apekṣā se sthitibaṃdha kā parimāṇa batalāne ke paścāta aba vikalendriya aura asaṃjñī paṃcendriya jīvoṃ ke liye usakā parimāṇa batalāte haiṃ|
ekendriya jīva ke jo sāgara ādi utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha batalāyā hai, usako paccīsa se guṇā karane para dvīndriya kā,pacāsa se guṇā karane para trīndriya kā, sau se guṇā karane para caturindriya kā aura hajāra se guṇā karane para asaṃjñī paṃcendriya jīvoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kā parimāṇa hotā hai | isakā artha yaha hai ki dvīndriya ādi jīvoṃ kā sthitibaṃdha ekendriya jīva ke sthitibaṃdha kī apekṣā paccīsa, pacāsa guṇā ādi adhika hai | jaise ekendriya jīva ke mithyātva kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti eka sāgara hai to hīndriya jīva ke usakī utkṛṣṭa sthiti paccīsa sāgara baṃdhatī hai | anya prakṛtiyoṃ ke liye bhī isī apekṣā ko samajha lenā cāhiye | isī prakāra śrīndriya ke lie jānanā cāhiye ki ekendriya jīva kī mithyātva kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti eka sāgara pramāṇa hai to usase pacāsa guṇī yānī pacāsa sāgara pramāṇa baṃdhatī hai | anya prakṛtiyoṃ ke sthitibaṃdha ke bāre meṃ bhī isī niyaya kā upayoga karanā cāhie| caturindriya jīva ke lie ekendriya jīva kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti meṃ sau kā

paṃcama phārmagranya
153
guṇā tathā asaṃśī paṃcendriya ke liye hajāra kā guṇā karanā cāhie | isakā jo guṇanaphala prāpta ho vaha una-una jīvoṃ kī usa-usa prakṛti kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti hogī|
dvīndriya se lekara asaṃśī paṃcendriya paryanta jo unakā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha batalāyā hai, usameṃ se palya' kā saṃkhyātavāṃ bhāga kama kara dene para unakā apanā-apanā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha hotā hai| isa prakāra ekendriya se lekara asaṃñī paṃcendriya paryanta jīvoṃ ke sthitibaṃdha kā pramāṇa samajhanā cāhiye|
1 karmagrantha kī taraha go0 karmakāṃḍa meṃ bhī ekendriya ādi jīvoṃ ke sthitibaṃdha kā pramāṇa batalāyā hai | usakī kathana praṇālo isa prakāra hai
evaṃ pagadi pāṇaṃ sapaṃ sahassaṃ ca mimavaravaṃdho| igavigalāṇaṃ avaraṃ pallāsaṃgṇasaṃkhaṇa ||144||
ekendriya aura vikalendrima catuṣka (dvīndriya, śrīndriya, paturindriya, asaṃgī pacendriya) jīvoṃ ke mithyātva kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kramaśaḥ eka sāgara, paccīsa sāmara, pacāsa sāgara, so māgara aura eka hajāra sāgara pramāṇa hai tathā usakā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha ekendriya ke pasya ke asaṃkhyāta bhāgahīna eka sāgara pramāṇa hai tathā vikanendriya jīvoṃ ke palya ke saṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga hīna apanī-apanī utkṛṣṭa sthiti pramāṇa hotā hai|
ādi saptarissa ettipametaṃ ki hodi tīsiyābogaṃ | hari saṃpāte sesāṇaṃ igivigale ubhayaṭhinī ||14||
yadi sattara koḍākoḍa़ī sāgara kī sthiti vālā midhyātma karma ekendriya jīva eka sāgara pramāṇa bāṃdhatā hai to sīsa koḍākoḍa़ī sāgara ādi kī sthiti vāle bākī karmoṃ ko ekendriya jīva kisanī sthiti pramāṇa bāṃdha sakatā hai ? isa prakāra rāśika vidhi karane se kendriya jīva kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti va sāgara pramāṇa hotī hai, isa prakāra donoṃ sthitiyāṃ rāśipha ke dvārā nikala ātī haiṃ|

śamaka
āyukama kī uttara prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha isa prakāra samajhanā cāhiye ki 'sūranarayāu samādasasahassa' devāyu aura narakāyu kī jaghanya sthiti dasa hajāra varṣa hai tathā devāyu va narakāyu ke sivāya śeṣa do āyuoṃ -tiryaṃcāyu, manuṣyāyu kī jaghanya sthiti kṣudrabhava pramāṇa hai | āgamoṃ meṃ jo manupyāyu aura tiryaṃcāyu kī jaghanya sthiti antamu hurta pramāṇa banalāī hai, usakā yahāṃ batalāye gaye bhudrabhava pramāṇa se koī virodha nahīṃ hai| isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki antamuhūrta ke bahuta se bheda hai, unameṃ se yahāṃ kṣudrabhava pramāṇa antamuhūrta lenā cāhiye | antamuha likhakara usake dhokā auka parimāna kā mūnaka kṣudrabhava likhā hai | kṣudabhava kā nirūpaṇa āge kiyā jā rahā hai|
ina prakāra se unara prakṛtiyoṃ ke jaghanya sthitibaṃdha kā kathana karake aba jaghanya abādhā tathā tīrthakara va āhārakadvika ke jaghanya sthitibaṃdha saṃbaṃdhī matāntara ko batalāte haiṃ |
savāṇavi lahubaṃdhe bhinnamuhū avāha āujiṭhe thi | ke i . surāusamaṃ jiṇamaṃtamūra diti bhāhāraṃ ||36||
śabdārtha-sanmāṇa- saba prakṛtiyoṃ kī.vi-tathā, samaṃdhe --- jayanya sthitibaṃdha kī, bhinnamutra- antarmuhūrta, abāha abādhākāla, āujinche thi- āyu ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kī bhī, ke-kucha eka, murāumāma devāyu ke samāna, jiṇaṃ tīryavara nāmakarma kī, aṃtamuha .anta mahataṃ, viti - kahate haiṃ, āhāraṃ āhāraddhika kro |
___ gādhārya-samasta prakṛtiyoṃ ke jaghanya sthitibaṃdha kī antama hurna kī abādhā hotī hai| āyukarma ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kī jaghanya abādhā antamuhurta pramāṇa hai| kinhī

ācāryoṃ ke mata se tīrthakara nāmakarma kī jaghanya sthiti devāyu kī jaghanya sthiti ke mamāna dasa hajāra varṣa kī hai aura āhārakadvika kī antamuharta pramāṇa hai | śiśeṣārtha- gāthā meṃ do bātoṃ kā kathana kiyā gayā hai | gāthā ke pūrvārdha meṃ sabhī uttara prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya abādhākāla aura uttarārdha meṃ tīrthaṃkara va āhārakādvika kī jaghanya sthiti kā matāntara batalāyā
jaghanya sthitibaṃdha meṃ jo avādhākāla hotā hai, use jaghanya abādhā aura utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha meṃ jo abādhākāla hotā hai use utkṛṣṭa abādhā kahate haiṃ| ataḥ jaghanya sthitibaṃdha meṃ sabhī uttara prakṛti ke jaghanya sthitibaṃdha kā abādhākāla antamuhūrta pramāṇa batalāyā hai-"savvāṇavi lahubaṃdhe bhinnamūha abāha |" lekina yaha niyama āyukarma ko choḍa़kara śeṣa sāta karmoṃ ke avādhākāla ko batalāne ke lie lāgū hotā hai | kyoṃki unakī abādhā sthiti ke pratibhāga ke anusāra hotī hai| lekina āyukarma ke bāre meṃ pratibhāga kī niścita nirṇayātmaka sthiti nahīṃ hai| āyukarma kī to utkṛṣṭa sthiti meṃ bhī jaghanya abādhā ho sakatī hai aura jaghanya sthiti meṃ bhī utkṛṣṭa abādhā ho sakatī hai| imīliye āyukarma kī abādhā meṃ cāra vikalpa māne jāte haiṃ-(1) utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha meṃ utkṛṣṭa abādhā, (2) utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha meṃ jaghanya abādhā, (3) jaghanya sthitibaṃdha meṃ utkṛṣṭa abādhā aura (4) jaghanya sthitibaṃdha meṃ jaghanya abādhā | inakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa nimna prakāra hai ki jaba koī manuṣya apanī pūrva koṭi kī āyu meṃ tīsarā bhāga śeṣa rahane para tetīsa sāgara kī āyu bāṃdhatā hai taba utkṛṣṭa sthiti meṃ utkṛṣṭa avādhā hotī hai aura yadi antamuhūrta pramāṇa āyu śeṣa rahane para tetīsa sāgara kī āyu bāṃdhatā

﻿________________

156
sātaka
hai taba utkṛṣṭa sthiti meṃ jaghanya abādhā hotī hai| jaba koī manuṣya eka pūrva koṭi kā tīsarā bhāga śeṣa rahate parabhava ko jaghanya sthiti bāṃdhatā hai jo antamuhūrta pramāṇa ho sakatī hai, taba jaghanya sthiti meṃ utkṛṣṭa abādhā hotī hai aura jaba koī antamuhūrta pramāṇa sthiti śeṣa rahane para parabhava kī antamuhūrta pramāṇa sthiti bāṃdhatā hai taba jaghanya sthiti meṃ jaghanya abādhā hotī hai| ata: āyukarbha kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti meṃ bhī jaghanya abādhā ho sakatī hai aura jaghanya sthiti meṃ bhī utkṛṣṭa abādhā ho sakatī hai| viśeṣa spaṣṭīkaraṇa pariśiṣṭa meṃ kiyā gayā hai|
isa prakāra se karmoṃ kī sthiti kī abādhā kā spaṣṭīkaraṇa samajhanā cāhiye | aba dūsarī bāta tīrthaṃkara nāmakarma va āhārakadvika ko jaghanya sthitibaṃdha ke matāntara para vicāra karate haiṃ| __ andhakāra ne pūrva meṃ tīrthakara aura āhārakadvika ina tīna prakṛtiyoṃ ko jaghanya sthiti antaḥkoḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgaropama batalāī hai| lekina koī-koī ācārya ina tīnoṃ kī jaghanya sthiti batalāte haiṃ
muranārayāuyāṇaṃ rasavāsasahassa laghu satittvāṇaṃ |' tīrthaṃkara nāmakarma sahita devāyu, narakāyu kī jaghanya sthiti dasa hajāra varṣa hai | yānī tīrthaṃkara nāmakarma kī jaghanya sthiti dasa hajāra varṣa pramāṇa hai 1 tathā
sāe bārasa hāragavigyāvaraṇāṇa kiṃvṛṇa |' sātā vedanīya kī bāraha muhūrta aura āhāraka, aṃtarāya, jñānāvaraṇa va darśanāvaraṇa ko kucha kama muhūrta pramāṇa jaghanya sthiti hai|

śataka
eka muhūrta meṃ paiṃsaṭha hajāra pāṃca sau chattīsa kṣudrabhava hote haiṃ aura eka kṣudrabhava meṃ do sau chappana āvalī hotī haiṃ |
viśeṣārtha - gāthā meṃ kṣudra ( kṣullaka) bhava kā svarūpa batalāyā hai 1 sampūrṇa bhavoṃ meṃ saba se choṭe bhava ko kṣullaka bhava' kahate haiṃ | yaha bhava nigodiyā jīva ke hotā hai| kyoṃki nigodiyā jīva kī sthiti saba bhavoṃ kī apekṣā alpa hotī hai aura vaha bhava manuṣya va tiyaṃtra paryāya meṃ hī hotā hai| jisase manuṣya aura titheca āyu kī jaghanya sthiti kṣullaka bhava pramāṇa batalāī hai| kṣullaka bhava kā parimāṇa isa prakāra samajhanā cāhie ki
jaina kālagaṇanā ke anusāra asaṃkhyāta samaya kī eka āvalī hotī hai | saṃkhyāta āvalī kā eka ucchvāsa niśvāsa hotā hai| eka niroga, svastha, niścinta, taruṇa puruṣa ke eka bāra śvāsa lene aura tyāgane ke kāla ko eka ucchvāsa kāla yā śvāsocchvāsa kāla kahate haiṃ | sāta śvāsocchvāsa kāla kā eka stoka hotā hai| sāta stoka kā eka laba tathā sāḍha़e aḍa़tīsa lava kī eka nālī yā ghaṭikā hotī hai| do ghaṭikā kā eka muhūrta hotā hai |"
tu 1
1 kālo paramaniruddho avibhājjo laṃ tu jāṇa samayaṃ samayā va asaṃkhejjā havai hu ussāsanislāso || ussāso nissāso yado'vi pātti bhannae ekko | pāṇā va satta yovā yovāviya matta jabamāhu || adbhuttamaṃ tu vā alavo deva nāliyā ho |
jyotiṣkaraṇḍaka, 6, 10 samaya kahate hai | asaṃgata

re
viśeṣārtha gāthā meṃ utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ke svāmiyoṃ kā kathana kiyā gayā hai ki baṃdhayogya 120 prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se kisa prakṛti kā kauna utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha karatā hai |
sarvaprathama tīrthaṃkara prakṛti ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ke svāmī kā saṃketa karate hue kahā hai ki- 'avirayasammo titthaṃ' avirata samyagdṛṣṭi manuṣya tīrthaṃkara prakṛti ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kā svāmī hai | isakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa nimna prakāra hai
tīrthaṃkara prakṛti ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kā svāmī manuṣya hai | isakā kāraṇa yaha hai, ki yadyapi tīrthaṃkara prakṛti kā baṃdha cauthe guṇasthāna se lekara āṭhaveṃ guṇasthāna taka hotā hai kintu utkṛṣṭa sthiti utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa se hī baṃdhatī hai aura vaha utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa tīrthaṃkara prakṛti ke baṃdhakoṃ meṃ se usa avirata sambanha manuṣya ke hotā hai jo avirata samyagdṛṣṭi samyaktva grahaṇa karane se pahale mithyātva guṇasthāna meṃ narakāyu kā baṃdha kara letā hai aura bāda meṃ kṣāyopaśamika samyaktva grahaṇa karake tīrthakara prakṛti kā baṃdha karatā hai, vaha manuṣya jaba naraka meṃ jāne kā samaya ātā hai to samyaktva kā vamana karake mithyātva ko aṃgīkāra karatā hai | jisa samaya meṃ vaha samyaktva ko tyāga kara mithyātva ko aṃgīkāra karatā hai, usase pahale samaya meṃ usa avirata samyagdṛṣṭi manuṣya ke tīrthaṃkara prakṛti kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha hotā hai |
devagati aura narakagati meṃ tīrthaṃkara prakṛti kā baṃdha to hotā hai kintu vahāṃ tīrthaṃkara prakṛti kā baṃdhaka cauthe guṇasthāna se cyuta hokara mithyātva ke abhimukha nahīṃ hotā hai aura aisā hue binā tīrthaṃkara prakṛti ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kā kāraṇa utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa nahīṃ sakatā | isīlie tīrthaṃkara prakṛti ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibandha

śataka
bhanuṣya kā grahaṇa kiyā tathā tīrthakara prakṛti kā baṃdha karane se pahale jo manuṣya narakāyu kā baṃdha nahīṃ karatā hai vaha tīrthakara prakṛti kā baṃdha karane ke vāda naraka meṃ utpanna nahīṃ hotā hai | ataḥ vaise manuṣya kā grahaṇa kiyā gayā jo tīrthakara prakṛti kā baṃdha karane se pahale narakāyu bāṃdha letā hai| koī-koī samika samāti, jīda rājāṇita jaise) samyaktva daśā meṃ marakara naraka meṃ jā sakate haiṃ kintu viśuddha pariṇāmoṃ ke kāraṇa ve jīva tīrthaṃkara prakṛti kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha nahīṃ kara sakate haiṃ| ataḥ tīrthaṃkara prakṛti ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ke prakaraṇa meṃ mithyātva ke abhimukha avirata samyāiṣṭi manuṣya kā hī grahaṇa kiyā hai|
tīrthakara prakṛti ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ke saṃbandha meṃ ukta kathana kā sārāṃśa yaha hai ki yadyapi cauthe se lekara āṭhaveṃ guṇasthāna taka tīrthaṃkara prakṛti kā baṃdha ho sakatā hai kintu utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ke lie utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa kī āvaśyakatā hai aura tīrthakara prakṛti ke baṃdhaka manuṣya ko utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa usī daśā meṃ ho sakatā hai jaba vaha mithyātva ke abhimukha ho aura aisā manuṣya mithyātva ke abhimukha tabhī hotā hai jaba usane tīrthakara prakṛti kā vaṃdha karane ke pahale narakāyu kā baṃdha kara liyā hai| baddhanarakāyu avirata samyagdṛṣṭi manuṣya jaba mithyātva ke abhimukha hotā hai, usī samaya meṃ usake tīrthakara prakṛti kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha hotā hai | kṣāyika samyaktva sahita jo naraka meṃ jātā hai vaha usase viśuddhatara hai ataḥ usakā yahāṃ grahaṇa nahīṃ kiyā gayā hai|

paṃcama karmagrantha
163
tīrthaṃkara prakṛti ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ke svāmī kā kathana karane ke bāda aba āhārakadvika aura devāyu ke baṃdhasvāmī ke bāre meṃ kahate haiṃ ki- 'āhāradugāmarāu ya pamatto' - āhārakadvika aura devāyu ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kā svāmī pramattasaṃyata muni hai| yahāṃ pramattasaṃyata śabda dvyarthaka hai | āhārakadvika āhāraka śarīra aura āhāraka aṃgopāṃga ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ke prasaṃga meṃ isakā artha yaha hai ki apramatta guṇasthāna se cyuta huā pramattasaṃyata muni | kyoṃki ina donoṃ prakṛtiyoṃ ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ke lie utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa honā āvaśyaka haiṃ aura unake baṃdhaka pramatta muni ke usī samaya utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa hotā hai jaba vaha apramatta guṇasthāna se cyuta hokara chaṭhe guṇasthāna meṃ ātā hai | ataḥ usake hī ina do prakṛtiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha jānanā cāhiye | "
devāyu ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ke liye āhārakadvika ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha se viparīta sthiti hai | āhārakadvika ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ke liye utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa kī āvaśyakatā hai| yaha utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa pramatta muni ke usī samaya hotā hai jaba vaha apramatta guṇasthāna se cyuta
1 (ka) tayāra āhārakadvika' āhārakaśarīra āhārakāṅa, gopā galakṣaṇaṃ 'pamuttu' tti pramattasaṃyato apramattabhāvānnivartamāna iti viśeṣo dṛśyaḥ, utkṛṣṭasthitikaṃ badhnāti | aśubhā hīyaṃ sthitirityutkṛṣṭasaṃ kleśenaṃ vātkuṣṭā badhyate tadbandhakāca pramattayatirapramattabhāvānnivartamāna evotkṛṣṭasaṃkleyukta labhyate itītthaṃ viśiṣyate |

﻿________________

śataka
hokara chaṭhe pramatta guṇasthāna meṃ ātā hai,lekina devāyu kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha apramattasaṃyata guṇasthāna ke abhimukha pramattasaṃyata muni ke hī hotā hai | kyoṃki yaha sthiti śubha hai | ataḥ isakā baṃdha viśuddha daśā meṃ hī hotā hai aura yaha viśuddha daśā apramatta bhāva ke ābhabhu pramasasaṃpata muni ke hī hotī hai|
āhārakadvika aura devāyu ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha hone ke ukta kathanoṃ kā sārāṃśa yaha hai ki āhāraka śarīra aura āhāraka aṃgopāṃga ina do prakutiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha pramatta guṇasthāna ke abhimukha hue apramattasaṃyata ko hotā hai| isake baṃdhayogya ati saṃkliṣṭa pariṇāma usī samaya hote haiṃ tathā devāyu ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kā svāmī bhī apramatta guṇasthānavartī hai kintu itanī viśeṣatā hai ki pramatta guṇasthāna meṃ āyubaṃdha ko prāraṃbha karake apramattasaṃyata guṇasthāna kā ārohaṇa kara rahā ho | yānī āhārakadvika kā baṃdha sātaveṃ guṇasthāna se chaṭhe guṇasthāna kī ora avarohaṇa karane vāle apramattasaṃyata muni ko aura devāyu kā baṃgha chaṭhe guṇasthāna meṃ prārambha karake sātaveṃ guṇasthāna kī ora ārohaṇa karane vāle muni ko hotā hai|

śataka
deva aura nārakoṃ ke ukta chaha prakṛtiyoṃ ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha hone kā kāraṇa yaha hai ki ukta prakṛtiyoṃ ke baṃdhayogya saṃkliṣṭa pari ṇāma hone para manuṣya aura tica ina chaha prakṛtiyoṃ ko adhika-seadhika aṭhāraha sāgara pramāṇa hī sthiti kā baṃdha karate haiṃ| yadi usase adhika saṃkleśa pariṇāma hote haiṃ to ina prakṛtiyoṃ ke baṃdha kā atikramaṇa karake ve narakagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karate haiṃ | kintu deva aura nāraka to utkṛṣṭa se utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa pariṇāmoṃ ke hone para bhī tiyaṃcagati ke yogya hī prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karate haiṃ, narakagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha nahīṃ kara sakate haiṃ| kyoṃki deva aura nāraka bharakara naraka meṃ utpanna nahīṃ hote haiṃ | isīlie utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa pariṇāmoṃ se yukta deva aura nāraka hī ina chaha prakṛtiyoṃ kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti bīsa koḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgara pramāṇa kā baṃdha karate haiṃ |
ukta chaha prakṛtiyoṃ ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ke prasaṃga meṃ itanā viśeṣa samajhanā cāhiye ki īśāna svarga se ūpara ke sanatkumāra ādi svargo ke deva hī sevārta saṃhanana aura audārika aṃgopāṃga kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha karate haiṃ, īśāna svarga ke deva nahīṃ karate haiṃ| kyoṃki īśāna svarga taka ke deva unake yogya saṃkleśa pariṇāmoṃ ke hone para bhī donoṃ prakṛtiyoṃ kī adhika se adhika aṭhāraha sāgara pramāṇa madhyama sthiti kā baṃdha karate haiṃ aura yadi unake utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa pariṇāma hote haiṃ to ekendriya ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karate haiṃ | sanatkumāra ādi ke deva' utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa hone para bhī paṃcendriya tiryaṃca ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karate haiṃ, ekendriya meṃ unakā janma nahīṃ hone se ekendriya yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha nahīṃ karate haiṃ | ataḥ sevārta saṃhanana aura odārika aṃgopāṃga ina do prakṛtiyoṃ kī bosa koḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgara pramāṇa utkṛṣṭa sthiti kā baṃdha utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa pariṇāma vāle sanatkumāra ādi svargoṃ

paṃcama pharmagranya
ke deva hī kara sakate haiṃ, nīce ke deva nahīṃ karate haiṃ| kyoṃki ekendriya ke saṃhanana aura aṃgopāṃga nahīṃ hone se ye do prakṛtiyāṃ ekendriya yogya nahīṃ haiṃ|
sārāṃśa yaha hai ki eka sarīkhe pariṇāma hone para bhī gati ādi ke bheda se unameṃ bheda ho jātā hai| jaise ki īśāna svarga taka ke deva jina pariṇāmoṃ se ekendriya ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karate haiṃ, vaise hī pariṇāma hone para manuṣya aura tiryaṃca narakagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karate haiṃ|
isa prakāra se mithyā dṛṣṭi ke baṃdhane yogya 116 prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se pūrvokta vikalatrika ādi seyāta saṃhanana paryanta 24 prakṛti ke sivāya śeṣa 62 prakṛtiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha cāroṃ gatiyoṃ ke mithyādṛṣṭi jīva karate haiṃ—sesa caugaiyā |'
1 go0 karmakāṃḍa meṃ bhī 116 prakṛtiyoṃ ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ke svāmiyoṃ ko batalāte hue likhā hai
ṇaratiriyā mesāu' vegubbiyachakkaviyalasuṭsamatiyaṃ | suraṇirayā oraliyatiriyadugujjovasaṃpattaṃ ||137|| devā puṇa iṃdimādāya thāparaṃ ca samāṇaṃ |
upakassasaṃki liṭṭhā cadurgādayā īsimajhimayā ||138|| devāyu ke vinā śeṣa tīna āyu, yakriyaṣaṭka, vikalatrika, mūkṣmatrika kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha mi pāṭi manuṣya, tiryaca karate haiṃ | audārikadvika, tiryaṃcadvika, udya:, asaṃprāptāmṛpā ṭakā (sevāta) saṃhanana kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibadha mithyāṣṭi, deva aura nāraka karate haiṃ| ekendriya, ātapa aura sthāvara kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha midhyāhṛṣṭi deva karate haiṃ | śeṣa prakṛtiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa sthiti maṃdha utkṛṣṭa saṃpaleśa vāle mithyāṣṭaṣṭi jīva apadā īṣat madhyama pariṇāma vāle mithyā ṣṭi jīva karate haiṃ|

śataka
aghanya sthitibaṃdha kā svāmitva
utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ke svāmiyoṃ ko batalākara aba jaghanya sthitibaṃdha ke svāmiyoṃ ko batalāte haiṃ| jaghanya sthitibaṃdha ke svāmitva ke bāre meṃ vicāra karane se pūrva do bātoṃ kā jānanā jarūrī hai | eka to jaise utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ke lie utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa honā āvaśyaka hai, vaise hī jaghanya sthitibaṃdha ke lie utkṛṣṭa viśuddhi honā cāhiye | dūsarī yaha hai ki jisa guṇasthāna taka yathāyogya kaṣāyoṃ kā sadbhāva rahane se jina-jina prakṛtiyoṃ kā bandha hotā hai aura usake āge ke guṇasthāna meṃ baṃdhaviccheda ho jāne se baṃdha kī saṃbhāvanā hī nahīṃ hai, usa guṇasthāna meṃ una karma prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha hotā hai | ataeva jaghanya sthitibaṃdha kā kathana prāraṃbha karate hue sarvaprathama tīrthakara nāma
aura āhāradvika ke jaghanya sthitibaṃdha ke liye kahate haiṃ ki 'āhārajiṇamapubyo' āhārakaṭika aura tīrthaṃkara nāmakarma ina prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha apūrvakaraṇa nāmaka āṭhaveṃ gaṇasthāna meṃ hotā hai| kyoṃki inake baṃdhakoṃ meṃ ukta guṇasthāna vāle jīva hī ati viśuddha pariṇāma vāle hote haiṃ aura 'aniyadi saṃjalaṇa purisa lahu' anivṛttibādara nāmaka nauveṃ guṇasthāna taka saṃjvalana krodha, māna, māyā, lobha aura puruṣa veda ina pāṃca prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha hotā hai|' āhārakadvika ādi puruṣa veda paryanta āṭha prakṛtiyoṃ ke jaghanya sthitibaṃdha ke svāmī ke saṃbaṃdha meṃ itanā viśeṣa jānanā cāhiye ki āṭhavāṃ aura nauvāṃ yaha donoṃ guṇasthāna kṣapaka śroṇi ke hī lenā cāhie, kyoṃki upaśama śreṇi se kṣapaka śroṇi meṃ viśeṣa viśuddhi hotī hai |
1 āṭhaveṃ, nauveṃ, dasaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ badhavicchinna hone vālī prakṛtiyoṃ ke
nāma dvitīya karmagrantha gā.6,10,11 meṃ dekhiye |

paṃcama karmagrantha
sātā vedanīya, yaśa kīti, uccagotra, matijñānāvaraṇa ādi jñānāvaraṇa karma kī pāṃca prakṛtiyāṃ, cakṣudarśanāvaraṇa ādi cāra darśanāvaraṇa karma kī prakṛtiyāṃ tathā dānāntarāya ādi pāṃca antarāya karma kī prakṛtiyāṃ, kula satraha prakṛtiyoṃ ke jaghanya sthitibaṃdha kā svāmī sūkṣmasaṃparāya nāmaka dasaveṃ guṇasthānavartī kṣapaka hai-sāyajasuccāvaraṇā vigdhaṃ suhumo | kyoṃki sātāvedanīya ke sivāya solaha prakṛtiyāṃ isī guṇasthāna taka baṃdhatī haiṃ, ataḥ unake baṃdhakoṃ meṃ yahī guṇasthāna viśeṣa viśuddha hai | yadyapi sātā vedanīya kā baṃdha terahaveṃ guṇasthāna taka hotā hai, tathāpi sthitibaṃdha dasaveṃ guṇasthāna taka hī hotā hai, kyoṃki sthiti baṃdha kā kāraṇa kaṣāya hai| saṃjvalana lobha kaṣāya kā udaya dasaveṃ sūkṣmasaṃparāya guṇasthāna taka rahatā hai, jisase sātā vedanīya kā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha bhī dasaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ hī batalāyā hai| ___ āyukarma kī cāroṃ prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha asaṃjñī jīva bhī karate haiṃ aura saṃjñī jīva bhī karate haiṃ-sannī vi āu | unameṃ se devāyu aura narakāyu kā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha paṃcendriya tiryaṃca aura manuṣya karate haiṃ tathā manuṣyāyu aura tirtha cāyu kā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha ekendriya ādi |
isa prakāra se āhārakadvika ādi āyucatuṣka taka meṃ antarbhūta 35 prakṛtiyoṃ ko baṃdhayogya 120 prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se kama kara dene para śeṣa rahī 85 prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaṃghanya sthitibaṃdha-bāyarapajjegidiu sesāṇaṃ -- bādara paryāpta ekendriya jīva karate haiṃ| kyoṃki prakṛtiyoṃ ke sthitibaṃdha ko batalāne ke prasaṃga meṃ yaha saṃketa kara āye haiṃ ki ina prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha bādara paryāpta ekendriya jīva ko hī hotā hai| ina prakṛtiyoṃ ke baṃdhakoṃ meṃ vahī viśeṣa viśuddhi vālā hotā hai aura anya ekendriya jīva utanī viśuddhi na hone ke kāraṇa ukta prakṛtiyoṃ

śataka
ko adhika sthiti bāṃdhate hai | yacāpa ekāndraya se vikāsandriyoṃ meṃ adhikaṃ viśuddhi hotī hai kintu ve svabhāva se hī ina prakṛtiyoṃ kī adhika sthiti bāṃdhate haiṃ, jisase śeṣa prakṛtiyoṃ ke jaghanya sthitibaṃdha kā svāmī bādara paryāpta ekendriya jīvoṃ ko hī batalāyā hai|

177
cāra prakāra kā hotā hai| bākī ke tīna baṃdha aura āyukarma ke cāroṃ baṃdha sādi aura adhruva, isa taraha do hī prakāra ke hote haiṃ|

178
śataka
karma ko choḍa़kara śeṣa sāta karmoṃ kā ajaghanya baṃdha sādi, anādi, dhruva, adhruva ina cāroṃ prakāra kā hotā hai, kyoṃki mohanīya karma kā jaghanya baṃdha kṣapakanaṇi ke anivṛttibādara saṃparāya nāmaka nauveṃ guṇasthāna ke anta meṃ hotā hai aura śeṣa chaha karmoṃ kā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha kṣapakaśroṇi vāle dasaveṃ sūkṣmasaṃparāya' guṇasthāna ke anta meṃ hotā hai| ___ anya guṇasthānoṃ va upaśamaṇi meṃ bhī ina sātoṃ karmoṃ kā ajaghanya baṃdha hotā hai | ataḥ gyārahaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ ajaghanya baṃdha na karake vahā~ se nā hokara java jīva sāta varṣoṃ kā agalādeśa karatā hai taba vaha baṃdha sādi kahā jātā hai | nauveṃ, dasarve ādi guṇasthānoṃ meṃ āne se pahale ukta sāta karmoṃ kā jo ajadhanya baṃdha hotā hai, vaha anādikāla se niraṃtara hote rahane ke kāraṇa anādi kahalātā hai | abhavya ke baṃdha kā aṃta nahīṃ hotā hai, ataḥ usako hone vālā ajaghanya baṃdha dhruva aura bhavya ke baṃdha kā aṃta hone se usako hone vālā ajaghanya baṃdha adhruva kahalātā hai | isa prakāra sāta karmoṃ ke ajaghanya baṃdha meṃ cāroṃ bhaṃga hote haiṃ| ___ ajaghanya baṃdha ke sibāya śeṣa tīna baṃdhabhedoṃ meṃ sādi aura adhruva, yaha do prakāra hote haiṃ | kyoṃki mohanīya karma kā nauveṃ guṇasthāna ke aṃta meṃ aura śeṣa chaha karmoṃ kā dasaveṃ guṇasthāna ke aṃta meṃ jaghanya sthitibaṃdha hotā hai, usase pūrva nahīṃ, ataḥ vaha baṃdha sādi hai aura bārahaveṃ ādi guṇasthānoṃ meṃ usakā sarvathā abhāva ho jātā hai ataḥ vaha adhruva hai| isa prakāra jaghanya baṃdha meṃ sādi aura adhruva yaha do hī vikalpa hote haiṃ| utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha saṃkliṣṭa pariṇāmī saṃjñī paṃcendriya paryāpta mithyā dṛṣṭi ko hotā hai | vaha baṃdha kabhī-kabhī hotā hai, sarvadā nahīṃ, jisase vaha sādi hai tathā annamuhūrta ke bāda niyama se usakā sthāna badala jāne se anutkṛṣṭabaṃdha sthāna le letā hai, ataḥ vaha adhruva

176 hai| jisase utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha meṃ bhī sādi aura adhruva yaha do vikalpa hote haiṃ|

***
viśeṣārya - isa gāthā meṃ uttara prakṛtiyoṃ ke utkṛṣṭa ādi baṃdhoṃ ke sādi ādi bheda batalāye haiṃ | jaisā mūla prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ sabase pahale ajaghanya baṃdha ke vikalpoṃ kā kathana kiyā gayā hai, vaise hī uttara prakratiyoṃ bhī ajaghanya baṃdha ke vikalpoṃ kā yahāṃ vivecana kiyā jā rahā hai | 120 prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se ajadhanya baṃdha vālī pratiyāṃ sipha hai | jinake nāma kramaśa: isa prakāra haiṃ— saṃjvalana krodha, māna, māyā, lobha, matijñānāvaraṇa, śrutajñānāvaraṇa, avadhijñānāvaraṇa, manaparyāyajñānāvaraṇa, kevalajñānāvaraṇa, cakṣudarśanāvaraṇa, acakṣudarśanāvaraṇa, avadhidarśanāvaraṇa, kevaladarśanāvaraṇa tathā dāna, lobha, bhoga, upayoga, vīrya antarāya — saṃjalaṇāvaraṇanavagavindhāṇaṃ | ina aṭhāraha prakṛtiyoṃ kī ajaghanya sthiti kī śurūāta upaśama śroṇi se patita hone vāle ke hotī hai |
ina aṭhāraha prakṛtiyoṃ ke ajaghanya baṃdha ke sādi, anādi, dhruva, adhruva ye cāroṃ hī vikalpa hote haiṃ, jo mūla karmoṃ ke ajayamya baṃdha kī taraha hī jānanā cāhiye| upaśama śreṇi meṃ ina aṭhāraha prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdhaviccheda karake jaba vahāṃ se cyuta hokara punaḥ unakā ajaghanya baṃdha karate haiṃ to vaha baṃdha sādi aura upaśama śra eṇi meṃ ārohaṇa karane se pahale vaha baṃdha anādi hotā hai| abhavya kī apekṣā vahī baṃdha dhruva aura bhavya kī apekṣā adhruva hai| isīliye ina prakṛtiyoṃ ke ajaghanya

śabdārtha-sāpāiapurvate-sāsādana se lekara apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna taka, aparaṃto koḍikolio-aṃta:koḍākoḍa़ī sāmaropama se, na hiṃgo-adhika (baṃdha) nahīṃ hotā hai, baṃdho - baṃdha, na hūnahīṃ hotā hai, hīko-hīna, na ya–tathā nahīṃ hotā hai, minchamithyāṣṭi, bhaśviyarasanimi-bhavya saṃśī va itara amanya saṃjī meṃ |
gāthātha-sāsādana se lekara apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna taka antaḥkoḍākoḍa़ī sāgaropama se adhika sthitibaṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai aura na hīna baṃdha hotā hai| mithyādṛṣṭi bhavya saṃjñī aura abhavya saṃjñī ke bhī hīna baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai| viśeṣārtha-pahale sāmānya se aura ekendriya ādi jīvoṃ kī apekṣā se sthitibaṃdha kā pramāṇa batalāyā gayā hai| aba isa gāthā meṃ guṇasthānoṃ kī apekṣā se usakā pramāṇa kā kathana kiyā jā rahā hai ki kisa guṇasthāna meṃ kitanā sthitibaṃdha hotā hai|
pūrva meṃ karmoṃ kī sattara, tīsa, bīsa koḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgaropama kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti batalāī hai| usameṃ se sāṇāiapulaṃto-yānī dūsare sāsādana guṇasthāna se lekara apūrvakaraṇa nāmaka āṭhaveṃ guṇasthāna taka antaḥkoḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgara se adhika sthitibaṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai | yānī dūsare se lekara āṭhaveṃ guṇasthāna taka hone vālā baṃdha antaḥkoḍa़ā

koḍa़ī sāgara pramāṇa hotā hai aura jo sattara ādi koḍākoḍa़o sāgaropama uskṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha batalāyā hai, vaha mithyātva guṇasthāna meṃ hī hotā hai | sāsādana se lekara apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna paryanta antaḥkoḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgara pramāṇa sthitibaṃdha hona kā kāraṇa yaha hai ki ina guṇasthānoṃ vāle jīva mithyātvagranthi kā bhedana kara dete haiṃ, jisase unake antaḥkoḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgara pramāṇa se adhika sthitibaṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai|
praśna- ukta kathana para jijñāsu praśna karatā hai ki karmaprakṛti ādi granthoṃ meṃ mithyātvagranthi kā bhedana karane vāloṃ ko bhī mithyātva kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha sattara koḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgaropama batalāyā hai | ataḥ yaha kaise mānā jāya ki sāsādana se lekara apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna taka jīva mithyātvagranthi kā bhedana kara dete haiṃ, ataḥ antaḥkoḍākoḍa़ī sāgara pramāṇa se adhika sthiti kā baṃdha nahīṃ karate haiṃ|
uttara yaha ṭhīka hai ki granthi kā bhedana karane vāloṃ ko bhī utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha hotā hai, kintu samyaktva kā damana karake jo punaḥ mithyātva muṇasthāna meṃ āte haiṃ, unake hī yaha utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha hotā hai | yahāṃ to granthi kā bhedana kara dene vāle sāsādana ādi guṇasthāna vāloṃ ke hī utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kā niṣedha kiyā hai| āvaśyaka ādi meṃ to saiddhāntika mata kā saṃketa karake pranthi kā bhedana kara dene vāle mithyāiṣṭi ko bhī utkṛṣṭa baṃdha kā pratiṣedha kiyā hai |' kārmapranthika mata se sādi mithyāiṣṭi ko bhī mithyātva kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti baṃdhatī hai lekina usameṃ tīvra anubhāga śakti nahīṃ hotī hai 1 ataḥ sāsādana se

paṃcama karmagrantha antaḥkoḍākoḍa़ī sāgara se hīna sthitibaṃdha hotā hī nahīṃ hai, kyoṃki pranthibhedana karane para hī hīna baṃdha honā saṃbhava hai lekina abhavya saṃjī granthideśa taka pahu~catā hai parantu usakā bhedana karane meṃ asamartha hone se punaḥ nīce ā jātā hai| ___ sāsādana se apūrvakaraṇa gṛṇasthāna taka ke sthitibaṃdha meṃ janma.koḍākoḍa़o sāgara pramāṇa se nyūnādhikatā nahīṃ hone para jijñāsu praśna pūchatā hai ki yadi nyūnādhikatā nahīṃ hai to āge sthitibaṃdha ke alpabahutva meṃ jo yaha kahā gayā ki virati ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha se deśavirati kā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha saṃkhyāta guṇā, usase avirata samyagdṛṣṭi aparyāpta kā jaghanya, utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha saṃkhyāta guṇā hotā hai, kaise mānā jāyegā ?. isakā uttara yaha hai ki jaise nau samaya se lekara samayanyūna muhūrta taka antamuhūrta ke asaṃkhyāta bheda hote haiṃ vaise hī sādhu ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha se lekara samayādhika paryāpta saṃjñī paṃcendriya ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha taka asaṃkhyāta ke sthitibaṃdha bheda hote haiṃ jo antaḥkoḍākoḍa़ī pramāṇa haiṃ | ataḥ saṃkhyātaguṇe mānane para kisī prakāra kā virodha nahīṃ hai| ___ isa prakāra se guṇasthānoṃ meṃ sthitibaṃdha kā nirūpaṇa karake aba āge kī gāthāoṃ meṃ ekendriya ādi jīvoṃ kī apekṣā se sthitibandha kā alpabahutva batalāte haiṃ|

sādhū kā utkṛṣṭa sthiti
1
deśa virati kā jaghanya, samyagdṛṣṭi ke cāra prakāra
baṃdha saṃkhaguṇa - saṃkhyāta guṇā besabiramahala iyaro utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha, sampaca ke sthitibaṃdha, samnicauro sabhī paṃcendriya mithyādṛṣṭi ke cāra, baṃdhā sthitibandha, aṇukama anukrama se saṃjaguṇā saṃkhyāta
guṇā |
śataka
—
gābhārtha - sādhu kā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha sabase alpa hotā hai | bādara ekendriya paryāpta kā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha usase asaṃkhyāta guṇā aura sūkṣma ekendriya paryāpta kā usase viśeṣādhika hotā hai | inake (bādara, sūkṣma ekendriya ke ) aparyāpta kā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha usase adhika hotā hai | usakī apekṣā sūkṣma ekendriya aparyāpta, bādara ekendriya aparyāpta, sūkṣma ekendriya paryāpta kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha anukrama se viśeṣādhika hotā hai |

pacama karmagrantha
11 hīndriya yo aura apati kā jāya sthiti unako apekṣā saṃkhyāta guṇā aura viśeṣādhika aura usakī apekṣā dvīndriya aparyāpta aura paryāpta kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha viśeṣādhika, vīndriya, caturindriya aura asaṃjñī paṃcendriya meṃ bhī isī prakāra (dvīndriya meṃ kahe gaye anusāra) jānanā cāhiye, kintu itanā viśeṣa hai ki dvīndriya paryāpta aura asaṃtrī aparyāpta meṃ saṃkhyāta guṇā samajhanā cāhie|
usakī apekṣā sādhu. kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha saṃkhyāta guṇā aura usakī apekṣā deśavirati kā jaghanya aura utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha, samyagdṛṣṭi ke cāroṃ sthitibaṃdha aura saṃjñī paṃce. ndriya mithyāiṣṭi ke cāroṃ sthitibandha anukrama se saṃkhyāta guṇa hote haiṃ|
viśeṣārtha-- ina tīna gāthāoṃ meṃ sthitibaṃdha kā alpabahutva batalāyā gayā hai ki kisa jīva ko adhika sthitibaṃdha hotā hai aura kisa jīva ko kama sthitibaṃdha | baṃdha kī isa hīnādhikatā ko sthitibaṃdha kā alpabahutva kahate haiṃ|
sthitibaṃdha ke isa alpabahutva ke pramāṇa kā kathana prāraṃbha karate hue kahā hai ki 'jailahubaṃdho' yānī sāghu ko sabase kama sthitibaṃdha hotā hai aura vaha bhī sūkṣmasaṃparāya nāmaka dasaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ| isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki dasaveṃ guṇasthāna taka sūkṣma kaṣāya kā sadbhāva pāyā jātā hai aura kaṣāya ke dvārā sthitibaṃdha hotā hai| dasave guṇasthāna se hīna sthitibaṃdha kisī bhī jīva ko nahīṃ hotā hai | yadyapi gyārahaveṃ ādi āge ke guṇasthānoṃ meṃ eka samaya kā sthitibaṃdha hotā hai, kintu ve guṇasthāna kaṣāyarahita haiṃ, ataḥ vahāṃ sthitibaṃdha kī vivakṣā nahīṃ hai| isaliye dasaveṃ guṇasthāna se hī sthitibaṃdha ke alpa

bahutva kā kathana prāraṃbha hotā hai aura saṃjñī paṃcendriya paryāpta mithyādṛṣṭi ko sabase utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha hotā hai| jisase alpabahutva kā varṇana vahāṃ ākara samāpta ho jātā hai | arthāt sthitibaṃdha kā aspabahutva batalāne ke prasaṃga meṃ sūkṣmasaṃparāya guṇasthāna eka chora hai aura saṃjñī paṃcendriya paryāpta mithyādṛṣṭi dūsarā chora | sūkṣmasaṃparāya guṇasthāna jaghanya sthitibaṃdha kā caramabindu hai aura saṃjñī paṃcendriya paryāpta mithyādṛṣṭi utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kā caramabindu aura ina donoṃ ke bīca alpabahutva kā kathana kiyā jātā hai |
carama jaghanya sthitibaṃdha se prāraṃbha hokara carama utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha taka ke alpabahutva kā krama isa prakāra hai
1. sabase jaghanya sthitibaṃdha sūkṣmasaṃparāya guṇasthānavartī sādhuvirati ko hotā hai |
2. usase yānī sūkṣmasaṃparāya guṇasthānavartī sādhu se bādara ekendriya paryāpta kā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha asaṃkhyāta guṇā hai |
3. bādara ekendriya paryāpta se sūkṣma ekendriya paryāpta ke hone vālā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha kucha adhika hai |
4. sūkṣma ekendriya paryāpta kī apekṣā bādara ekendriya aparyāpta ke honevālā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha kucha adhika hai |
5. bādara ekendriya aparyāpta se sūkṣma ekendriya aparyāpta ke hone vālā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha kucha adhika hai |
6. usase sūkṣma ekendriya aparyāpta kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kucha adhika hai |
7. usase bādara ekendriya aparyāpta kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kucha adhika hai |
8. usase sūkṣma ekendriya paryāpta kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kucha adhika hai |

paṃcama karma grantha
163 sthinibaṃdha ke alpabahutva darśaka ina sthānoṃ kī saṃkhyā 36 hai| yadyapi jīvamamāsa ke 14 bheda haiṃ aura loka jīvasamāma kī jayanṭara aura utkṛṣṭa ke bheda se do-do sthitiyāṃ hotī haiṃ| jisase jovasamāsoṃ kī apekṣā 28 sthāna hote haiṃ, kintu sthitibaṃdha ke alpabahutva ke nirūpaṇa meṃ avirata samyagdṛṣṭi ke cāra sthāna, deśabirati ke do sthāna, saṃyata kā eka sthāna aura sūkṣmasaṃparāya kā eka sthāna aura milāne se kula chattīsa sthāna ho jāte haiṃ |
ina chattīsa sthānoṃ meṃ āge āge kā pratyeka sthāna pūrvavartī sthāna se yā to guṇita hai yā adhika hai|' ukta sthitisthānoṃ ko yadi ūpara se nīce kī ora dekhā jāye to sthiti adhikādhika hotī jātī hai, aura nīce se ūpara kī ora dekhane para sthiti ghaṭatī jātī hai| isase yaha saralatā se samajha meṃ ā jātā hai ki kauna-sā jīva adhika sthiti bāṃdhatā hai aura kauna-sā kama | ekendriya se hondriya, dvīndriya satrīndriya, vīndriya se caturindriya, caturindriya se asaṃjhī paṃcendriya beḥ adhika sthitibaṃdha hotā hai aura asaṃjī paṃcendriya se saṃyamī ke, saṃyamo se deśavirati ke, deśaviti se avirata samyagdṛṣṭi ke aura avirata sambagdṛṣṭi se saṃjñī paṃcendriya mithyāiṣṭi ke sthitibaṃdha adhika hotā hai| unameṃ bhī paryāpta ke jaghanya sthitibaṃdha se aparyāpta kā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha adhika hotā hai| isī prakāra ekendriya se
kisī rāśi meṃ guṇā karane se utpanna hone vālī rāśi guṇita rāśi kahalātī hai, jaise 4 se 2 kā guṇā karane para āṭha labdha ātā hai, yaha āṭha apane pūrvavartī 4 se do guṇita hai| kintu 4 meṃ 2 kā bhāga dekara labdha 2 ko 4 meṃ joḍa़ā jāye to 6 saṃkhyā hogī | use viśeṣādhika yā kucha adhika kahā jāyegā | kyoṃki yaha rāśi guṇādhika nahīṃ hai kintu bhāgādhika hai | muṇita aura viśeṣādhika meṃ yahī antara hai|

re
lekara asaṃjñī paṃcendriya paryanta aura asaṃjñī paṃcendriya se saṃyamī ke hone vāle uttarottara adhika sthitibaṃdha se yahī spaṣṭa hotā hai ki caitanyaśakti ke vikāsa ke sātha maṃkleśa kī saṃbhāvanā bhī adhika adhika hotī hai | ekendriya se lekara amaṃjñī paṃcendriya paryanta sabhī jīva prāyaḥ hitāhita ke viveka se rahita midhyādṛṣṭi hote haiṃ aura unameṃ itanī śakti nahīṃ hotī ki ve apanī vikasita caitanya śakti kā upayoga saṃkleśa pariṇāmoṃ ke rokane meṃ kareṃ | isalie unako uttarottara adhika hī sthitibaṃdha hotā hai, kintu saṃjñī paṃcendriya hone ke kāraṇa saṃyamī manuṣya kī śakti vikasita hotī hai| jisase saṃyamī hone ke kāraṇa saṃjñī paṃcendriya mithyādṛṣṭi kī apekṣā unakā sthitibaṃdha bahuta kama hotā hai kintu asaṃjī paṃcendriya kī apekṣā se vaha adhika hī hai |"
śataka
1 go0 karmakāḍa meṃ sthitibaṃdha kā apabahutva to nahīṃ batāyā hai kintu ekedriya ādi jīvoṃ ke avāntara bhedoṃ meṃ sthitibaṃdha kā nirūpaṇa kiyā hai | jisase alpabahutva kā jñāna ho jātā hai, ekendriya ādi jīvoṃ ke avāntara bhedoṃ ke sthitibaṃdha kā nirūpaṇa nimna krama se kiyā hai
vāsūpa bāsū varaṭṭhidīo subāa sūbāpa jahaṇakālo |
vīvadhī vijaṣṇakālo sesāṇameva vacaṇīyamadaṃ || 148 ||
bāpa - vādara sūkṣma paryāpta aura vāsūtra - vādara sūkṣma aparyāpta donoṃ milākara kāra taraha ke jīvoṃ ke karmoṃ kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti tathā mubā -- sūkṣma bādara aparyāpta subā - sūkṣma bādara paryāpta jīvoṃ ke karmoṃ kī jaghanya sthiti, isa taraha ekendriya jīva kī karmasthiti ke āṭha bheda haiṃ| bībībara: hīndriya paryāpta aura dvīndriya aparyāpta ina donoṃ kī utkṛṣṭa karmasthiti tathā hīndriya aparyāpta aura dvīndriya paryāpta dhana donoṃ kā jaghanya kāla, isa taraha dīndriya kī sthiti ke cāra bheda haiṃ | (śeṣa pṛṣṭha 115 para)

paṃcama karma grantha
yahāṃ yaha viśeṣa samajhanā cāhiye ki saṃyata ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha se lekara saṃjñī paṃcendriya aparyāpta ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha taka ke batāye po sthitibaṃna sthānoṃ kā pramāṇa ataḥkoḍākoḍa़ī sāgara hī hai| arthāt sabhī sthitibaṃdhoṃ kā pramāṇa anta koḍākoḍa़ī sāgara pramāṇa hī hogā 1' saṃjī paṃcendriya paryāpta ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kā pramāṇa sāmānya se batāye gaye utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ke pramāṇa ke samāna samajhanā cāhiye |
isī tarasa ādima se lekara sajī paṃcendriya taka ko sthiti ke bhī cāracāra bheda jānanā cāhie | arthāt bādara paryāpta kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti, mukṣma paryāpta kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti, bādara aparyāpna kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti, sūkṣma aparyāpta kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti, sūkṣma aparyāpta kī jaghanya sthiti, bādara aparyāpta kī jaghanya sthiti, mukṣma paryāpta kī jaghanya sthiti, bādara paryāpta kī jaghanya sthiti, ye ekendriya ke bhedoṃ kā krama hai | hīndriya paryāpta aura dondriya aparyāpta kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti, hīndriya aparyāpta
aura dvīndriya paryāpta kī jaghanya sthiti, isī prakāra śrīndriya ādi meṃ jānanā cāhiye |
ekendriya vīndriya ādi ke ina abāntara meṃdoṃ meṃ jo sthiti batalāī hai, vaha attarotara kama hai | unake isa krama ko nīce se ūpara kī ora pahane para karma grantha ke pratipādana ke anukūla ho jātā hai | 'okoso sanisma hoī pajjanagaraseva ||sā' | 'abhitārato 3 koḍākoḍī e' ti evaṃ maṃjasamma nakosāto āḍhattaṃ koḍākoḍīe amitarato bhavati |'
.....karmaprakṛti varṇi saṃyata ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha se lekara aparyāpta suṃjī pacendriya ke utkṛṣṭa piyatibadha taka jitanā bhī dhiniyaṃdha hai, vaha koḍākoḍa़ī sāgara ke andara hī ānanā cāhiye |

sutuṃ -- choḍa़kara naraamaratiriyā - manuṣya, deva aura tica āyu ko |
gāthārtha manuṣya, deva aura niryaṃca āyu ke sivāya mabhī prakṛtiyoṃ ko utkṛṣṭa sthiti ati maṃtrale pariṇāmoṃ se baṃdhane ke kāraṇa aśubha kahī jātī hai | jaghanya sthiti kā baṃdha vidyuddhi dvārā hotā hai|
viśeṣārtha gāthā meṃ devāvu manuṣyāyu aura niryatrāyu ko choḍa़kara
śeṣa sabhī prakṛtiyoṃ kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti ko aśubha aura jaghanya sthiti ko śubha batalāyā hai | isakā kāraṇa jana sādhāraṇa kī usa bhrāṃti kā nirākaraṇa karatā hai ki vaha śubha prakṛtiyoṃ kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti ko adhika samaya taka śubha phala dene ke kāraṇa acchā aura aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti ko adhika samaya taka aśubha phala dene ke kāraṇa burā mānatā hai| lekina śāstrakāroṃ kā kahanā hai ki adhika sthiti kā baṃdhanā acchā nahīṃ hai | kyoṃki sthitibaṃdha kā mūla kāraṇa kapāya hai aura kaṣāya kī śra eṇī ke anusāra sthitibaṃdha bhī usī śra eṇī

kā hotā hai | utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha utkṛṣṭa kaṣāya se hotā hai isīliye use acchā nahīṃ kahā jātā hai | utkṛṣṭa anubhāgabaṃdha śubha kyoṃ ?
utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ko jo aśubha mānā gayā hai, usakā kāraṇa utkṛṣṭa kaṣāya hai | isa para jijñāsū kā praśna hai ki sthitibaṃdha kī taraha anubhāgabaṃdha bhī kaṣāya se hotā hai—ṭhii aṇubhāgaṃ kasāyao' iti bacanāt | ataḥ utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kī taraha utkṛṣṭa anubhāga ko bhī aśubha mānanā cāhiye | kyoṃki donoṃ kā kāraṇa kapāya hai | kintu śāstroṃ meṃ śubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke anubhāga ko śubha aura aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke anubhāga ko aśubha batalāyā hai| __isakā samādhāna yaha hai ki sthiti aura anubhāga baṃdha kā kāraṇa kapāya avaśya hai | kintu donoṃ meṃ baḍa़ā antara hai| kyoṃki kaṣāya kī tīnatā hone para aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ anubhāga baṃdha adhika hotā hai aura śubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ kama tathā kaṣāya ko maṃdatā hone para śubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke anubhāga meṃ adhikatā aura aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke anubhāga meṃ hīnatā hotī hai | isa prakāra pratyeka prakṛti ke anubhāga baṃdha kī honādhikatā kapāya kī honādhikatā para nirbhara nahīṃ hai| kintu śubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke anubhāga baṃdha kī hīnādhikatā kaṣāya kī tīvratā aura maṃdatā para avanaṃbita hai aura aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke anubhāga baṃdha kī hīnatā aura adhikatā kāpāya kī maṃdatā aura tīvratā para| kintu sthitibaṃdha meṃ yaha bāta nahīṃ hai| kyoṃki kaṣāya kī tīnatā ke samaya śubha athavā aśabha jo bhī prakṛtiyāṃ baṃdhatī haiṃ, una maba meṃ sthitibaṃdha adhika hotā hai | anaḥ sthitibaṃdha kī apekṣā se kapāya kī nīvratā aura maṃdatā kā prabhāva sabhī prakṛtiyoṃ para eka-sā paḍa़tā hai kintu anubhāga baṃdha meṃ yaha bāta nahīṃ hai | anubhāga meṃ śubha aura aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ para pāya kā alaga-alaga prabhāva paḍa़tā hai|

દ
isī bāta ko yoṃ bhī kaha sakate haiṃ ki jaba-jaba śubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ utkṛṣṭa anubhāga hotā hai taba-taba jaghanya sthitibaṃdha hotā hai aura jaba-jaba unameṃ jadhanya anubhāgabaṃdha hotā hai taba-taba unameṃ utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha hotā hai| kyoṃki śubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ utkṛṣṭa anubhāgabaṃdha kā kāraṇa kaṣāya kī maṃdatā aura jaghanya anubhāgabaṃdha kā kāraṇa kaṣāya kī tīvra hai! lekina sthitibaṃdha meṃ kaṣṭāya kī maṃdatā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha kā kāraṇa aura kaṣāya kī tīvratā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kā kāraṇa hai| yaha to huī śubha prakṛtiyoṃ kī bāta | aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ to anubhāga adhika hone para sthiti bhī adhika hotī hai aura anubhāga kama hone para sthitibaṃdha kama hotā hai| kyoṃki donoṃ kā kāraṇa kapāya kī tīvratā hai | ataḥ utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ho aśubha hai kyoṃki usakā kāraṇa kaṣāyoṃ kī tīvratā hai aura śubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa anubhāgabaṃdha śubha hai, kyoṃki usakā kāraṇa kaṣāyoṃ kī maṃdatā hai | isīliye utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha kī taraha utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha ko sarvathā aśubha nahīṃ mānā jā makatā hai |
isa prakāra utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa se utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha aura viśuddhi se jaghanya sthitibaṃdha hotā hai, kintu devāyu, manuṣyāyu aura tiryaṃcāyu ina tīna prakṛtiyoṃ ke bāre meṃ yaha niyama lāgū nahīṃ hotā hai | kyoṃki ina tīna prakṛtiyoṃ kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti śubha mānī jātī hai aura usakā baṃdha viśuddhi se hotā hai aura jaghanya sthiti aśubha, kyoṃki usakā baṃdha saṃkleśa se hotā hai | sārāṃśa yaha hai ki ina prakṛtiyoṃ ke sivāya śeṣa prakṛtiyoṃ kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti tīvra kaṣāya se baṃdhatī hai aura jaghanya sthiti maṃda kaṣāya se | kintu ina tīna prakṛtiyoṃ kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti maṃda kaṣāya se aura jaghanya sthiti tīvra kaṣāya se baṃdhatī hai | isīliye ina tīna prakṛtiyoṃ ko grahaṇa nahīṃ kiyā gayā hai |

200
pātaka
gāthāyaṃ-sūkṣma nigodiyā lakṣyaparyāpta jīva ko pahale mamaya meṃ alpa yoga hotā hai, usakī apekṣā bādara ekendriya, vikalatrika, asaṃjñī aura saṃjñī paṃcendriya labdhyapaptika ke pahale samaya meṃ krama se asaṃkhyāta guṇā hotā hai| usake anantara prāraṃbha ke do labdhyaparyāpta arthāt mūkṣma aura vādara ekendriya kā utkṛṣṭa yoga asaṃkhyāta guṇā hai| usase donoṃ hī paryāpta kā jaghanya va utkṛṣṭa yoga anukrama se asaṃkhyāta guṇā hai|
usakī apekṣā aparyāpta asa kā utkṛṣṭa yoga, paryāpta trasa kā jaghanya aura latkṛṣṭa yoga anukrama se asaṃkhyāta guṇā hai| isī prakāra sthitisthāna bhī aparyāpta aura paryāpta ke saṃkhyāta guṇe hote haiṃ kintu aparyāpta dvīndriya ke sthitisthāna asaṃkhyāta guṇe haiṃ|
viśeṣāya-- ina do gāthāoṃ meṃ yoga ke alpabahutva kā kathana kiyā gayā hai | yoga kā artha hai sakarmā jīva kī śaktiviśeṣa jo karmoṃ ke grahaṇa karane meṃ kāraṇa hai | yoga ke dvārā karma raja ko ātmā taka lāyā jātā hai| karmaprakṛti (baṃdhanakaraṇa) meṃ yoga kī paribhāṣā isa prakāra dī gaī hai
pariṇāmā laṃbaṇa gahaṇa sāhaṇaṃ seṇa lavanāmasigaṃ || arthāt pudgaloṃ kā pariṇamana, ālambana aura grahaṇa ke sādhana yānī kāraṇa ko yoga kahate haiṃ|' ātmā meṃ vīrya-śakti hai aura

paṃcama karmagrantha
saṃsārī jīva meṃ vaha śakti dhīryāntarāya karma ke kṣaya yā kṣayopaśama se pragaṭa hotī hai | usa vīrya ke dvārā jīva pahale audārika ādi śarīroṃ ke yogya pudgaloṃ ko grahaṇa karatā hai aura grahaṇa karake unheṃ audārika ādi śarīra 25 :riṇamā : sabā bāvā, yā mana ke yogya pudgaloṃ ko grahaṇa karake unheṃ śvāsocchdāsa ādi rūpa pariṇamātā hai aura pariṇamā kara unakā avalaṃbana yānī sahāyatā letā hai | yaha krama satata calatā rahatā hai | padgaloṃ ko grahaṇa karane ke tīna nimitta hai—mana, vacana aura kāya | isīliye yoga ke bhī tīna nāma ho jāte haiṃ—manoyoga, vacanayoga, kāyayoga |' mana ke avalaṃbana se hone vāle yoga-vyāpāra ko manoyoga, vacana ke avalaṃbana se hone vāle yoga vyāpāra ko bacanayoga aura śvāsocchvāsa ādi ke avalambana se hone vāle yoga vyāpāra ko kāyayoga kahate haiṃ | sārāṃśa yaha hai ki jīva meṃ vidyamāna yoga nāmaka śakti se vaha mana, vacana, kāya ādi kā nirmāṇa karatā hai aura ye mana, vacana aura kāya usakī yoga nāmaka śakti ke avalaṃbana hote haiṃ| isa prakāra se yoga pudgaloṃ ko grahaṇa karane kā, grahaṇa kiye hae pudgaloṃ ko śarīgāni rūpa pariṇamāne kā aura unakā avalaṃbana lene kā sādhana hai|
yoga, vīrya, sthāma, utsāha, parākrama, ceṣṭā, śakti, māmarthya, ye yoga ke nāmāntara haiṃ|

śataka
33. śeṣa deva, nāraka, tiryaṃca aura manuṣyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa yoga uttarottara asaṃkhyāta guṇā hai |
isa prakāra meṃ saba jīvoṃ ke yoga kā alyabahutva jānanā cāhiye |2 mabatra guṇākāra kā pramāṇa palyopama ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga jānanā arthāt pahale-pahale yogasthāna meṃ palya ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga kā guṇā karane para āge ke yogasthāna kā pramāṇa ātā hai| isakā yaha artha huā ki jyoṃ-jyoṃ uttarottara jīva ko śakti kā vikāsa hotā jātā hai, tyoṃ-tyoṃ yogasthāna meṃ bhī vṛddhi hotī jātī hai | jaghanya yoga se jīva jaghanya pradeśabaṃdha aura utkṛṣṭa yoga se utkṛṣṭa pradeśabaṃdha karatā hai|
isa prakāra ma bogasthānoṃ ke alpabadbhutva kā kathana karane ke paścāt aba sthitisthānoṃ meṃ kathana karane meṃ - śikāṇā apajeyara saṃkhaguṇā- aparyāpta se paryāpta ke sthitisyāna saṃkhyāta guṇe haiṃ kintu
__ kapaṃprakṛti (baṃdhanakaraṇa) meṃ amajī paṃcendriya paripta ke utkṛṣṭa yoga se anutaravāmī devoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa yoga asaṃkhyāta guṇā batalāyā hai ...
amaṇāṇuttaragevina bhogabhūmigayataiyataṇugesu | phamaso asaṃkhaguṇio sesemu ma joga ukkoso || 16 ||
jaba amaṃjño paṃcendriya paryāpta ke uskṛṣṭa yoga ko kahane ke bāda anuttaravāsī devoṃ ādi ke utkṛṣṭa proga kā kathana kareṃge to 28 vā sthāna 27 dhā hogā aura kula milākara maba sthāna 32 hoṃge | karmaprakṛti meṃ isī prakāra hai| saba jīvoṃ ke yoga kā alpayatva bhagavatī 25|1 meṃ batalāyā hai | usameṃ paryāpla ke jaghanya yoga se aparyāpta kā utkṛṣṭa yoga adhika kahā hai | bola
bhī āge pīche hai| isakā kāraṇa to bahubhutagamya hai| 3 go. karmakāṃḍa nā0 218 meṃ 242 naka yogasyānoṃ kā vistṛta varṇana kiyā

13 usase saṃjñī paṃcendriya apayāṃpta ke sthātasthāna saṃkhyāta guṇe haiṃ| 14 usase saṃjī paṃcendriya paryāpta ke sthitisthāna saṃkhyāta guṇe haiṃ|
isa prakāra sthiti ke pramāṇa meṃ vṛddhi ke sātha sthitisthānī kī bhī saṃkhyā baḍha़tī jātī hai |
yoga ke prasaṃga meṃ yogoṃ ke alpābahutva, sthitisthānoṃ kā nirūpaṇa karane ke bāda aba apamāna jovoṃ ke prati samaya jitane yoga kī vṛddhi hotī hai, usakā kathana karate haiṃ|
paḍa़āvaṇa pasaṃkha gaṇaciriya apaja paṭhihamasaṃkhalogasamā | ajanasāyā ahiyā sattasu āusu asaṃkhaguṇā ||55||
śabdārya - paikhaṇaṃ- pratyeka samaya meṃ, asaṃkhapuṇaviriya - asaṃnyāta guṇā vīrya vāle, apaja–aparyāpta jīva, paiṭhiīpratyeka sthitibaṃdha meṃ, asaṃkhalogasamā -asaṃkhyāta lokākāśa ke pradeśa pramāṇa, amavasāyā –adhyatra sāya, ahiyā adhika, sattasu -- sāta karmoṃ meṃ, ājasu -āghukarma meṃ, asaṃlaguṇā - asaṃkhyāta guṇā | ___gāthārya-aparyāpta jīva pratyeka samaya asaṃkhyāta guṇe vīrya vāle hote haiṃ aura pratyeka sthitibaṃdha meṃ asaṃkhyāta lokākāśa ke pradeśa pramāṇa adhyavasāya hote haiṃ | sāta karmoṃ meṃ to sthitibaṃdha ke adhyavasāya viśeṣādhika aura āyukarma meṃ asaṃkhyāta guṇa hote haiṃ| viśeṣārtha - pūrva gāthā meṃ sthitisthānoṃ kā pramāṇa batalāyā hai | aba yahāṃ batalāte haiṃ ki aparyāpta jovoṃ ke yogamthānoṃ meṃ prati samaya asaṃkhyāta guṇī vṛddhi hotī hai kintu paryāpta jīvoṃ meṃ aisā nahīṃ hotā hai | yaha asaṃkhyāta guṇī vṛddhi utkṛṣṭa sthiti paryanta samajhanā cāhie

paṃcama karmagrantha
sabvodi apajjatto paskhaṇaṃ asaṃkhaguṇāe jogavuḍkīe baḍḍhaīti | eka-eka sthitisthāna ke kāraṇa asaṃkhyāta adhyavasāyasthāna hote haiṃ |
207
sthitibaṃdha ke kāraṇa kaṣāyajanya ātmapariṇāmoṃ ko adhyabasa sthāna kahate haiṃ| kaṣāyoṃ ke tīvra, tīvratara, tīvratama maṃda, maṃdatara, maṃdatama rūpa meṃ udaya hone se adhyavasāyasthānoṃ ke aneka bheda ho jāte hai | eka sthitibaṃdha kā kāraṇa eka hī adhyavasāyasthāna nahīṃ hai kintu aneka adhyavasāyasthāna haiṃ | arthāt eka hī sthiti nānā jīvoṃ ko nānā adhyavasāyasthānoṃ se baṃdhatī hai| jaise kucha vyaktiyoṃ ne do sāgara pramāṇa kī devāyu kā baṃdha kiyā ho, lekina yaha āvaśyaka nahīṃ ki una sabake sarvathā eka jaise pariṇāma hoṃ | isīlie eka-eka sthitisthāna ke kāraṇa adhyavasāyasthāna asaṃkhyāta lokapramāṇa kahe jāte haiṃ |
āyukarma ke sivāya jñānāvaraṇa ādi sāta karmoṃ ke adhyavasāyasthāna viśeṣādhika haiṃ| jaise jñānāvaraṇa karma kī jaghanya sthiti ke kāraṇa adhyavasāyasthāna sabase kama haiṃ, usase dvitīya sthitisthāna ke kāraṇa adhyavasāya adhika haiṃ, usase tṛtīya sthitisthāna ke kāraṇa adhyavasāyasthāna adhika haiṃ | isī prakāra cauthe, pāṃcaveṃ yāvat utkṛṣṭa sthitisthāna taka samajhanā cāhie| lekina ina sabakā sāmānya se pramāṇa asaṃkhyāta lokapramāṇa hī hai | jñānāvaraṇa kī taraha darśanāvaraṇa, bedanīya, mohanīya nāma, gotra aura aṃtarāya karma kī dvitīya ādi sthiti se lekara apane-apane utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha paryanta adhyavasāyasthānoṃ kī saṃkhyā adhika adhika jānanā cāhie|
lekina āyukarma ke adhyavasāyasthāna uttarottara asaṃkhyāta guṇe haiṃ | arthāt cāroṃ ho āyukamoṃ ke jaghanya sthitibaṃdha ke kāraṇa adhyavasāyasthāna asaṃkhyāta loka pramāṇa haiṃ aura usake bāda unake dūsare sthitibaṃdha ke kāraṇa adhyavasāyasthāna usase asaṃkhyāta guṇe haiṃ,

201
sāgaropama utkṛṣṭa abandhakāla hai | sthāvaracatuṣka, ekendriya jāti, vikalendriya aura ātapa nāmakarma kā manuṣya bhatra sahita cāra palyopama adhika ekasau pacāsī sāgaropama utkṛṣṭa avandhakāla jānanā cāhie|
pahale saṃhanana aura saṃsthāna va bihāyogati ke sivāya śeṣa pāṃca saṃhanana, pāṃca saṃsthāna, vihāyogati, anaṃtānubaṃdhī kaṣāya, mithyātva mohanīya, durbhagatrika, nīca gotra, napuṃsaka veda aura strī veda kī abaṃdhasthiti manuṣya bhava sahita ekaso battīsa sāgaropama hai| ina prakṛtiyoṃ kī abaṃdhasthiti paṃce. ndriya meṃ jānanā cāhiye|

śataka
taba naka nahīṃ ho sakatā jaba taka ve samyaktva se cyuta hokara pahale athavā dūsare guṇasthāna meṃ nahīṃ āte. kintu pahale athavā dūsare guṇasthāna me āne para bhī kabhī-kabhī ukta prakṛtiyāṃ nahīṃ baṃdhatī haiṃ | ina saba bātoṃ ko dhyāna meṃ rakhakara ukta prakṛtiyoṃ ke utkṛṣṭa abandhakāla ko ina do gāthāoṃ meṃ batalāyā hai|
ina ikatālīsa prakṛtiyoṃ ko tīna bhāgoṃ meṃ vibhājita kara abaṃdhakāla batalāyā hai | pahale bhāga meṃ sāta, dūsare bhāga meṃ nau aura tīsare bhāga meṃ paccīsa prakṛtiyoṃ kā grahaṇa kiyā hai | pahale bhāga meṃ grahaṇa kī gaī nāta prakṛtiyoṃ ke nāma isa prakāra haiṃ-tiryaṃcantrika (tiryaṃcati, tiryaṃcānupūrvī niyaṃtrāyu), narakanika (narakagati, narakānupūrvī, narakāyu)
aura udyotu | ṭanakā utkṛṣṭa abandhakāla-narabhavajuyaṃ sacauparala tesajheṃmanuṣya bhava mahita cāra palya adhika eka sau besaṭha sāgaropama batalāyā hai | jimakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa nimna prakāra hai - koī jīva tīna palya kī āyu bāṃdhakara devakuru bhogabhūmi meṃ utpanna huā| vahāṃ usake ukta sāta prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai| kyoṃki ye sāta prakṛtiyāṃ naraka, tiryaṃca gati bocca , ataḥ ina prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha vahī karatā hai jo narakagati yā tiyaṃcagati meṃ janma le sakatā hai | kintu bhogabhūmija jīva marakara niyama ma deva hī hote haiṃ| ataḥ ina naraka, tiryatra gati yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha nahīṃ karate haiṃ| isake bāda bhogabhūmi meṃ samyaktva ko prāpta karake vaha eka. palya kī sthiti vāle devoṃ meṃ utpana huā, ataḥ mamyaktva hone ke kāraṇa vahāṃ bhī usane ukta sāta prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha nahīṃ kiyā| isake bāda devagati meṃ samyaktva mahita maraṇa karake manupyati meṃ janma lekara aura dīkṣā dhāraṇa kara nauveṃ gna veyaka meṃ 31 sāgarāpama ko jiyota bālā deva huā | utpanna hone ke antamuhūrta ke yāda samyaktva kā vamana karake mithyā dṛṣṭi ho gayā | mithyāiṣṭi ho jāne para bhī maveyaka devoṃ ke ukta sāta prakṛtiyāṃ janma se ho na baṃdhane

panama karmagrantha
ke kāraṇa unakā baṃdha nahīṃ huā | vahāṃ marate samaya kṣayopaśama samyaktva ko prāpta karake manāyagati meṃ janma lekara mahāvana dhāraṇa karake do bāra vijayādika meṃ janma lekara punaḥ manuṣya huā| vahāṃ antamuhūrta ke liye samyakatva se cyuta hokara tīsare miśra guṇasthāna' meṃ calā gayā | punaḥ kṣayopaśama samyaktva ko prāpta karake tīna bāra acyuta svarga meṃ janma liyā| isa prakāra veyaka ke 31 sāgara, bijayādika meṃ do bāra janma lene ke 66 māgara aura tīna bāra acyuta svarga meṃ janma lene se vahāṃ ke 66 sāgara milāne se 163 sāgara hote haiṃ| isameṃ devakuru bhogabhumija kī āyu tīna palla, devagati ko āyu eka palya isa prakāra cāra palya aura milā denā cāhie | bīca meṃ jo manuṣyabhava dhāraṇa kiye unheṃ bhī usameṃ joḍa़kara manuṣyabhava sahita cāra palpa adhika eka sau saṭha sāgaropama ukta sāta prakṛtiyoṃ kā abaṃdhakāla hotā hai | 1 kā prathika mata se cautha guṇasthāna se dhuta hokara jīva tīsare guṇasthāna meṃ pā sakatā hai | lekina maiddhāṃtika mala isake viruddha haimicchatā maṃkatī aviruddhā hoī sammamī se mu | mīsāi vā do sammā minchana uṇa mīsa || -hatka- bhāṣya 114 - jīva mithyātva guṇasthāna me vīga aura coye guṇasthāna meṃ jā sakatā hai, isameṃ koī virodha nahīṃ haiṃ tathā miśra gṛṇasthāna se bhī pahale aura cauthe guṇasthāna meṃ jā sakatā hai, kiṃtu samyamāna ne cyusa hokara mithyātva meṃ jā sakatā hai. miśra guṇasthāna meṃ nahīṃ jā sakatā hai|
paliyāī tini bhogāṇimmi mamapaccayaṃ paliya megaṃ | sohamme mammaṇa narabhave saba viraīṇa || micchī bhavapaccapano neviuje sāgarāiṃ igatosa | aṃśamukhnuṇāī sammattaṃ tammi lihiṇaṃ || virayanarapavanarimo aguttagmage 7 ayara chātraṭṭī | misa mahattamegaṃ kāmiya maṇulo puṇo vigoṃ || chāuṭuī ayāṇaṃ accuyae virayana rabhavaṃtarito | tirinaravatigujjoyāṇa esa kālo abaṃdhami ||

mataka
isa abandhakāla ko batalāne meṃ jo veyaka meṃ samyaktva se patana batalāyā hai, vaha kṣāyopamika samyaktvakā utkṛṣṭa kāla 66 sāgara pūrā ho jāne ke kāraṇa batalāyā, hai | isī prakāra vijayādika meṃ 66 sāgara pūrṇa kara lene ke bāda manuṣya bhava meṃ jo antamuhūrta ke lie tīsare guṇasthāna meṃ gamana batalāyā hai, vaha bhī samyaktva ke 66 sāgara pūre ho jāne ke kāraṇa hī batalāyā hai| kṣāyopaśamika samyaktva kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti 66 sāgara hai|
