115
gāthārtha - vijayādika meṃ bevaka aura vijayādika meṃ tathā tamaḥ prabhā aura graiveyaka meṃ gaye jīva kī utkṛṣṭa abandhasthiti anukrama se eka so battīsa, eka sau va saṭha aura eka sau pacāsī sāgaropama manuṣyabhava sahita hotī hai| devadvika aura vaikriryādaka kā nirantara baṃdhakāla tīna palya hai |
viśeṣārtha - isase pūrva kī do gāthāoṃ meṃ jo 41 prakṛtiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa abandhakāla batalāyā vaha kisa prakāra ghaṭita hotā hai, isakā.. saṃketa yahāṃ kiyā gayā hai tathā adhruvabaṃdhinī tihattara prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se kucha prakṛtiyoṃ ke nirantara baṃdhakāla ko batalāyā hai |
yadyapi akāla kā spaṣṭīkaraṇa pūrva ko do gāthāoṃ ke bhāvārtha meṃ kara diyā gayā hai, tathāpi prasaṃgavaśāt punaḥ yahāṃ bhī karate haiṃ |
eka sau battīsa sāgara isa prakāra hote haiṃ ki vijaya, vaijayanta, jayanta aura aparājita vimānoṃ meṃ se kisī eka vimāna meṃ do bāra janma lene para eka bāra ke 66 sāgara pūrṇa hote haiṃ | phira antarmuhūrta ke liye tīsare guṇasthāna meṃ ākara punaḥ acyuta svarga meṃ tīna bāra

215
janma lene meṃ dūsarī bāra ke 66 sāgara pūrṇa hote haiṃ| hama prakāra vijayādika meṃ janma lene se 132 sāgara pūrṇa hote haiṃ|
eka sau saṭha sāgara isa prakāra hote haiṃ ki nauveṃ graṃ vebaka meṃ ikatīsa sāgara kī āyu bhogakara vahāṃ se cyuta hokara manuṣyagati meṃ janma lekara pūrva kī taraha bijayādika meṃ do bāra jāne se do bāra chiyāsaṭha māgara pūrṇa karane para eka sau vesaṭha sāgara pūrṇa hote haiṃ|
eka sau pacāsī sāgara hone ke liye isa prakāra samajhanā cāhie ki tamaḥprabhā nāmaka chaṭhe naraka meṃ bāīsa sāgara kī sthiti pūrṇa kara usake bāda naubeṃ praveyaka meṃ ikatīsa sāgara kī āyu bhogakara usake bāda vijayādika meṃ do bāra chiyāsaṭha sāgara pūre karane se eka mau pacāsī sāgara kā antarāla hotā hai ||
isa prakāra ikatālīsa prakṛtiyāṃ adhika-se-adhika itane kāla taka paṃcendriya jīva ke baṃdha ko prāpta nahīṃ hotī haiṃ|
adhruvabaṃdhinī prakṛtiyoṃ ke nirantara baṃdhakāla ke jaghanya va utkṛṣṭa pramāṇa kā vivecana prāraṃbha karate hue sarvaprathama utkṛṣṭa baṃdhakāla batalāte ki :-pallatigaṃ suraviundhiduge yānī devadvika (devagati aura devānupūrvī) tathā vaikriyādrika (vaikriya' śarīra, vaikriya aṃgopāṃga) ina cāra prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha yadi barābara hotā rahe to adhika-se-adhika tīna palya' taka ho sakatā hai| __isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki bhogabhūmija jīva janma se hī devati ke yogya ina cāra prakṛtiyoṃ ko tīna palyopama kāla taka barābara bāṃdhate haiṃ| kyoṃki bhogabhumija jīvoṃ ke narakā, tiyaṃtra aura manavagati ke yogya nāmakarma kī prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai| isalie pariṇāmoṃ meṃ antara paḍa़ne para bhī ina cāra prakṛtiyoṃ kī kisī virodhinī prakṛti kā baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai|

221
kāla utanā hī samajhanā cāhiye | kyoṃki unake avandhakāla meṃ hī inakā bandha ho sakatā hai| isa samayapramāṇa ko isa prakāra samajhanā cāhie ki
koī jīva bāīsa sāgara pramāṇa sthitibaṃdha karake chaṭhe naraka meṃ utpanna huā. vahāṃ parādhāta ādi ina sāta prakṛtiyoṃ kī pratipakṣī prakṛtiyoṃ kā bandha na hone se ina sā prakṛtiyoṃ kā nirantara baca kiyā aura aṃtima samaya meṃ samyaktva ko prāpta karake manuṣyagati meṃ janma liyā | yahā~ aṇuvratoṃ kā pālana karake cāra palya kī sthiti vāle devoṃ meṃ janma liyā aura samyaktva sahita maraṇa karake punaḥ manuṣya huā aura mahāvata dhāraṇa karake marakara nauveṃ veyaka meṃ ikatīsa sāgara kī āyu vālā deva huā| vahāṃ mithyāiṣṭi hokara marate samaya punaḥ samyaktva ko prāpta karake manuṣya huā | vahāṃ se tīna bāra maramarakara acyuta svarga meṃ janma liyā aura isa prakāra chiyāsaṭha sāgara pūrṇa kiya | antamuhūrta ke lie tīsare mina guṇasthāna meṃ āyā aura usake bāda punaḥ samyaktva prāpta kiyā aura do bāra vijayādika meṃ janma lekara chiyāsaṭha sāgara pūrṇa kiye | isa prakāra chaṭhe naraka vagairaha meṃ bhramaṇa karate hue jīva ko kahīṃ 'bhavasvabhāva se aura kahīṃ samyaktva ke kāraṇa parādhāta ādi prakṛtiyoṃ kā bandha hotā rahatā hai|

śanaka
māgara hai| anubandhino prakṛtiyāṃ hone se unakā jaghanya bandhakāla eka samaya hai lekina utkṛṣṭa bandhakāla ekasau battīsa sāgara hone kā kāraṇa yaha hai ki gāthā 57 meṃ inakī vipakṣī prakṛtiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa akāla ekasa battīsa sāgara bālāyā hai, ataḥ inakā bandhakāla usī krama se utanā hī samajhanā cāhiye 1
eka samaya se lekara antarmuhūrta taka bandhane vālī ikatālīsa prakṛtiyoṃ ke nāma isa prakāra haiṃ
aśubha vihāyogati, aśubha jāticatuṣka (ekendriya dvīndriya, bīndriya, caturindriya) vajraṛṣabhanārāca saṃhanana ko choḍa़kara śeṣa ṛṣabhanārāva ādi pāṃca aśubha saṃhanana, nyagrodhaparimaṇḍala ādi pāṃca aśubha saṃsthāna, āhārakalika, narakadrika, udyotavika, sthira, śubha, yaśaḥ kīrti, sthāvara daśaka, napuṃsakaveda, strīveda, yugaladvika, (hāsya rati aura śoka - arati) aura asātā vedanīya |
ukta ikatālīsa prakṛtiyoṃ kā nirantara vandhakāla kama-se-kama eka samaya aura adhika se adhika antamuhūrta batalāyā hai| ye prakṛtiyā~ adhdhruvabandhinī haiṃ ataḥ apanī-apanī virodhī prakṛtiyoṃ kī bandhayogya sāmagrī ke hone para inakā antarmuhūrta ke paścāt bandha ruka jātā hai | ina ikatālīsa prakṛtiyoṃ ke nirantara bandha hone ke utkṛṣṭa kāla ko antarmuhūrta mānane kā kāraṇa yaha hai ki sātā vedanīya, rati, hāsya, sthira, śubha aura yaśa kīrti kī virodhinī prakṛtiyāṃ asātā vedanīya, arati śoka, asthira, aśubha aura ayaśaḥ kīrti kā bandha chaṭhe guṇasthāna taka hotā hai, ataḥ vahā~ taka to inakā nirantara bandha antamuhūrta taka hotā hai kintu usake bāda ke guṇasthānoṃ meṃ bhī inakā bandhakāla antarmuhūrta hai, kyoṃki una guṇasthānā kā kāla bhī antamuhūrta pramāṇa hai |

﻿________________

paṃcama karmagrantha
___ manuṣyaddhika (manuṣyagati, manuṣyānupūrvī), tīrthakara nāma, vanaṛṣabhanārāja saṃhanana, ā~tārita gogāṃka mā nirantara kāla utkṛṣṭa se tetīsa sāgara hai| kyoṃki anuttaravāsī devoṃ ke manuṣyagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā ho baṃdha hotā hai| jisase ve apane janma-samaya se lekara tetīsa sāgara kī āyu taka ukta prakṛttiyoṃ kī birodhinī narakadvika, tiryaṃcadvika, deva dvika, vaikriyadvika, pāṃca aśubha saṃhanana ṛṣabhanārāca ādi kā baṃdha nahīṃ karate haiṃ| tīrthaṃkara prakṛti kī koī virodhinī prakṛti nahīṃ hai, ataḥ usakā bhī tetīsa sāgara taka barābara baṃdha hotā hai|
manuṣyadrika ādi ukta pāṃca prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se tīrthakara prakṛti ke sibāya cāra prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya baṃdhakāla eka samaya hai, kyoṃki unakī virodhinī prakṛtiyā~ haiṃ|
sāmānyataḥ yaha batāyā gayā hai ki adhruvabaṃdhinī prakṛtiyoṃ kā jadhanya baṃdhakāla eka samaya hai | lekina kucha prakṛtiyoṃ ke jaghanya baṃdhakāla meṃ viśeṣatā hone se granthakāra ne saṃketa kiyā hai ki 'lahū vi āu. jiṇe' cāra āyukarmoṃ aura tīrthakara nāmakarma kā jaghanya baṃdhakāla bhī antamuhūrta hai | arthāt tīrthakara nāmakama aura narakāyu ādi cāra āyū, kula pāṃca prakṛtiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa aura jaghanya baṃdhakāla annamuhūrta hī hai| na ki jaghanya baṃdhakāla eka samaya aura utkṛṣṭa baṃdhakāla antamuhūta hai|
āyukarma ke baṃdhakāna ke bāre meṃ pahale batā cuke haiṃ ki eka bhava meṃ eka bāra hī āyu kā baṃdha hotā hai aura vaha bho antamuharna ke liye hī hotā hai | tīrthakara prakṛti kā jaghanya baṃdha antamuharta pramāṇa isa prakāra samajhanā cāhie ki koī jīva tīrtha kara prakṛti kā baṃdha karake upaśama śreṇi caḍha़ā, bahāṃ nīce se lekara gyārahaveṃ guṇasthāna taka tīrthakara prakṛti kā baṃdha nahīṃ kiyā kyoṃki tīrthakara prakṛti ke baṃdha kā nirodha

āṭhaveṃ guṇasthāna ke chaṭhe bhāga meṃ hī ho jātā hai| punaḥ upaśama śroṇi se girakara antamuhūrta taka tīrthakara prakṛti kā baṃna karake baha jīva upaśama śrīṇi caḍha़ā aura vahāṃ usakā abandhaka huā| usa samaya tīrtha kara prakṛti kā jaghanya aṃdhakāla antamuhūrta ghaṭita hotā hai | ___ isa prakāra se adhruvabaṃdhinī prakṛtiyoṃ ke nirantara' baiśvakāla ke kathana ke sātha sthitibaṃdha kā vivecana pūrṇa hotā hai| aba āge rasabaṃdha (anubhāga baṃdha) kā vivecana karate haiṃ| rasabaṃdha ___baṃdha ke prakṛti, pradeśa, sthiti aura rasa ina cāra bhedoṃ meṃ se prakṛtibaṃdha aura sthitibaṃdha kā varṇana karane ke bāda aba rasabaṃdha athavā anubhāga baṃdha kā varṇana karate haiṃ | sabase pahale granthakāra śubha aura aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke tībra aura maṃda anubhāga baṃdha ke kāraṇoṃ ko batalāte haiṃ|

gāvārtha - aśubha aura śubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā tīvra rasa anukrama se saṃkleśa aura viśuddhi ke dvārā baṃdhatā hai | parvata, pṛthvī, retī aura pānī meṃ kī gaī rekhā ke samāna kaṣāya dvārā

226
isīlie baṃdha ko prāpta karma pudgaloṃ meṃ phala dene kī jo śakti hotī haiṃ, use rasabaṃdha athavā anubhāga baṃdha kahate haiṃ| isako aba udāharaṇa dvārā spaṣṭa karate haiṃ— jaise sukhā ghāma nīrasa hotā hai, lekina ūṃṭanī, bhaiṃsa, gāya aura bakarī ke peṭa meṃ pahu~cakara vaha dūdha ke rūpa meṃ pariṇata hotā hai tathā usake rasa meṃ cikanāī kī hīnādhikatā dekhī jātī hai| arthāt usī sūkhe ghāsa ko khākara ūṃṭanī khūba gāḍha़ā dūdha detī hai aura usame cikanāī bhī bahuta adhika hotī hai| bhaiṃsa ke dūdha meṃ usase kama gāḍha़āpana aura cikanāī rahatī hai| gāya ke dūdha meṃ usase bhī kama gāḍha़āpana aura nikatāī hai tathā bakarī ke dūdha meṃ gāya ke dūdha se bhī kama gāḍhāpana va cikanāī hotī hai| jaise hī kāra bhinna-bhinna paśuoṃ ke peṭa meṃ jākara bhinna-bhinna rasa rūpa pariṇata hotā hai, usī prakāra eka hī prakāra ke karma paramāṇu mitra- bhinna jīvoṃ ke bhinna-bhinna kapāya rūpa pariṇāmoṃ kā nimita pākara bhinna-bhinna rasa vāle ho jāte haiṃ | jo yathāsamaya apanā phala dete haiṃ |
!..
jaise ūṃṭanī ke dūdha meṃ adhika śakti hotī hai aura bakarī ke dudha meṃ kama 1 jaise hī śubha aura aśubha donoṃ hī prakāra kī prakṛtiyoṃ kā anubhāga tīvra bhī hotā hai aura maṃda bhī | arthāt anubhāga baṃdha ke do prakāra haiṃ—tīya anubhāga baṃdha aura maṃda anubhāga baṃdha | ye donoṃ prakāra ke anubhāga baṃdha śubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ bhī hote haiṃ aura aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ bhī | isīliye granthakāra te anubhāga baṃdha kā varṇana śubha aura aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke tīvra aura maṃda anubhāga baṃdha ke kāraṇoṃ ko batalāte hue prāraṃbha kiyā hai |
aśubha aura śubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke tīvra aura maṃda anubhāga baṃdha hone ke kāraṇoṃ ko batalāte hue kahā hai ki saṃkleśa pariṇāmoṃ se aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ tīvra anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai aura viśuddha bhāvoṃ se śubha

paṃcama pharmagrantha
prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ tīna anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai tathā isase viparīta bhāvoṃ se maṃda anubhāga baṃna hotā hai arthāt viśuddha bhāvoṃ se aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ maṃda anubhāga baṃdha tathā saṃkleśa bhāvoṃ se śubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ maṃda anubhāga
aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke anubhāga ko nīma vagairaha ke kaṅa ve rasa ko upamā aura śubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke anubhāga ko īkha ke rasa ko upamā dī jātī hai| isakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa yaha hai ki jaise nīma kā rasa kaṭuka hotā hai, vaise hī aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ ko aśubha phala dene ke kāraṇa unakā rasa burā samajhā jātā hai | īkha kā rasa moṭhā aura svādiṣṭa hotā hai, vaise hī śubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā rasa sukhadāyaka hotā hai |
aśubha aura śubha donoṃ hī prakāra kī prakṛtiyoṃ ke tīva aura maṃda rasa ko cāra-cāra avasthāyeṃ hotī haiṃ| jinakā prathama karmagrantha kī gāthā 2 kī vyākhyā meṃ saṃketa mātra kiyā gayā hai| yahāṃ kucha viśeṣa rūpa meṃ kathana karate haiṃ|
tīna aura saṃda rasa kī avasthāoṃ ke cāracāra prakāra isa taraha haiṃ-1-totra, 2 tīvratara, 3 tīvratama, 4 atyanta tīvra aura 1 maṃda, 2 maṃdatara, 3 maṃdatama aura atyanta maṃda | yadyapi isake asaṃkhya prakāra haiṃ vānī eka-eka ke asaṃkhya prakāra jānanā cāhiye lekina una sabakā samāveśa ina cāra sthānoṃ meṃ ho jātā hai | ina cāra prakāroṃ ko kramaśaḥ ekasthānika, dvisthānika, tristhānika aura catuḥsthānika kahā jātā hai | arthāt ekasthānika se tīna yā maṃda, dvisthānika se tīvratara yā maṃdatara, tristhānika se tīvatama yā maṃdatama aura catuḥsthānika se atyanta tīvra yā atyanta maṃda kā grahaṇa karanā cāhiye| inako isa taraha samajhanā cāhiye ki jaise nīma kā turanta nikalā huā rasa svabhāva se hī kāṭukra hotā hai jo usakī tīna avasthā hai | jaba usa tasā

śataka
228
ko agni para pakāne se sara kā ādhā sera raha jātā hai to vaha kaṭukatara ho jātā hai, yaha avasthā tīvratara hai| seṃra kā tihāī rahane para kaṭukatama ho jātā hai| yaha tīvratama avasthā hai aura jaba sara kā pāva bhara raha jātā hai jo atyanta kaṭuka hai, yaha atyanta tīvra avasthā hotī hai | yaha aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke tīvra rasa (anubhāga ) ko cāra avasthāoṃ kā dṛṣṭānta hai | śubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke tīvra rata kī cāra avasthāoṃ kā hṛṣṭānta isa prakāra hai-jaise īkha ke perane para jo svābhāvika rasa nikalatā hai, vaha svabhāva se madhura hotā hai | usa rasa ko āga para pakā kara sera kā ādhā sera kara liyā jātā hai to vaha madhuratara ho jātā hai aura sera kā eka tihāī rahane para madhuratama aura sera kā pāva bhara rahane para atyanta madhura ho jātā hai | isa prakāra totra rasa ko cāra avasthāoṃ ko samajhanā cāhiye |
aba maṃda rasa kī cāra avasthāoṃ ko spaṣṭa karate haiṃ| jaise noma ke kaṭuka rasa yā īkha ke madhura rasa meṃ eka cullū pānī ḍāla dene para baha maṃda ho jātā hai | eka gilāsa pānī ḍālane para maṃdatara, eka loṭā pānī ḍālane para mandatama tathā eka ghaḍa़ā pānī ḍālane para atyanta maṃda ho jātā hai| isī prakāra aśubha aura śubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke maṃda rasa kī maṃda, maṃdatara, mandatama aura atyanta maṃda avasthāyeṃ samajhanā cāhiye |
isa tīvratā aura maṃdatā kā kāraṇa kaṣāya kī tīvratā aura maṃdatā hai | tīvra kaṣāya se aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ totra aura śubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ maṃtra anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai aura maṃda kaṣāya se aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ maṃda aura śubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ tīvra anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai| arthāt saṃdeśa pari ṇāmoṃ kī vṛddhi aura viśuddha pariṇāmoṃ kī hāni se aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā tīvra, tīvratara, tīvratama aura atyanta tīna tathā śubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā maṃda, maṃdatara, maṃdatama aura atyanta maṃda anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai aura viśuddha pariṇāmoṃ kī vṛddhi tathā sakleśa pariṇāmoṃ kī hāni se śubha prakṛtiyoṃ

paṃcama karmagrantha
226
kā tīvra, tītara, tīvatama aura atyanta tīna anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai tathā aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā maṃda, maṃdatara. maṃdatama aura atyanta-maṃda anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai|
aba tīna aura maṃda anubhāga vaṃdha ke ukta cāra-cāra bhedoṃ ke kāraṇoṃ kā nirdeśa karate haiṃ ki 'girimahirayaṃjalarehāsarisakasāhiṃ'-parvata kī rekhā ke samāna, pṛthvī kā rekhā ke samāna, phule kī rekhā ke samāna aura jala kī rekhā ke samāna kapāya pariṇāmoṃ se kramaśaḥ atyanta tīva (catuḥsthānika), tīvatama (visthānika), tīvratara (dvisthānika) aura tīvra (ekasyānika) anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai | yaha saṃketa aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ kī apekṣā se kiyā gayā hai aura śubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ isake viparīta samajhanā cāhiye | arthāt jala va dhūli rekhā ke samāna pariṇāmoṃ se atyanta tīna (catuḥsthānika), pṛthvī ko rekhā ke samāna pariṇāmoṃ se tīvratama (tristhānika) aura parvata kī rekhā ke samāna pariṇāmoṃ se tīvratara (visthānika) anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai | śubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ tīna (ekasthānika) rama baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai, jisakā viśeṣa spaṣṭīkaraṇa nīce kiyā jā rahā hai|
pūrva meṃ yaha batāyā gayā hai ki anubhāga baṃdha kā kāraṇa kaṣāya hai aura tīvra, tīvratara ādi va maṃda, maṃdatara ādi cāra-cāra bheda anubhāga vaṃdha ke hī haiṃ| inakā kāraṇa hetu kāṣāyika pariṇāmoṃ kī avasthāyeṃ haiṃ| kapāya ke cāra bheda haiṃ krodha, māna, māyā aura lobha aura inameṃ se pratyeka kī cāra-cāra avasthāyeṃ hotī hai | arthāt krodha kapāya kī cāra avasthāyeṃ hotī haiṃ | isī prakāra māna kī, māyā ko aura lobha kī cāra-cāra avasthāyeṃ hotī haiṃ | jinake nāma kramaśaḥ anantānubaṃdhī kaṣāya, apratyākhyānāvaraṇa kapāya, pratyākhyānāvaraṇa kaṣāya aura saṃjvalana kaṣāya haiṃ | śāstrakāroṃ ne ina cāroṃ kaṣāyoṃ ke liye cāra upamāyeṃ dī haiṃ| jinakā saṃketa gāpā meṃ kiyā gayā hai| anantānubaṃdhī ruṣāya

230
mataka
kī upamā parvata kī rekhā se dī jātī hai| jaise parvata meṃ paḍa़ī darāra saikaḍa़oṃ varṣa bītane para bhī nahīṃ miṭatī hai, vaise hī anantānubaṃdhī kaṣāya ko vāsanā bhī asaṃkhya bhāgo taka banī rahatī hai | ināya ne sapā se jīva ke pariṇāma atyanta saṃkliṣṭa hote haiṃ aura pāpa prakṛtiyoṃ kā atyanta tīna rūpa catuḥsthānika anubhāga baṃdha karatā hai | kintu śubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ kevala madhuratara rūpa dvisthānika hī rasabaṃdha karatā hai, kyoṃki śubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ ekasthānika rasabaṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai |
apratyākhyānāvaraṇa kapāya ko pṛthvī kī rekhā kī upamā dī jātī hai | arthāt jaise tālāba meṃ pānī sūkha jāne para jamīna meṃ darāreṃ paḍa़ jātī haiṃ aura ve darāreṃ samaya pākara pura jātī haiṃ | isī prakāra apratyākhyānāvaraṇa kaṣāya hotī hai ki isa kaṣāya ko vāsanā bhī apane samaya para śāṃta ho jātī hai| isa kaṣāya kā udaya hone para aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ bhī visthānika rasabaṃdha hotā hai aura śubha prakṛtiyāṃ meṃ bhī visthānika rasabaṃdha hotā hai | arthāt kaṭukkatama aura madhuratama anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai|
pratyākhyānāvaraṇa kaṣāya ko bālū yā dhūli kī rekhā kī upamā dī jātī hai | jaise bālū meṃ khīṃcī gaī rekhā sthāyī nahīṃ hotī hai, jaldī hī pura jātī hai| usī taraha pratyāsthānāvaraṇa kaṣāya kī vāsanā ko samajhanā cāhie ki vaha bhī adhika samaya taka nahīṃ rahatī hai| usa kaṣāya kā udaya hone para pāpa prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ dvisthānika arthāt kaṭukatara tathā puṇya praṛtiyoṃ se catuḥsthānika rasabaṃdha hotā hai|
saṃjvalana kaṣāya kī upamā jala rekhā se dī jātī hai | jaise jala meṃ khīṃcī gaī rekhā khīṃcane ke sātha hī tatkāla miṭatī jātī hai, vaise hī saṃjvalana kapāya kī vāsanā bhī antamuhūrta meṃ hī naṣṭa ho jātī hai | isa kaṣāya kā udaya hone para puṇya prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ catuḥsthānika rasabaṃdha

pacama karmapatya
21 hotā hai aura pāpa prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ kevala ekasthānika arthāt kaṭuka rūpa hī rasabaṃdha hotā hai| ___ isa prakāra anaṃtānubandhī, apratyākhyānāvaraṇa, pratyākhyānāvaraṇa aura saṃjvalana kapāya se aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ kramaśaḥ catuḥsthānika, tristhānika, dvisthānika aura ekasthānika rasabaṃdha hotā hai tathā śubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ dvisthānika, tristhānika aura catuḥsthānika rasabandha hotā hai|
anubhāga baṃdha ke cāroṃ prakāroṃ ke kāraṇa cāroṃ kapāyoṃ ko batalākara aba kisa prakṛti meṃ kitane prakāra kā rasabandha hotā hai, yaha spaṣṭa karate haiṃ| ___ baṃdhayogya 120 prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ 82 aśubha prakṛtiyāṃ aura 42 śubha prakṛtiyāṃ haiṃ|' ina 12 pāpa prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se antarāya karma ko 5. jñānāvaraṇa ko kevalajñānāvaraṇa ko choḍa़kara śeṣa 4, darśanāvaraṇa kī kevaladarśanāvaraṇa ko choḍa़kara cakṣu darśanāvaraṇa ādi 3, saṃjvalana kapāya catuṣka
aura puruṣaveda ina satraha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ ekasthānika, dvisthānika, visthānika aura catuḥsthānika, isa prakāra cāroṃ ho prakāra kā rasabaṃdha hotā hai| kyoṃki ye satraha prakṛtiyāṃ deśaghātinī haiṃ| ghāti kau ko jo sarvaghātinī prakṛtiyāṃ haiṃ unake to sabhī spardhaka sarvaghātī hī hai kintu deśaghāti prakṛtiyoṃ ke kucha spardhaka sarvaghātī hote haiṃ aura kucha spardhaka deśaghātī | jo spardhaka tristhānika aura catuḥsthānika rasa vāle hote haiṃ 1 vaṇacatuṣka ko puṣya aura pāpa dono rūpa hona se donoṃ meṃ brahaṇa kiyā

paṃcama karmagrantha
hai, viśuddha bhāvoṃ ke hone para uttane ho sthānā se uttaratā hai tathā upaśama śreṇi baḍha़te samaya jitane viśuddhisthānoṃ para caḍha़tā hai, girate samaya utane hī saṃkleśasthānoṃ para utaratā hai| isa prakāra se to jitane makleśa ke sthāna, utane hī viśuddhi ke sthāna haiṃ| kintu jaba kṣapaka śreṇi kī dṛṣṭi se vicāra karate haiṃ to viśuddhi ke sthāna praveśa ke sthānoṃ se adhika hai | kyoṃki kṣapaka dhaṇi trane bālā jīva jina viśuddhisthānoṃ para caḍha़tā hai, una se nīce nahīṃ utaratā hai, yadi una viśuddhi ke sthānoṃ ke barābara saṃkleśasthāna bhī hote to upaśama śreṇi ke samāna kṣapaka thaṇi meṃ jīva kā patana avaśya hotā, kiṃtu aisā hotā nahīṃ hai, kṣapaka śroṇi para ārohaṇa karane ke bāda jīva nīce nahīṃ ātā hai| isakā phalitārtha yaha huā ki kṣapaka śrīṇi meṃ viśuddhi ke sthānoṃ kī saṃkhyā adhika hai aura saṃkleśasthānoṃ kī saṃkhyā viśuddhi ke sthānoṃ kī apekṣā kama | viśuddhisthānoṃ ke rahate hue śubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā kevala catuḥsthānika hī rasabaṃdha hotā hai tathā atyanta maṃkleśa sthānoṃ ke rahane para śubha praṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hī nahīṃ hotā hai| koī jīva atyanta saṃkleśā ke samaya narakagati yogya vaikriya śarīra ādi śubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha karate haiṃ, kiṃtu unake bhī bhavasvabhāva ke kāraṇa usa samaya dvisthānika hī ramabaṃdha hotā hai tathā madhyama pariṇāmoṃ se baṃdhane vālī śubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ bhī visthānika ramabaṃdha hotā hai | ataeva śubha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ kahīṃ bhī ekasthānika rasabaṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai|

śabdārtha-miṭṭaraso-nīma aura īkha kā rasa, sahajosvābhāvika tivā '-do, tīna aura cāra bhāga meṃ uvāla jāne para, ikkamāgaṃto-eka bhāga śeṣa rahe bala, igaṭhāṇāī -- ephasthānika ādi, anuho-aśuma rasa, asuhāṇaṃ-aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā, muho-śubha rasa, suhāga-śuma prakṛtiyoṃ kā, su--aura !
gāthārtha-nīma aura īkha kā svābhāvika rasa tathā usako do. tīna, cāra bhāga meṃ ubāle jāne para eka bhāga śepa rahe, use aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā ekasthānika ādi aśubha rasa aura śubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā śubha rasa jānanā cāhiye || viśeṣārtha-pūrva gāthā meṃ anubhāga baṃdha ke ekasthānika, visthānika ādi cāra bheda batalāye haiṃ| unakā viśeṣa spaṣṭīkaraṇa karane ke sāthasātha śubha aura aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke svabhāva kā bhī saṃketa yahāṃ kiyā gayā hai|

cāhiye ki nīma aura īkha kā eka-eka sera rasa lekara unheṃ bhāga para ubālā jāye aura jalakara ādhā sera raha jāye to vaha dvisthānika rasa kahā jāyegā, kyoṃki pahale ke svābhāvika rasa se usa pake hue rasa meṃ dūnī kaḍa़vāhaṭa aura dunī madhuratā ā gaī| vahī rasa ubalane para sera kā nihāī raha jātā hai to visthānika rasa samajhanā cāhie, kyoṃki usameṃ pahale ke svābhāvika rasa se tigunī kaḍa bāhaṭa yā tiganī madhuratā ā gaī hai | vahī rasa jaba ubalane para eka sera kā pāva bhara raha jātā hai to vaha catuḥsthānika rasa hai, kyoṃki pahale ke svābhāvika rasa se usameṃ caugunī kaḍa़vāhaṭa aura caugunā mīṭhāpana pāyā jātā hai|

paṃcama pharmaprastha
karate haiṃ aura ekendriya tathā sthāvara prakṛti ke utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha ke liye jitane saṃkleśa bhāvoṃ kī āvaśyakatā hai, utanā saṃkleśa hone para ve narakagati ke yogya aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā vadha karate haiṃ | kintu devagati meṃ utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa ke hone para bhī narakagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha bhavasvabhāva se hī nahīṃ hotā hai | ataḥ nāraka. manāma aura niyana ukta tīna prakṛtiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha nahīṃ karate haiṃ, lekina īśāna svarga taka ke deva hī unakā utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha karate haiṃ |
vikalanika (hīndriya, bīndriya, caturindriya), sukṣmatrika (sūkṣma, sādhāraṇa, aparyāpta), narakatrika (narakagati, narakānupūrvī, narakāyu), tithaṃcāyu aura manuṣyāyu ina gyāraha prakṛtiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha mithyā dṛṣṭi tiryaṃca aura manuṣya karate haiṃ-bigalasahamanirayatigaṃ tirimaṇuyāu tirinarā | isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki tiryaṃcāyu aura manupyāyu ke sivāya śeṣa nau prakṛtiyoṃ ko nāraka aura deva janma se hī nahīṃ bāṃdhate haiṃ tathā tiryaca aura manujya āyu kā utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha ve hī jīva karate haiṃ jo marakara bhogabhūmi meṃ janma lete haiṃ, jisase deva aura nāraka ina do prakṛtiyoṃ kā bhī utkṛṣṭa anubhāga bandha nahīṃ kara sakate haiṃ| kintu unakā utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha mithyādṛṣṭi manuṣya aura tithaṃca hī karate haiṃ | isī prakāra śeṣa prakṛtiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa anubhāga bhī apane-apane yogya saṃkleśa pariṇāmoṃ ke dhāraka mithyādṛṣṭi manuṣya aura tiryaṃna hī karate haiṃ| ataḥ ukta gyāraha prakṛtiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha mithyāiṣṭi manuṣya aura tiyaṃtroṃ ko hotā hai | ___'tiridugachevaṭa suraniriyā-tiyaṃcadvika aura sevāta nahanana ina tīna prakṛtiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha mithyā dṛṣṭi deva aura nāraka karate haiṃ| kyoṃki yadi tiryaṃca aura manuṣyoṃ meṃ utane saṃkliṣṭa pariṇāma ho to unako narakati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha hotā hai kintu deva

236
aura nāraka ati saṃkliṣṭa pariṇāma hone para tiryaṃcagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā hī baṃdha karate haiṃ | isīliye ukta tīna prakṛtiyoṃ ke utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha kā svāmī devoṃ aura nārakoṃ ko batalāyā hai |
ukta prakṛtiyoṃ ke utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha hone ke bāre meṃ itanā viśeṣa jānanā cāhiye ki devagati meṃ sevārta saṃhanana kā utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha īśāna svarga se ūpara ke sānatkumāra ādi kī karate haiṃ| eka ke dedi saṃkliṣṭa pariṇāmoṃ ke hone para ekendriya bhogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā hī baṃdha karate haiṃ, kintu sevā saṃhanana ekendriya yogya nahīṃ hai, kyoṃki ekendriyoṃ ke saṃhanana nahīṃ hotā hai |
biuvisurāhāradugaṃ sukhagaḍa bannacauteya jiṇasāyaṃ | samaca uparaghātasa sa paṇivisāsucca babagāu ||67|| tamatamagā ujjoyaṃ sammasurā maṇuyauraladugadhairaṃ | apamato amarājaṃ caugaimichā u semāṇaṃ || 68 ||

baikriyadvika, devadvika, āhārakadvika, śubha vihāyogati, varṇacatuṣka, tejamacatuṣka (taṃjasa, kārmaṇaagṛhalaghu, nirmāṇa), tīrthaṃkara, samacaturasra saṃsthāna, parāghāta, yaśa kīrti nāmakarma ko choḍa़kara sadaśaka meṃ gabhita prasa, bādara, paryāpta ādi nau prakṛtiyāṃ, paṃcendriya jāti, ucchvāsa, ina unatīsa prakṛtiyoṃ ke utkṛṣṭa anubhāga kā baṃdha āṭhaveṃ apūrvakaraṇa gaṇasthāna ke chaṭhe bhāga meṃ devagati yogya prakṛtiyoṃ ke baṃdhaviccheda ke samaya hotā hai|

śataka
242 āge ke samaya meṃ samyaktva kī prāpti hotī hai, usa samaya meṃ usa jīva ke udyota prakṛti kā utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai | kyoṃki yaha udyota prakṛti śubha hai aura viśuddha pariṇāmoṃ se hī usakā utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai tathā usake bāṃdhane vāloṃ meṃ sātaveṃ naraka kā ukā nāraka hī ati viśuddha pariṇāma vālā hai | kyoṃki anya gatiyoṃ meṃ itanī viśuddhi hone para manuṣyagati athavā devagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai | udyota prakṛti tiryaṃcagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ se hai aura sātaveṃ naraka kā nāraka marakara niyama se tiyaṃca meṃ janma letā hai, jisase sātaveṃ naraka kā nāraka mithyātva meṃ pratisamaya tiryaṃcagati yogya karmoṃ kā baṃdha karatā hai|
manuṣyadvika, audārikadvika aura vajaṛṣabhanārāca saṃhanana, ina pāṃca prakṛtiyoṃ ke utkṛṣṭa anubhāga bandha kā svāmī samyagdṛṣṭi devoṃ ko batalāyā hai—sammasurā magu uraladugadhāira| yadyapi ina pāṃca prakatiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa anubhāgabaṃdha viśuddha pariṇāma vāle nāraka bhī kara sakate haiṃ, lekina ve naraka ke duḥkhoṃ se pīḍa़ita rahane ke kāraṇa utanī viśuddhi prāpta nahīṃ kara pāte haiṃ tathā unako devoṃ kī taraha tīrthaṃkaroṃ kī vibhūti ke darśana, upadeśaśravaṇa, vaṃdana ādi pariṇāmoṃ ko viśuddha karane vālī sāmagrī bhī nahīṃ milatī hai, jisase nārakoṃ kā grahaṇa nahīṃ kiyā gayā hai | tiryaṃca aura manuṣya to ati viśuddhi pariṇāma vāle hone para devagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā hī bandha karate haiṃ | isīliye ina prakṛtiyoṃ ke utkṛṣṭa anubhāga bandha kā svāmī samyagdṛṣṭi devoṃ ko batalāyā hai|

243
isa prakāra se 42 puṇya prakṛtiyoṃ aura 14 pāpa prakṛtiyoṃ ke utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha ke svāmiyoṃ ko to alaga-alaga battalā diyā hai | inase śeṣa rahī 68 prakṛtiyoṃ ke utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha kā svāmī cāroṃ gati ke saṃśliṣṭa pariṇāmī mithyāṣṭi jīvoṃ ko batalāyā hai-caugaimicchā u sesāṇaṃ |'

śabdārtha-thotirga-styānaddhitrika, amilcha -anaṃtānutraṃdhī kaṣāya aura mithyātva modanīya kā, pasara-naghanya anubhāga baṃdha saṃjabhummuho- samyaktva caritra ke abhimukha, micyomithyādṛṣṭi, vipatiyakatāya-dūsarī aura tīsarī kapāya kā, aviraya - avirata samyagdṛṣṭi, desa–deśavirati, pamato-pramattavirata, araisoe-arati aura śoka mohanīya kā|
1 yahā~ sāmānya se 68 prakṛtiyoṃ ke utkṛṣṭa anubhāga bandhaka yāroṃ gati ke
tīna kaṣāyavaṃta mithyāiṣṭi jīva batalāye haiṃ| isameṃ utanā viśeṣa samajhanā cāhie ki hāsya, rali, strīveda, puruṣaveda, pahale aura antima ko choḍa़kara śeṣa saṃhanana aura saṃsthāna ke sivāya 56 prakṛtiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa anubhāga bandha tīna kaṣāyī cāroṃ gati ke mithyā dṛṣṭi karate haiṃ aura ukta bāraha prakṛtiyoṃ kā usa-usa prakṛti ke bandha yogya saṃvaleśa meṃ vartamāna jīva utkṛṣṭa anubhāganandhra karate haiṃ| jaise ki napusakaveda ke rasabaṃdha meṃ tīvra saṃkleśa cāhie, usakī apekṣā strīveda ko rasabaṃdha meṃ kama aura usakī apekṣā bhī puruṣaveda ke utkṛṣṭa rasabaṃdha meṃ hīna saṃklega cāhiye | so prakāra sarvatra samajhanā cāhie |

244
śataka
gāthārtha - styānaddhitrika, anaṃtānubaṃdhī kaṣāya aura mithyātva samaya kā samyaktva sahita cāritra prāpta karane ke abhimukha mithyādṛṣṭi jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha karate haiṃ| deśavirati cāritra ke sammukha huā avirata samyagdṛṣṭi dūsarī kaṣāya kā aura sarvavirati cāritra ke sanmukha hone vālā deśa - virati tīsarī kaṣāya kā aura pramattasaṃyata arati va śoka mohanīya kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha karatā hai |
viśeṣārtha - utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha ke svāmiyoṃ ko batalākara isa gāthā se jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha ke svāmiyoṃ kā kathana prārambha karate haiṃ |
pūrva meṃ yaha batalāyā gayā hai ki viśuddha pariṇāmoṃ se aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha aura saṃkleśa pariṇāmoṃ se śubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai| isa gāthā meṃ jina prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha lāyā hai, ve saba aśubha prakṛtiyāṃ hai| ataḥ unakā anubhāga baṃdha karane vāle svāmiyoṃ ke liye viśeṣaṇa diyā hai'ho' saṃyama ke abhimukha manuṣya, jo gāyā meṃ batāī gaī aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ ke jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha kā svāmī hai |
gāthā meṃ āye isa 'saṃjamummuha' pada ko pratyeka ke sātha lagāyā jātā hai arthāt jo saṃyama dhāraṇa karane ke abhimukha hai - jo jīva tatkāla dūsare samaya meṃ hī saṃyama dhāraṇa kara legā, usake apane-apane usa guṇasthāna ke aṃtima samaya meṃ usa prakṛti kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai| yahāṃ saṃyama ke abhimukha pada ko pratyeka guṇasthāna ke sātha joḍa़kara āśaya samajhanā cāhiye| jo isa prakāra hai - styānaddhitrika, anubaṃdhī kapācaka aura mithyātva mohanīya ina āṭha prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha samyaktva saṃyama ke abhimukha midhyādṛṣṭi jīva apane guṇasthāna ke aṃtima samaya meṃ karatā hai| apratyākhyānāvaraṇa

245 kapāyacatuṣka kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha saṃyama-deśasaṃyama ke abhimukha avirata samyagdṛṣṭi jīva apane guṇasthāna ke anta samaya meṃ karatā hai| pratyākhyānāvaraṇa kaṣāyacatuṣka kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha saṃyama arthāt sarvavirati-mahāvatoṃ ko dhāraṇa karane ke sanmukha deśavirati guṇasthāna vālā joba apane guṇasthāna ke aṃta samaya meṃ karatā hai tathā arati va śoka kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha saṃyama arthāt apramatta saṃyama ke abhibhukha pramatta muni apane guṇasthāna ke anta meṃ karatā hai | ' sārāṃśa yaha hai ki styānaddhitrika ādi āṭha prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha pahale guṇasthāna vālā jaba samyaktva ke abhimukha hokara cauthe guṇasthāna meṃ jātā hai taba pahale guṇasthāna ke antima samaya meṃ karatā hai | apratyākhyānādharaṇa kaṣāyacatuṣka kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha pāṃcaveṃ gaṇasthāna-deviti
'sajamummuhati samma kasvasaṃkamāmimukhaḥ samyaktvasāmāyika pratipitsaḥ" | apratyāsthānāvaraṇa lakṣaṇastra " avirata sabhyamhaṣṭi saṃpagāmimukha:-deśaviratisāmāyika pratipitlumandarasaṃ vaghnāti | tathā tṛtīyakaṣāyacatuṣṭayasya deśa virati: " saṃyamonmukhaḥ""sarvaviratisāmāyika praninsuimandarasaṃ baghnāti | tathā pramattamatiḥ saṃyamonpuna:-apramattasaṃyama prati pityaḥ-|

kī ora unmukha cauthā guṇasthānavī jīva cauthe guṇasthāna ke antima samaya meṃ karatā hai | pratyākhyānāvaraṇa kaṣāyacatuṣka kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha pāṃcaveṃ guṇasthāna se chaṭhe guṇasthāna meṃ jātā hai taba pāṃcaveṃ guṇasthāna ke antima samaya meṃ tathā arati aura śoka ina do prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha chaṭhe guṇasthāna se sātaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ jāne dālā chaṭhe guṇasthāna ke aṃtima samaya meṃ karatā hai| yānī āge-āge kā guṇasthāna prāpta karane se pahale samaya meṃ styānaddhivika ādi prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai|
ukta prakṛtiyoṃ ke jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha hone ke prasaṃga meṃ itanā aura samajha lenā cāhiye ki yadi pahale guṇasthāna se cauthe guṇasthāna meṃ na jākara pāṃcaveṃ yā chaṭhe yā sāta guṇasthāna meṃ jāye, isī taraha cauthe guṇasthāna se pāṃcaveṃ meṃ na jākara chaṭhe yā sātaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ jāye to bhī unakā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha hogā| kyoṃki ukta prakṛtiyoṃ ke jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha ke liye viśuddha pariṇāmoṃ kī āvaśyakatā hai aura usa daśā meṃ to pahale se bhī adhika viśuddha pariṇāma hote haiṃ| isī se gāthā meṃ 'saṃjamummuho' pada diyā gayā hai| jisakā yaha artha hai ki amukaamuka guṇasthāna vāle saṃyama ke medoṃ meṃ se kisī bhī saṃyama kī ora abhimukha hote haiṃ to unako ukta prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai|
aba āge anya prakṛtiyoṃ ke jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha ke svāmiyoṃ ko batalāte haiṃ|
apamāi hāragadugaṃ duniasuvamahāsarahakucchā | bhayamuvadhāyamapuskho aniyaṭṭī purisasaṃjalaṇe ||7||

cama karma grantha
247
nāmakarma kā, apuṣyo--apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna vālā, aniyaṭṭīanivṛttivādara guṇasthāna bālā, purisa .. puruṣa veda, saṃjalaṇesaṃjvalana kaṣāya kā|
gāyā -āhārakaddhika kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha apramatta muni karate haiṃ| do nidrā, apraśasta varṇacatuṣka, hāsya, rati, jugupsā, bhaya aura upaghāta nāmakarma kā apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthāna vāle jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha karate haiṃ aura anivṛttibādara guṇasthānavartī puruṣa veda, saṃjvalana kaṣāya kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha karate haiṃ|
viśeṣārtha - isa gāthā meṃ āhārakadvika ādi prakṛtiyoṃ ke jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha ke svāmiyoṃ ko batalāte haiṃ|
sarvaprathama āhārakadvika ke bāre meṃ kahate haiṃ ki 'apamāi hāragadurga' āhārakadvika (āhāraka śarīra aura āhāraka aṃgopāṃga) kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha apramatta muni-sāta apramatta saṃyaṃta guṇasthānavartī muni karate haiṃ | lekina kaba karate haiṃ, isakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa yaha hai ki āhārakadvika yaha praśasta prakṛtiyāṃ haiṃ ataḥ inakā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha apramattamuni usa samaya karate haiṃ jaba ve chaṭhe pramatta saṃyata guṇasthāna ke abhimukha hote haiṃ | yāni sātaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ chaṭhe guṇasthāna kī ora avarohaṇa karane kī sthiti meṃ hote haiṃ taba unake pariṇāma saṃkliṣṭa hote haiṃ aura usa sthiti meṃ āhārakadvika kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha karate haiṃ|
nidrādvika (nidrā aura pracalā), aśubha varṇacatuṣka, (aśubha varṇa, aśubha gaṃdha, aśubha rasa, aśubha sparsa) tathā hāsya, rati, jugupsā, bhaya aura upaghāta, ina gyāraha prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha apūrvakaraṇa guṇasthānavāle tathā puruṣa veda aura saṃjvalana kaṣāya kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha anivṛttibādarasaṃparāya guṇasthāna vāle karate haiṃ| yahā~ ye donoṃ

mūkṣmatrika (sūkṣma, sādhāraṇa, aparyāpta nāmakama), vikalatrika, cāra āyu aura vaikriyaṣaṭaka (bakriya śarīra, traikiya aṃgopāṃga, devagati, devānupūrvī, narakagati, narakānupūrvī), ina solaha prakṛtiyoṃ ke jaghanya anubhāga ke svāmī manuṣya aura tiryaṃca haiṃ | ina solaha prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ te manuṣyāyu aura tiryaṃcāyu ke sivāya caudaha prakṛtiyoṃ ko to deva va nāraka janma se hī nahīṃ bāṃdhate haiṃ tathā manuṣya aura tiryaṃca āyu kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha jaghanya sthitibaṃdha ke sātha hī hotā hai| kyoṃki ye donoṃ praśasta prakṛtiyāṃ haiṃ ataḥ inakā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha to saṃkleśa pariṇāmoṃ se hotā hī hai kintu jaghanya sthitibaṃdha bhī saṃkleśa pariṇāmoṃ se hotā hai | deva aura nāraka jaghanya sthiti vāle manuṣya

śakṣaka
vandha na hone kā kāraṇa yaha hai ki jo jīva tiryaṃcati ke yogya prakatiyoṃ kā bandha karatā hai, vahī inakā bhī jaghanya anubhāga bandha karatā hai | kintu manuṣya aura tiryaṃcoṃ ke utane saṃkliṣṭa pariṇāma hoṃ jitane ki ina tīna prakṛtiyoṃ ke jaghanya anubhāgabaṃdha ke liye āvaśyaka haiṃ to be narakagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā ho bandha karate haiṃ | isīliye manuṣya aura tiryaṃcoṃ ko ina prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāgabaṃdha nahīṃ batāyā hai | tiriduganiaṃ tamatamā jimaviraya nirayaviṇigathāvarayaṃ | āsuhamāyaba sammo va sāthirasubhajasā siarā ||72||
śabdārtha tiriduga–tiyaṃcadvika, niraṃ--nīcagotra kā, tamatamā - tamaḥtamaprabhā ke cāraka jiṇaṃ-tīrthakara nāmakarma kā, aniraya-avirata samyagdṛṣṭi manuṣya, niraviṇa - naraka ke sivāya tīna gati vāle jīva, igāvarayaṃ-ekendriya āti aura sthāvara nāmakarma kā, āmuhamā saudharma īśāna svarga taka ke deva, āyava ātapa nāmakarma kā, sammo va- samyagdṛṣṭi athavā mithyāṣṭi, sāyadhirasubhajasāmānāvedanīya, sthira nāma, śubha nāma aura śakīpti nāmakoṃ kā, siarā-inako pratipakṣī prakṛtiyoṃ sahita |
gāthārtha – tiyaṃcadvika aura nocagotra kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha tamaḥtamaprabhā nāmaka sātaveṃ naraka ke nāraka karate haiṃ | tīrthakara nāmakarma kā jaghanya anubhāgabandha avirata samyagdṛṣṭi jīva karatā hai| narakagati ke sivāya śeṣa tīna gati vāle jīva ekendriya jāti aura sthāvara nāmakarma kā jaghanya anubhāgabandha karate haiṃ | saudharma aura īśāna svarga taka ke deva ātapa nāmakarma kā jaghanya anubhāgabaṃdha karate haiṃ | sātāvedanīya, sthira, śubha, yaśaḥkīrti aura ina cāroṃ ko pratipakṣī prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāgabaṃdha samyagdṛṣṭi athavā mithyādṛṣṭi jīva karate haiṃ|

viśeṣārtha-'tiridugani tamatamā' tiryaṃcagati, tiyaṃcānapūrvī aura nīcagotra ina tīna prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāgabaṃdha sāta naraka meṃ batalāyā hai | jisakā spaṣṭīkaraṇa yaha hai ki sātaveṃ naraka kā koī nāraka samyaktva kī prāpti ke liye jaba yathāpravṛtta ādi tīna karaṇoṃ ko karatā huā anta ke anivṛtikaraṇa ko karatā haiṃ taba vahāṃ anivṛttikaraṇa ke antima samaya meṃ ina tīna prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāgabaṃdha hotā hai| ye tīnoṃ prakṛtiyāṃ aśubha haiṃ aura sarvaviśuddha jīva hī unakā jaghanya anubhāgabaṃdha karatā hai | ataḥ inake baṃdhakoṃ meṃ sātave naraka kā ukta nāraka hī viśeṣa viśuddha hai | kyoṃki isa sarīkhī viśuddhi hone para to dūsare jīva manuṣyadvika aura ucca gotra kā bandha karate haiṃ | jisase tiyaṃcadvika aura nīca gotra ina tīna prakṛtiyoṃ ke liye sātaveṃ naraka ke nāraka kā grahaṇa kiyā hai|

ekendriya jāti aura sthāvara nāmakarma kā jaghanya anubhāga bandha narakagati ke sivāya śeṣa tiryaca, manuṣya aura deva ina tona gatiyoṃ ke jīva karate haiṃ| lekina ina tīna gatiyoṃ vāle jīvoṃ ke saṃbandha meṃ yaha viśeṣa jānanā ki parāvartamāna madhyama pariṇāma vāle jīva karate haiṃ | kyoṃki ye donoṃ prakṛtiyāṃ aśubha haiṃ, ataḥ ati saṃkliṣṭa pariṇāma vāle jīva unakā utkṛṣṭa anubhāga bandha karate haiṃ aura ati viśuddha jīva paṃcendriya jāti aura tasa nāmakarma kā bandha karate haiṃ| isīliye madhyama pariṇāma kā grahaṇa kiyā hai| sārāṃśa yaha hai ki jaba koī jīva ekendriya jāti aura sthāvara nāmakarma kā bandha karake paṃcendriya jāti aura asa nāmakarma kā baṃdha karatā hai aura unakā baṃdha karake punaḥ ekendriya va sthāvara nāmakarma kā baṃdha karatā hai taba isa prakāra kā parivartana karake baṃdha karane vālā parāvartamāna madhyama pariṇāma vālā apane yogya viśuddhi ke hone para ukta do prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāgabaṃdha karatā hai|

kāraṇa yaha hai ki ātapa śubha prakṛti hai aura śubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha viśeṣa makliṣṭa pariṇāmoṃ se hotā hai | ataḥ una devoṃ ke ekendriya ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ ke baṃdha ke samaya ātapa prakṛti kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai | yadi ātapa prakṛti ke jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha karane yogya saṃklipṭa pariṇāma manuṣya aura tiryaṃcoṃ ke hoṃ to ve narakagati ke yogya prakṛtiyoṃ kā hī bandha karate haiṃ tathā nāraka aura sānatkumāra ādi kalpoṃ ke deva janma se hī isa prakṛti kā bandha nahīṃ karate haiṃ| isīliye īśāna svarga taka jo devoṃ ko hī isakā vandhaka batalāyā hai |

isakā sārāṃśa yaha hai ki sātā vedanīya ke jaghanya anubhāga bandha ke yogya parāvartamāna madhyama pariṇāma sātā vedanīya kī pandraha koḍākoḍa़ī sāgara sthitibaṃdha se lekara chaṭhe guṇasthāna meṃ asātāvedanīya ke antaḥkoḍākoḍa़ī sāgara pramāṇa jaghanya sthinibaṃdha taka pāye jāte haiṃ | parāvartamāna pariṇāma labhī taka ho sakate haiṃ jaba taka pratipakṣī prakṛti kā baṃdha hotā hai | yānī taba taka sātā ke sātha asātā vedanīya kā bhī baṃdha saṃbhava hai jaba taka parāvartamāna pariṇāma hote haiṃ | lekina sātā vedanīya ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha se lekara āge jo pariṇāma hote haiṃ ve itane saṃkliṣṭa hote haiṃ ki unase asātā vedanīya kā hī baṃdha ho sakatā hai | isīliye sātā aura asātā vedanīya ke jaghanya anubhāgavata kā svāmī parāvartamāna madhyama pariṇāma vāle samyagdṛṣṭi aura mithyāiṣṭi jīvoṃ ko batalāyā hai | ___ asthira, aśubha, ayaśaḥkīti kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti bīsa koḍākoḍa़ī sāgara aura sthira, śubha, yaśaḥkorti kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti dasa koḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgara batalāī hai | pramatta muni asthira, aśubha, ayaśaḥkīti kī antākoḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgara pramāṇa jaghanya sthiti bāṃdhatā hai aura viśuddhi ke kāraṇa phira inakī pratipakṣī sthira, śubha, yaśakīrti kā baṃdha karatā hai, usake bāda punaḥ asthira ādika kā baṃdha karatā hai| isī prakāra deśavirati, avirata samyagdṛṣṭi, minadṛṣṭi, sāsādana, mithyādṛṣṭi sthirādika ke bāda asthirādika kā aura asthirādika ke bāda sthirādika kā baṃdha karate haiṃ| unameṃ se mithyādṛṣṭi ina prakṛtiyoṃ kā ukta prakāra se taba taka baṃdha karatā hai jaba taka sthirādika kā utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai| samyagdṛṣṭi aura mithyāiṣṭi ke yogya ina' sthitibaṃdhoṃ meṃ hī ukta prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai | spoṃki mithyādṛṣṭa guṇasthāna meṃ sthirāvika ke utkṛṣṭa sthitibaṃdha ke

paścāta to asthirādika kā hī baṃdha hotā hai aura apramatta ādi guṇasthānoṃ meṃ sthirādika kā hī | mithyā dṛṣṭi meṃ saṃkleśa pariṇāmoṃ kī adhikatā hai aura apramatta meṃ viśuddha pariṇāmoṃ ko adhikatā, ataḥ donoṃ meṃ hī anubhāga baṃdha adhika mātrā meṃ hotā hai | isīlie ina donoṃ ke sivāya śeṣa batāye gaye sthānoṃ meṃ hī asthira ādi chaha prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai|

śabdārtha-tasavannateyaghau- savatuṣka, varṇacatuṣka, taṃjasacatuṣka, maṇukhagaiduga—manuṣya nika, vihāyogati dvika, paṇivi-paṃcendriya jāti, sāsa-ucchvāsa nāmakamaṃ, paraghucca-parāghāta nāma aura ucca gotra kā, saṃghayaṇāgiha-chaha saṃhanana aura chaha esthāna, maputthī-napusakaveda, strīveva, sumagiyarati-subhagatrika aura itara durmagatrika kā, minṭa - mithyā dṛṣṭi, ghajagaiyā-cāroṃ gati vāle |

gāthā meṃ batāye gaye khedoṃ kā vivaraṇa isa prakāra hai ki tejasacatuṣka (taṃjasa, kārmaṇa, agurulaghu, nirmāṇa) tathā varṇacatuṣka-varṇa, gaṃdha, rasa aura sparśa (yahāṃ śubha varṇacatuṣka samajhanā cāhiye), bedanīya karma aura nāmakarma kā anutkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha sādi, anādi, dhruva, adhruva isa prakāra bāra naraha kā hotā hai | jo isa prakāra hai - __ tejasacatuṣka aura śubha varṇacatuṣka ina āṭha prakṛtiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha kṣapaka apūrvakaraṇa guṇasyāna meṃ avagati yogya tīsa prakṛ. tiyoṃ ke bandhaviccheda ke samaya hotā hai | isake sivāya upaśama aṃṇi ādi anya sthānoṃ meṃ ukta prakṛtiyoṃ kā anutkṛṣṭa badha ho hotā hai | kintu gyāraha gṛṇasthāna meṃ bilkula baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai kora gyārahaveṃ guṇasthāna se girakara koī jāṃca ukta prakṛtiyoṃ kā punaḥ anutkṛṣṭa anu. bhāga bandha karatā hai taba vaha sādi kahalātā hai aura isa avasthā ko prāpta hone se pahale unakā baṃdha anādi kahalātā hai, kyoṃki usake vaha baidha anādi se hotā calā ā rahā hai | bhavya jīva kā baṃdha adhruva

vicāra karate haiṃ | tejasa catuṣka ke sivāya śeṣa dhruvabaṃdhinī prakṛtiyoṃ kā ajaghanya anubhāga baṃdha cāra prakāra kā hotā hai| pāṃca jñānāvaraṇa, cāra darśanāvaraṇa aura pāṃca aṃtarāya, ye caudaha prakṛtiyāṃ aśubha haiṃ

pratyākhyānāvaraṇa kaṣāya catuṣka kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha deśavirati guṇasthāna ke aṃta meṃ saṃyamābhimukha karatā hai aura usase pahale hone vālā baṃdha ajaghanya baṃdha hai| apratyākhyānāvaraṇa kaṣāya catuṣka kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha kṣāyika samyaktva aura saṃyama prāpta karane kā icchuka avirata samyagdṛṣṭi jīva apane guṇasthāna ke aṃta meṃ karatā hai|

263 isake sivāya sarvatra usakā ajaghanya anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai| styānaddhi, nidrā-nidrā aura pratralā-pracalā, mithyātva aura anantānabaṃdhī kaṣāya kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha viśuddha pariṇāmī mithyādaṣṭi apane guṇasthāna ke aṃtima samaya meṃ karatā hai aura śeṣa sarvatra unakā ajaghanya anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai| usake bāda saṃyama vagairaha ko prāpta karake vahāṃ se girakara punaḥ unakā ajaghanya anubhāga baṃdha karatā hai to vaha sādi aura usake pahale kā anādi, abhavya kā baṃdha dhruva aura bhavya kā baṃdha adhruva hotā hai| isa prakāra 43 dhruvaprakṛtiyoṃ kā ajaghanya anubhāga baṃdha cāra prakāra kā hotā hai| ____ aba unake jaghanya, utkṛṣṭa aura anutkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha ke do-do prakāroṃ ko spaṣṭa karate haiṃ | ukta 43 prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha sūkṣmasaṃparāya ādi guṇasthānoṃ meṃ hotā hai jo una-una guṇasthānoṃ meṃ pahalī bāra hone se sādi hai| bārahaveṃ ādi ūpara ke guṇasthānoṃ meṃ nahīṃ hone se adhruva hai| utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa vālā paryāpta saṃjñī paṃcendriya mithyā dṛṣṭi jīva karatā hai jo eka yā do samaya taka hotā hai | usake bāda anutkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha karatā hai| kālāntara meṃ utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa ke hone para punaḥ unakā utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai| isa prakāra utkṛṣṭa aura anutkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha meṃ sādi
aura adhruva do hī bikalpa hote haiṃ| ____ aba adhruvabaṃdhinī prakṛtiyoṃ ke utkṛṣṭa ādi cāroṃ anubhāga baṃdhoṃ ko batalāte haiṃ | adhruvabaṃdhinī hone se ina prakṛtiyoṃ ke utkṛṣṭa, anu skṛṣṭa, jaghanya aura ajaghanya anubhāga baṃdha ke sādi aura adhruva yaha do prakāra hote haiṃ|

264
śataka
hai | aśubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha aura śubha prakṛtiyoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha viśuda pariṇāmī baṃdhaka karanā hai | jānāvaraṇa. darśanāvaraṇa aura aṃtarāya aśubha haiṃ ataḥ inakā jaghanya anubhāga vaṃtra apaka sūkṣmasaṃparāya' mṛṇasthāna ke aṃta samaya meṃ hotā hai aura mohanīya kā baṃdha nauveṃ gaṇasthāna taka hotā hai | jisase nauveṃ guṇasthāna ke aṃta meṃ usakā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai | ina guṇasthānoṃ ke sivāya śeṣa sabhī sthānoṃ meṃ ukta cāroṃ karmoṃ kā ajaghanya anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai | gyārahaveṃ aura dasaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ ukta cāroṃ karmoṃ kā baṃdha na karake vahāṃ se girane ke bāda jaba punaḥ unakā ajaghanya anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai taba vaha sādi hai aura jo jīva nauveṃ, dasaveṃ ādi guṇasthānoṃ meṃ kabhī nahīṃ āye, unako apekṣā vaha ajaghanya baṃdha anādi hai | abhavya kā baṃdha dhruva hai aura bhavya kā baṃdha adhruva hai |
aba ghātikarmoṃ ke śeṣa tona-jaghanya, utkṛṣṭa aura anutkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdhoṃ meṃ hone vāle sādi aura adhruva prakāroṃ ko spaṣṭa karate haiṃ| mohanīya karma kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha kṣapaka anivṛttibādara ke aṃtima samaya meṃ aura śeṣa jñānāvaraṇa, darśanāvaraṇa aura aṃtarāya kā kṣapaka sūkṣmasaṃparāya guṇasthāna ke anta meṃ | yaha baṃdha pahalī bāra hī hotā hai ataḥ sādi hai aura bārahaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ jāne para hotā hī nahīṃ ataḥ adhruva hai| yaha anādi nahīṃ hai | kyoṃki ukta guṇasthānoṃ meṃ āne se pahale kabhī nahīṃ hotā hai aura abhavya ke nahīṃ hone se dhruva bhī nahīṃ hai | anutkṛṣṭa ke bāda utkṛṣṭa baṃdha hotā hai ataḥ sādi hai aura usake eka yā do samaya bāda punaḥ anutkuṣṭa baṃdha hotā hai ataḥ utkṛṣṭa baṃdha adhruva hai aura anutkṛṣṭa baṃdha sādi hai | kama-se-kama antamuhūrta aura adhika-se-adhika anantānanta utsarpiṇī aura avasarpiṇī kāla ke bāda utkṛṣṭa saṃkleśa hone para punaḥ utkṛṣṭa baṃdha hotā hai

265
jisase anutkṛṣṭa baṃdha adhruva hai | isa prakāra utkṛṣṭa aura anutkṛṣṭa baṃdha badalate rahane ke kāraṇa mādi aura adhruva haiṃ|
gotra karma meṃ ajaghanya aura anutkṛṣṭa baṃdha cāra prakāra kā aura jaghanya aura utkṛṣṭa baṃdha do prakāra kā hotā hai | salkā aura anutkṛṣṭa anubhāga baṃdha ke prakāra vedanīya aura nāma kama ke samāna samajhanā cāhiye | aba javanya aura ajaghanya baṃdha ke bāre meṃ vicāra karate haiṃ ki sātaveṃ naraka kā nāraka samyaktva ke abhimukha hotā huā yathāpravṛtta ādi tīna karaṇoṃ ko karatā hai taba anivṛttikaraṇa meṃ mithyātva kā antarakaraṇa karatā hai, jisase mithyātva kī sthiti ke ho bhā ho bhāle | eka noje kī antamuhūrta pramāṇa sthiti aura dūsaro śeṣa para kī sthiti | nīce kī sthiti kā anubhava karate hae antumuharta pramāṇa sthiti ke antima samaya meṃ nīca gotra kī apekṣā se gotra karma kā jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai| anya sthāna meṃ yadi itano viśuddhi ho to usase ucca gotra kā ajaghanya anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai | sātaveṃ naraka meṃ mithyātva daśā meṃ nīca gotra kā hī baṃdha hone se usakā grahaṇa kiyā hai tathā jo nāraka mithyā dṛṣṭi samyaktva ke abhimukha nahī, usake noca gotra kā ajaghanya anubhāga baṃdha aura samyaktva prāpti hone para ucca gotra kā ajaghanya anubhāga baṃdha hotā hai| nīca gotra kā yaha jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha anyatra sambhava nahīṃ hai aura usī avasthā meṃ pahalī bāra hone se sādi hai | samyaktva kī prāpti hone para bahī jova ucca gotra kī apekṣā se nīca gotra kā ajaghanya anubhāga baṃdha karatā hai ataḥ jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha adhruva hai aura ajaghanya anubhāga baṃdha sādi hai | isase pahale honevālā ajaghanya anubhāga baṃdha anādi hai | abhavya kā ajaghanya baṃdha dhruva aura bhavya kā adhruva hai| isa prakāra gotra karma ke jaghanya anubhāga baṃdha ke do aura ajaghanya anubhāga baṃdha ke cāra vikalpa jānanā cāhie|

296
ghātaka
___ āyukarma ke jaghanya, ulkaṣṭa aura anuhaṭa anubhāga meṃ śādi
aura adhruva ye do hī vikalpa hote haiṃ | kyoṃki bhupayamāna āyu ke vibhāga meṃ hī āyu karma kā baṃdha hotā hai jisase usakā jaghanyādi rūpa anubhāga baṃdha sādi hai aura antamuharta ke bāda usa baṃdha ke avaśya ruka jāne se adhruva hai | isa prakāra āyukarma ke jaghanya ādi anubhāga baṃdhoṃ ke sādi aura adhruva prakāra samajhanā cāhiye|
isa prakāra se mūla evaṃ uttara prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ utkṛṣṭa ādi anubhāga baṃdhoṃ ke sādi ādi bhaṃgoṃ ko jānanā cāhiye |' aba anubhāga baṃdha kā varṇana karane ke paścāta āge pradeśabaṃdha kā vivecana prārambha karate haiṃ| pradeśabaṃdha ke prārambha meṃ sarvaprathama vargaṇāoṃ kā nirūpaṇa karate haiṃ | praveśabaṃdha
...igadugaṇugāī jā abhavaṇaṃtaguṇiyānū | khaṃghā uralodhiyavaggaṇā u taha agahaṇatariyā ||75||
śabdārtha--igaḍugaṇugāha-ekāṇuka, hamaṇuka nādi, jā--yāvat, gaka anavaṇatāṇiyā – abhadhya meṃ anaṃta guṇe paramāṇu vālā khaṃdhā-pha, urasāniyavaggaṇā---audārika ke yogya vargaṇā, taha...-tathā, agahaṇaṃtariyā-praṇayogya vargaṇā ke bīca agrahaṇayogya vargaṇā |
gāthārṣa- ekāṇuka, yaṇaka ādi se lekara abhavya jīvoṃ se bhī anantaguṇe paramāṇu vāle skaṃdhoṃ taka hī audārika kī
go0 karmakāṃḍa meṃ anubhāga baṃdhna ke jaghanya, ajaghanya āvi prakāroṃ meṃ sādi ādi kā vivāra do gāthāoṃ meṃ kiyā gayā hai| eka meṃ mūla prakṛtiyoṃ kī apekṣā, dūsarī meṃ uttara prakṛtiyoṃ kī apekṣā | ukta vicāra karmagraṃtha ke samāna hai| gāthāyeṃ pariśiṣṭa meṃ dekhiye |

paṃcama pharma grantha
grahaṇayogya vargaṇā hotī hai tathā epha-eka paramāṇu kī vṛddhi se grahaṇayogya vargaṇā se antarita agrahaṇayogya vargaṇā hotī hai|
viśeṣārya-yaha loka paramāṇu aura skaṃdha rūpa pudgaloṃ se ṭhasāṭhasa bharā huā hai aura pudgalakāya aneka vargaṇāoṃ meṃ vibhājita hai, jinameṃ eka karmavarga bhī hai| ye narmamā jīma ke yoga aura kaṣāya kā nimitta pākara karma rūpa pariṇata ho jātī haiṃ | pudgala ke eka paramāṇu ke avagāhasthāna ko pradeśa kahate haiṃ| ataḥ karma rūpa pariṇata hue pudgala skaṃdhoṃ kā parimāṇa paramāṇu dvārā āṃkā jātā hai ki amuka samaya meṃ itane paramāṇu vāle pudgalaskandha amuka jīva ko karma rūpa meṃ pariṇata hue haiṃ, isī ko pradeśabaṃdha kahate hai | ataḥ pradeśabaṃdha kā svarūpa samajhane ke pūrva karmavargaṇā kā jñāna honā jarūrī hai| karmavargaṇā kā svarūpa samajhane ke lie bhī usake pūrva kī audārika ādi vargaṇāoṃ kā svarūpa jāna liyā jāye| isīliye una-una vargaṇāoṃ kā bhī svarūpa samajhanā cāhiye | isa kāraṇa audārika ādi vargaṇāoṃ kā yahāṃ svarūpa kahate haiṃ|
ye audārika ādi vargaṇāyeṃ do prakāra kī hotī haiṃ-grahaṇayogya, agrahaṇayogya | agrahaṇavargaṇā ko ādi lekara karmavargaṇā taka vargaṇāoṃ kā svarūpa gāthā meṃ spaṣṭa kiyā jā rahā hai|
samāna jātīya pudgaloṃ ke samūha ko vargaṇā' kahate haiṃ | ye vargaṇārya
1 karmagrantha kī dīkā meṃ svajātīya skaṃdhoṃ ke samūha kā nāma vargaṇā kahā hai|
jabaki karmaprakṛti kī ṭokā meṃ skaṃdha aura vargaṇā ko ekārthaka kahā hai| kyoṃki skaṃdha - vargaṇā kī avagāhanā aṃgula ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga phahī hai | yadi jātīya skaṃdhoṃ ke samūha ko vargaṇā kahī jāye to usake loka
(śeṣa agale pṛṣṭha para)

26
śatakaṃ
anaṃta hotī hai| jaise samasaloka kāmo kucha kāpī paramāṇu pāye jāte haiṃ, unheṃ pahalī vargaṇā kahate haiṃ| do pradeśoṃ ke mela se banane vāle skaṃdhoṃ ko dūsarī vargaṇā, nīna pradeśoṃ ke mela se banane vāle skaṃdhoṃ kī tīsarī vargaṇā kahalātī hai| isī prakāra eka-eka paramāṇu baḍha़te. baḍha़te saṃkhyāta pradeśī skaṃdhoṃ ko saṃkhyātāṇu vargaṇā, asaṃkhyāta pradeśī skaṃdhoṃ ko asaṃkhyātāṇu vargaṇā, anaṃta pradeśo skaṃdhoṃ ko anantāṇu vargaṇā aura anaṃtānanta pradeśī sradhā kī anantānantāṇu vargaṇā samajhanā cāhiye|
ye vargaṇāyeṃ agrahaṇayogya aura grahaṇayogya, do prakāra kī hai | jo vargaṇāyeṃ alpa paramāṇu bālo hone ke kāraṇa jīva dvārā grahaṇa nahīṃ ko jātī, unheṃ agrahaṇavargaṇā kahate haiṃ | abhabya jīvoṃ ko rāśi se anaṃtaguṇe aura siddha jīvoṃ kī rāśi ke anantaveṃ bhāga pramāṇa paramāṇuoṃ se bane skaṃdha yānī itane paramāṇu vāle skaṃdha joba ke dvārā grahaṇa karane yogya hote haiṃ aura jīva unheṃ grahaṇa karake audārika śarīra rūpa pariṇamātā hai | isaliye unheṃ audārika vargaṇā kahate haiṃ | kintu audārika śarīra kī grahaṇayogya vargaṇāoṃ meṃ yaha vagaṃṇā sabase jaghanya hoto hai, usake ūpara eka-eka paramāṇu baḍha़te skaṃdhoṃ ko pahalī, dūsarī, tīsarī ādi ananta vargaṇāyeṃ audārika śarīra ke grahaṇa yogya hotī haiṃ | jisase audārika śarīra kī grahaṇayogya jaghanya vargaṇā se ananta|
dhyāpī hone se usakī avagāhanā lokapramāṇa hogī | vargaṇā aura skaṃdha ko jahā~ ekārthaka kahā gayā ho vahā~ to avagahanā maṃbadhī āpatti nahīṃ | kintu jahāṃ svajātīya skaṃdhoṃ ke samūha kā nāma vargaṇā kahā jāye vahāṃ avagāinā skandha kī lī jāye to barābara ekarūpalā vanatī hai | ataḥ karmagrantha kī ṭīkā ke anusāra rakaṃgha kī avamāhanā lenā cāhiye kintu vargaṇā ko nahīṃ|

266
bhāga adhika paramāṇu bālī audārika śarīra kī grahaṇayogya utkṛṣṭa vargaṇā hotī hai | isa anantaveṃ bhāga meṃ ananta paramāṇu hote haiṃ | ataḥ jaghanya vargaṇā se lekara utkṛṣṭa vargaṇā paryanta ananta vargaṇāyeṃ audārika śarīra kī grahaṇayogya jānanā cāhiye |
audārika śarīra kī utkṛṣṭa vargaṇā se ūpara eka-eka paramāṇu barate skandhoṃ se banane vālī vargaṇārye audārika kī apekṣā se adhika pradeśa vālī aura sūkṣma hotī haiṃ, jisase audārika ke grahaṇa-yogya nahīṃ hotī haiṃ aura jina skandhoṃ se vaikriya śarīra banatā hai. unakī apekṣā se alpa pradeśa bālī aura sthūla hotī haiṃ jisase ve vaikriya śarīra ke grahaṇayogya nahīṃ hotī haiṃ | isa prakāra audārika śarīra kī utkṛṣṭa vargaṇā ke ūpara eka-eka paramāṇu baḍha़te skaṃdhoṃ kī ananta agrahaṇayogya bargaṇā hotī haiṃ | jaise audārika śarīra kī grahaṇayogya jaghanya vargaṇā se usī kī utkṛṣṭa vargaṇā anaṃta bhāga adhika hai, vaise hī agrahaṇayogya jatranya vargaṇā se usako utkṛṣṭa vargaṇā anaṃtagṛṇī hai | isa guṇākāra kā pramāṇa abhatrya rāśi se anaṃtagaṇā aura siddharāśi kā anaṃtavāṃ bhāga hai|
isa agrahaṇayogya vargaṇā ke ūpara punaḥ grahaṇayogya vargaṇā ātī hai aura grahaṇayogya vargaṇā ke ūpara agrahaṇayogya vargaṇā ! isa prakāra ye donoṃ eka dūsare se antarita haiṃ|
isa prakāra se audārika śarīra kī prahaṇayogya aura agrahaṇayogya vargaṇāoṃ kā kathana karane ke bāda baiṃkriya ādi kī mahaṇayogya, agrahaṇayogya vargaṇāoṃ kā spaṣṭīkaraṇa karate haiṃ|
emeṣa vivāhārateyabhāsāṇupāṇamaṇakamme | suhamā kamāvagāho ūṇūṇaṃgulaasaṃkheso || 76 ||
śabdārtha-emeva-pūrvokta ke sarāna, viuvyāhārateyabhāsāṇapāṇamaṇahamme-kriya, āhāraka, tejasa, māṣā, svāsocchvāsa, mana

সারদ
pāyācaṃ - śarīrādi aṣṭaka, nīna veda, do yugala, solaha karāya, udyotaddhika, govatika, vedanīyadvika, pā~ca nidrā, vamavīśaka aura cāra āma ye parāvartamāna prakṛtiyā~ haiṃ| cāra ānupūrvī kṣetravipākī haiṃ| viśeṣārtha-gāthā meṃ parāvatamāna aura kṣetravipākī prakṛtiyoṃ kā kathana kiyā hai| ___parāvartamāna prakṛtiyāṃ dūmarī prakṛtiyoṃ ke baṃdha, udaya athavā baṃdhodaya donoṃ ko roka kara apanā baṃgha, udaya yā baṃdhodaya karane ke kāraṇa parāvartamāna kahalātī haiṃ| inameṃ aghātī- vedanīya, āyu, nāma, gona kamoṃ kī adhikāṃśa prakṛtiyoṃ ke sātha ghātī karma darśanāvaraṇa va mohanīya ko bhī prakṛtiyoṃ haiṃ | jinake nāma kramaśaḥ isa prakāra haiṃ -
(1) darśanāvaraṇa-nidrā, nidrā-nidrā, pracalā, pracalā-pracalā, snyānaddhi |
(2) varanoya-sātā vedanīya, asātā vedanīya |
(3) mohanīya-anantānubaṃdhī kapāya catuṣka, apratyākhyānāvaraṇa kapāya catuṣka, pratyākhyānāvaraṇa kapāya catuṣka, maṃgvalana kapāya catuṣka, hāsya, rati, śoka, arati, strī, puruṣa, napuṃsaka veda |
(4) āyakama-naraka, tithaṃca, manuṣya, deva ādhu |
(5) nāmakarma–śarīgaṣṭaka kī 33 prakṛtiyāṃ (audārika, vaikriya, āhāraka śarīra, audārika aṃgopāṃga ādi nīna aṃgopāṃga, chaha saṃsthāna, chaha mahanana, ekendriya ādi raca jāti, narakagani ādi cāra gani, śubha-aśubha bihāyogati, cāra ānupūrvī), ātapa, udyona, nama daśaka, sthāvara daśaka | '
(E) gotrakarma-ucna gotra, nīca gotra |

paṃcama karmagrantha
isa prakāra 5+2+23+4+5+2-1 prakṛtiyāṃ parāvartamāna haiṃ | inameṃ se anaṃtānubaṃdhī kapāya catuṣka ādi solaha kapāya aura pāṃca nidrāyeṃ dhruvabaṃdhinī hone se no baṃdhadaśā meṃ dumarī prakṛniyoṃ kā uparodha nahīṃ karatī haiṃ lekina udayakāla meṃ majānīya prakṛti ko roka kara pravṛtta hotī haiṃ, kyoṃki krodha, māna, māyā, lobha meṃ ma eka jīva ko eka mamaya meṃ eka pāya kā udaya honā hai| isī prakāra pāṃca nidrāoṃ meṃ kisī eka kā udaya hone para doṣa cāra nidrāoṃ kā udaya nahīṃ hotā hai. 1 anaḥ parāvarnamāna haiṃ|
sthira, śubha, agthira, aśubha ye cāra prakṛtiyāṃ udayadaśā meṃ virodhinī nahīṃ haiṃ kintu bananadaśā meṃ virodhinī haiṃ| kyoṃki sthira ke sātha asthira kā aura śubha ke sātha aśubha kā baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai| isalie ye cāroṃ prakṛtiyāṃ parāvatamāna haiṃ| śeṣa 66 prakṛtiyāṃ vaṃgha aura udaya donoṃ sthiniyoṃ meṃ parampara virodhinī hone meṃ parāvartamāna haiṃ ||
uma prakāra meṃ parāvarnamāna karma prakṛtiyoṃ kā varṇana karane ke mātha granthakāra dvārā nirdiṣṭa dhutravandhri ādi aparāvartamāna paryanta bāraha hāroṃ kā vivecana kiyā jā cukā hai| jinakā vivaraṇa pṛ072 para diye gaya koṣṭaka meṃ dekhiye |
aba karma prakṛtiyoṃ kā vipāka kī apekṣā nirūpaṇa karate haiṃ|
vipāka meṃ āśaya ramodaya kā hai| karmaprakṛti meṃ viśiṣṭa athavā vividha prakāra ke phāna dene kī śakti ko aura phala dene ke abhimukha hone ko vipāka kahate haiṃ | jaise āma ādi phana jaba paka kara taiyāra hone haiṃ, natra unakā vipāka hotā hai| baime hī kama prakṛtiyāṃ bhī jaba apanā phala dene ke abhimukha honī haiṃ natra unakā vipākākāla kahālānā hai|

śataka
22 māṇuoṃ kā saṃjāta hotā hai tyoṃ-tyoṃ unakā sūkṣma, sūkṣmatara rūpa pari māṇa hotā hai|
audārika ādi bargaṇāoṃ ko avagāhanā jo uttarottara hona-hona aṃgula ke asaṃkhyāta bhāga pāho hai vaha purva ko apekṣā krama se eka ke bāda dumarī uttaronara asaṃkhyātavāṃ bhāga hona samajhanā cāhiye | isa nyulatara kī vajaha se hī alpa paramāṇu vāle audārika śarīra ke dikhane para bhī usake mātra vidyamāna rahane vāle taijasa aura kāmaṇa śarīra usase kaī gane paramāṇu vāle hone para bhī dikhāī nahīṃ dete haiṃ|
tejasa bargaṇā ke bāda bhāṣā, śvāsośvāsa aura manovargaṇā kā ullekha karake manase aṃta meṃ kārmaṇa vargaṇā ko rakhā hai, isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki ta jamma vargaṇā se bhī bhāṣā ādi vargaṇāyeṃ adhika sukṣma haiṃ | athāt taijasa śarīra ko grahaṇayogya vargaṇāā se ve barmaṇāyeṃ adhika sūkṣma haiṃ jo bātacīta karate samaya śabda rūpa pariṇata hotī haiṃ, unase bhī ye vargaṇāyeṃ sūkṣma haiṃ jo śvāsocchvāsa rūpa pariṇata hotī hai | śvāsocchabāsa vargaṇā se bhī mānasika cintana kā ādhāra banane vālā manovargaṇāyeṃ aura adhika sūkṣma haiṃ| karmavargaṇā manovargaṇā se bhī sūkṣma haiṃ| isase yaha anumāna ho jāe ki ve kitanī adhika sūkṣma hai kintu unameṃ paramāṇuoṃ kī saṃkhyā kitanī adhika hotī hai| ___ audārika śarīra kī grahaṇayogya aura agrahaṇayogya vargaṇāoṃ kā vivavecana pūrva gāthā meṃ kiyā jā cukā hai | śeṣa rahī vaikriya ādi kī grahaṇayogya aura agrahaṇayogya vargaṇāoṃ ko yahāṃ spaṣṭa karate haiṃ| ___audārika śarīra kī agrahaṇayogya' utkṛṣṭa bargaṇā ke skaṃdhoṃ ke paramāṇuoṃ se eka adhika paramāṇu jina skaṃdhoṃ meṃ pāye jāte haiṃ una skaṃdhoṃ kī samūha rūpa vargaṇā vainiya śarīra ko grahaṇayogya jaghanya vargaṇā hotī hai | isa jaghanya vargaṇā ke skaṃdha ke pradeśoṃ se eka adhika pradeśa jisa-jisa skaṃdha meṃ pāyā jātā hai unakā samūha rūpa dūsarī

parama pāmagranya
vargaṇā vaikriya śarīra kī grahaṇayogya vargaṇā hotī hai| isī prakāra eka-eka pradeśa adhika skaṃdhoṃ kī ananta vargaṇāyeṃ vaikriya śarīra kī grahaṇayogya hotī haiṃ | baiṃkriya śarīra kī grahaṇayogya jaghanya vargaṇā se usake anantaveṃ bhāga adhika vaikriya śarīra ko grahaṇayogya utkṛṣṭa vargaṇā hotī hai|
vakriya śarīra ko grahaṇayogya utkṛṣṭa vargaṇā se eka pradeśa adhika skaṃdhoṃ ko jo vargaṇā hai vaha vaikriya śarīra kī apekṣā sa bahuta pradeśa bālī aura sūkṣma hotī hai tathā āhāraka śarīra kī apekṣā se kama pradeśa bālī aura nthūla hotī hai | ataḥ baṃkriya aura āhāraka śarīra ke lāyaka na hone se use agrahaṇavargaṇā kahate haiṃ| yaha jaghanya agrahaṇa vagaṇā hai | usake ūpara eka-eka pradeśa baḍa़te skandhoṃ kī ananta vargaṇāyeṃ agrahaṇayogya haiṃ|
agrahaṇayogya utkṛṣṭa vargaṇā se eka pradeśa adhika skandhoṃ kī jo vargaṇā hotī hai vaha āhāraka śarīra kī grahaṇayogya jaghanya bagaṇā hotī hai aura isa jaghanya vargaṇā se anantaveṃ bhāga adhika pradeśa vāle smāndhoṃ kī āhāraka śarīra ko grahaṇayogya utkṛṣṭa vargaṇā hotī hai| āhāraka śarīra kī isa grahaṇayogya utkṛṣṭa vargaṇā se eka pradeśa adhika skandhoṃ ko agrahaṇayogya jaghanya vargaṇā hotī hai aura usake ūpara 7ka-eka pradeśa baḍha़te-baḍha़te jaghanya vargaṇā se anantagṛṇe pradeśoṃ kī vṛddhi hone para agrahaṇayogya utkṛṣṭa vargaṇā hotī hai| ye vargaṇāyeṃ āhāraka śarīra kī apekṣā bahupradeśa vālī aura sūkṣma haiṃ aura taṃjasa śarīra ko apekṣā se alpa pradeśa vālī aura sthūla haiṃ, ataḥ grahaṇayogya nahīṃ haiṃ|
ukta utkṛṣṭa agrahaṇayogya vargaṇā se eka pradeśa adhika skandhoṃ kī vargaṇā tejasa śarīra ko grahaṇayogya jaghanya vargaṇā hotī hai|

usake ūpara eka-eka pradeśa baḍha़te baḍha़te anantaguṇe pradeśa vāle kaṃdhoṃ ko utkṛṣṭa agrahaṇayogya vargamā hotī hai | isa bagaṇā ke skaṃdhoṃ se eka pradeśa adhika skaṃdhoṃ kī manodaya ko grahaṇayogya jaghanya vargaṇā hoto hai | jaghanya vargaṇā ke ūpara eka-eka pradeśa baḍha़te-baḍha़te jaghanya vargaṇā ke skaṃdhoṃ ke pramo.he. anan gaganaśibāle kī nodravya kī grahaṇayogya utkṛṣṭa bargaṇā hotī hai| __manodravya kī grahaṇayogya utkṛṣṭa vargaṇā se eka pradeśa adhika skaṃdhoṃ kī agnāśayogya jaghanya vargaṇā hotī hai| usake ūpara eka-eka pradeśa baḍha़te-baḍa़te jaghanya vargaṇā ke skaṃdha pradeśoṃ se anantaguṇe pradeśa vāle skaṃdhoṃ kī agrahaṇayogya utkṛṣṭa vargaṇā hotī hai | isa utkṛṣṭa bargaṇā ke skandha ke pradeśoṃ se eka pradeśa adhika skandhoṃ kī vargaṇā karma ko grahaṇa yogya jaghanya vargaṇā hotī hai aura usake ūpara eka-eka pradeśa baḍha़tebaḍha़te jaghanya vargaṇā ke ananta bhāga adhika pradeśa bāle skandhoṃ kī karma kī yogya utkṛṣṭa vargaṇā hoto hai|

pacama karmagrantha
277
gāthārtha -audārika ādi vargaṇāoṃ ke madhya meṃ eka-eka paramāṇu dvārā adhika siddhoṃ ke anaṃta bhāga parimāṇa vālī agrahaṇayogya vargaṇā hotī haiṃ| audārika ādi mabhī varga: ṇāoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa apane-apane yogya jaghanya se anaṃtaveṃ bhāga adhika hotā hai| viśeṣārtha pūrva kī do gāthāoṃ meṃ graṇahayogya vargaṇāoṃ ke nāma aura unakī avagāhanā kā pramāṇa batalāyā hai aura yaha bhī kahā hai vimaraṇayogae nārīṇāamahāyomya bargaṇāoṃ meṃ antarita hotī haiṃ | isa gāthā meṃ agrahaṇayogya vargaṇāoṃ kā pramāṇa aura grahaṇayogya vargaṇāoṃ ke jaghanya aura utkṛṣṭa bhedoṃ kā annara batalāyā hai|
yadyapi pūrva meṃ grahaṇayogya vargaṇāoṃ kā vicāra karate samaya agrahaṇayogya vargaṇāoṃ ke pramāṇa kā bhī saṃketa kara āye haiṃ, tathāpi saṃkṣepa meṃ punaḥ yahāṃ spaṣṭa kara dete haiṃ ki utkṛṣṭa grahaṇayogya vargaṇā ke pratyeka skandha meṃ jitane paramāṇu hote haiṃ, unameṃ eka adhika paramāṇu vāle skandhoṃ ke samūha kī agrahaṇayogya jaghanya vargaṇā hotī hai | isake bāda do adhika paramāṇa vāle skandhoṃ ke samūha kī dūsarī agrahaṇayogya vargaṇā jānanā cāhie | isī prakāra tīna adhika, cāra adhika, ādi nosarī, cauthī ādi agrahaṇayogya vargaṇāyeṃ samajha nenā cāhie | ____ agrahaṇayogya jaghanya vargaṇā ke eka skandha meṃ jitane paramāṇu ho unako siddharāśi ke anantaveṃ bhāga se guṇā karane para jo pramāṇa ātā hai, utane paramāṇu vāle skandhoṃ ke samūha ko agrahaṇayogya utkṛṣṭa vargaṇā hotī hai | isīliye pratyeka agrahaṇayogya vargaṇā ko saṃkhyā siddha rāśi ke anantaveṃ bhāga batalāī hai| kyoṃki jaghanya agrahaṇa vargaṇā ke eka skandha meṃ jitane paramāṇu hote haiṃ ve siddharāśi ke anantaveṃ bhāga se guṇā karane para āte haiṃ| isīliye jaghanya se lekara utkṛṣṭa taka

śataka
vargaṇā ke utane hī vikalpa hote haiṃ yānī agrahaṇā vargaṇā ke jo ananta bheda hote haiṃ, ve bheda pratyeka agrahaṇa vargaṇā ke jānanā cāhiye | na ki kula agrahaṇa vargaṇāyeṃ simī ke anantanāḥ prama: | __agrahaṇa vargaṇāoṃ ke bāre meṃ dūsarī bāta yaha bhī jānanā cāhiye ki ye grahaṇa vargaṇāoṃ ke antarāla meṃ grahaṇa vargagā ke bāda agrahaṇa vargaṇā aura agrahaṇa vargaṇā ke bāda grahaṇa vargaṇā, isa krama se hotī haiṃ| aisā nahīṃ hai ki unameṃ se kucha vargaṇāyeṃ audārika vargaṇā se pahale hotī haiṃ aura kucha bāda meṃ | isī prakāra vaikriya ādi kī grahaṇayogya vargaṇāoṃ ke bāre meṃ samajhanā cāhiye |
agrahaṇa vargaṇāoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa apane-apane jaghanya se siddha rāśi ke anantabai bhāga guṇita hai aura grahaṇayogya bargaṇāoṃ kā utkṛṣṭa apaneapane jaghanya se anantaveṃ bhāga adhika hai| yānī jaghanya grahaṇayogya skandha se ananta bhāga adhika paramāṇu utkṛṣṭa grahaṇayogya skandha meṃ hote haiṃ|
isa prakāra se vargaṇāoṃ kā grāhya-agrāhya, utkṛṣṭa-jaghanya ādi sabhī prakāroṃ se vivecana kiye jāne ke paścāt aba āge kī gāthā meṃ jība jisa prakāra ke karmaskandha ko grahaṇa karatā hai, use batalāte haiṃ |
aṃtimaca uphāsabugaṃdhapaṃcavannarasammabaṃdhavalaṃ | samvajiyaṇaṃtaguṇarasamajuttamaṇatayapaesa ||7|| egapaesogāvaṃ niyasambapaesaja gaheḍa jiū |
śabdārtha antimaghauphāsa – anta meṃ cāra sparṇa, durgadha -- do gadha, paṃcakalarasa -- pāṃca varṇa aura pāṃca gma pāne, kammarṇadhavalaṃkarmaskandha balikoṃ ko. samvajiyaṇataguṇarasaṃ– maya jīvoṃ meṃ bhī ananta guṇe rama vāle. aṇuyukta-aṇuoṃ se yukta. amaṃtapapaesaṃananta pradeśa bāne, egapaesogāvaṃ-eka kṣetra meṃ abamāda rūpa me vidya

paṃcama karmagrantha
259
pāna, niyasavapaesau - apane samasta pradeśoṃ dvārā, gahecha - grahaṇa karatā hai, jiu-jīva |
gāthārtha – anta ke cāra sparśa, do gaṃdha, pāṃca varṇa aura pani rasa dāle mānīko hebhī ananta puṇe rasa vāle aṇuoṃ se yukta ananta pradeśa vāle aura eka kṣetra meṃ avagāḍa़ rūpa se vidyamāna karmaskandhoṃ ko jīva apane sarva pradeśoṃ dvārā grahaṇa karatā hai| viśeṣārtha - gāthā meṃ jīva dvārā grahaṇa kiye jāne vāle karmaskandhoṃ kā svarūpa vatalāte hue yaha spaṣṭa kiyā hai ki jīva krisa kṣetra meṃ rahane vāle karmaskandhoṃ ko grahaṇa karatā hai aura unake grahaṇa kī kyā prakriyā hai|
jīva dvārā jo karmaskandha grahaṇa kiye jāte haiṃ ve paudgalika hai arthāt pudgala paramāṇuoṃ kā samūhavigeṣa haiṃ| isīlie unameṃ bho pudagala ke guṇa - sparśa. rasa, gandha aura varṇa pāye jāte haiṃ | arthāt jaise pūdgala rūpa, rasa, gaṃdha, sparśa vālā hai vaise hī karmaskandha bhī rūpa ādi vāle hone se pudagala jātīya haiṃ|
eka paramāṇu meṃ pāṃca prakāra ke rasoṃ meṃ se koī eka rasa, pāṃca prakāra ke rūpoṃ meṃ sa koī eka rūpa, do prakāra kī gaṃdhoṃ meṃ se koī eka gaṃdha aura āṭha prakāra ke spo . guru-laghu, komala-kaṭhora, śīta-uṣṇa, snigdha aura rūkṣa meṃ se do aviruddha sparśa hote haiṃ|'

aura gotra karma se adhika hissā milatā hai | kyoṃki nāma aura gotra karma kī sthiti to bīsa-bīsa koḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgara hai jabaki antarāya ādi tīna karmoṃ meṃ se pratyeka kī sthiti tīsa-tīsa koḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgara hai | lekina ina tīnoṃ karmoṃ kī sthiti samāna hone se unakā bhāga āpasa meṃ barābara-barābara hai | ina tīnoṃ karmoṃ se mohanīya karma kā bhāga adhika hai, kyoṃki usakī sthiti sattara koḍa़ākoḍa़ī sāgara kī haiṃ |
.
isa prakāra vedanīya karma ke sivāya śeṣa sāta karmoṃ ko unakī sthiti ke anusāra kramaśaḥ adhika pudgalaskandhoṃ ke prāpta hone ko batalāyā | aba vedanīya karma ko adhika dravya milane ke kāraṇa ko spaṣṭa karate haiṃ manvovara veyaṇīya | kyoṃki bahuta dravya ke binā vedanīya karma ke sukha-duḥkha ādi kā anubhava spaṣṭa nahīṃ hotā hai | alpa dravya milane para bedanīya karma apane sukha-duḥkha kā vedana karāne rūpa kārya karane meṃ samartha nahīṃ hotā hai— jeṇappe tassa phuḍatta na havaī | kintu adhika dravya milane para hī vaha apanā kārya karane meṃ samartha hai|' bedanīya karma ko adhika dravya milane kā kāraṇa yaha hai ki sukha-duḥkha ke nimitta se vedanīya karma kī nirjarā adhika hotī hai | arthāt pratyeka jīva pratisamaya sukha-duḥkha kā vedana karatā hai, jisase vedanīya karma kā udaya pratikṣaṇa hone se usakī nirjarā bhī adhika hotī hai | isī
1 kramamo buḍḍhaṭhiṇaṃ bhāgo daligassa hoḍa sarviso | taiyamya maccajaṭṭo lasma phuḍataṃ
||

isa prakāra se mūla prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ karmaskandhoṃ ke vibhāga ko batalā. kara aba āge kī gāthā meṃ uttara prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ usakā krama batalāte haiṃ |
niyajāiladdhavaliyāgaṃtaso hoi samvaghāīṇa | bajmatīṇa vimajjaī sesaṃ sesāṇa paisamayaṃ ||1||
śabdārtha --niyajāilabadaliya --apanī mūla prakṛti rūpa jāti dvārā prāpta kiye gaye kama dalikoṃ kā, aśaṃsaṃso-anantavā bhāga, hoī—honā hai, savvaghāī-sarvaghātī prakṛtiyo kā, bajjhatīṇabaṃdhane vālā, vimāna-vibhājita hotā hai. sesa śeṣa bhāga, sesāṇa- bākī kī prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ, paisamayaṃ-pratyeka samaya meṃ |
__ gāthārya--apanī-apanī mūla prakṛti dvārā prāpta kiye gaye karmadalikoṃ kā anantavāṃ bhāga sarvaghāti prakṛtiyoṃ ko prāpta hotā hai aura śeṣa banā huā hissā pratisamaya baṃdhane vālī prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ vibhājita ho jātā hai| viśeṣārtha-gāthā meṃ yaha batāyā gayā hai ki mūla karmaprakṛtiyoṃ ko prāpta hone vālā pudgala dravya hī una-una karmoṃ kī uttara prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ vibhājita hokara unheṃ prāpta hotā hai| kyoṃki uttara prakṛtiyoṃ ke

śataka
aura dūsarā bhāga cāritramohanīya ko | darśanamohanīya ko prāpta pūrā bhāga usakī uttara prakṛti mithyātva ko ho milatā hai, kyoṃki vaha sarvaghātinī hai| kintu cāritramohanīya ke prāpta bhāga ke bāraha bheda hokara anantānubaṃdhī kaṣāya catuṣka, apratyākhyānādharaṇa kaṣāya catuṣka aura pratyākhyānāvaraṇa kaṣāya catupka, ina bāraha bhāgoṃ meṃ baṃṭa jātā hai | mohanīya karma ke deśaghātī dravya ke do bhāga hote haiṃ| unameṃ se eka bhāga kaṣāyamohanīya kā aura dūsarā nokaṣāya mohanīya kā hotā hai | kaṣāyamohanīya ke dravya ke cāra bhāga hokara saṃcalana krodha, māna, māyā aura lobha ko mila jāte haiṃ aura nokaṣāya mohanīya ke pāṃca bhāga hokara kramaśaḥ tīna vedoṃ meṃ se kisī eka badhyamāna veda ko, hāsya aura rati ke yugala tathā śoka aura arati ke yugala meṃ se kisī eka yugala ko (yugala meṃ se pratyeka ko eka bhāga) tathā bhaya aura jugupsā ko milate haiṃ|'
1 (ka) ukkosa rasassada mincha addha tu iyaraghāīgaṃ | saṃjalaṇa nokasāyā sesaṃ adaddhayaṃ leti ||

antarāya karma ko prāpta bhāga pāṃca vibhāgoṃ meṃ vibhājita hokara usakī dāna-antarāya ādi pāṃcoṃ uttara prakṛtiyoṃ ko milatā hai| kyoṃki antarāya karma deśaghātī hai aura dhruvabaṃdhī hone ke kāraṇa dānāntarāya ādi pāṃcoṃ prakṛtiyāṃ sadā baṃdhatī haiṃ|
dhātikarmoṃ kī uttara prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ prāpta dravya ke vibhājana ko batalāne ke paścāta aba vedanīya, āyu, nāma aura gotra karmoṃ ko prāpta bhāga ke vibhāga ko spaṣṭa karate haiṃ|

nāmakarma ko jo mūla bhāga milatā hai vaha usakī baṃdhane vālī uttara prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ vibhājita ho jātā hai | arthāt gati, jāti, śarīra, upāṃga, baṃdhana, saṃghāta, saṃhanana, saṃsthāna ānupūrvī, varṇacatuṣka, agurulaghu, parāghāta, udyota, upaghāta, ucchvāsa, nirmāṇa, tīrthakara, ātapa, vihāyogati aura sadaśaka athavā sthāvaradaśaka meṃ se jitanī prakṛ
mohanīya ko aura ādhā bhāga nokapāya mohanīya ko milatā hai| kaṣāyamohanīya ko milane vāle bhāga ke punaḥ cāra bhāga hote hai aura ve cāroṃ bhāga saṃjvalana krodha, māna, māyā aura lobha ko diye jāte haiṃ | nokaṣāya mohanīya ke pāṃca bhāga hote haiṃ | jo tīna vedoṃ meṃ se kisī eka veda ko, hāsya-rani aura śoka-arati ke yugaloṃ meṃ se kisī eka yugala ko, praya aura jugupsā ko diye jāte haiṃ| kyoṃki eka samaya meṃ pāṃcoṃ hī nokaṣāya kāṃ badha hotā hai|

215
isake sivāya nāmakarma kī anya prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ koī avāntara vibhāga nahīṃ hone se jo bhāga milatā hai vaha pūrā baṃdhane vālī usa eka prakṛti ko hī mila jātā hai| kyoṃki anya prakṛtiyāṃ āpasa meṃ virodhinī haiṃ ataḥ eka kā baṃdha hone para dūsarī kā baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai | jaise ki eka gati kā baṃdha hone para dūsarī gati kā baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai | isī taraha jāti, saṃsthāna aura saṃha kī eka meṃ eka sī aura sadaśaka kā baṃgha hone para sthāvaradaśaka kā baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai|

1
1 vedanīya, āyu, gotra aura nāma pharma ke dravya kā baṭavārā unakī utara prakṛtiyoṃ meṃ karane kā krama karmaprakṛti meṃ isa prakāra batalāyā haiveṇimagotsu bajjhamāṇīṇa bhāgo si || pipagatīsu bajjhatigāṇa vanarasagaṃdhaphāsāṇaṃ | samvāsi saṃghāta taśummi ya tige caukke vā ||

vedanīya, āyu aura gotra karma ko jo mūla bhāga milatā hai, vaha unakī baṃdhane vālī eka-eka prakṛti ko hī mila jātā hai, kyoṃki ina kama kī eka samaya meṃ eka hī prakṛti baṃdhatī hai| nāmakarma ko jo bhāga milatā hai, vaha usakī banate bālo prakṛtiyoṃ kā hotā hai| varṇa, gaṃdha, rasa aura spardhā ko jo bhāga milatā hai, vaha unako saba avāntara prakṛtiyoṃ ko milatā hai | saṃghāsa aura śarīra ko jo bhāga milatā hai, vaha tīna mā cāra bānoṃ meṃ baṃṭa jātā hai|

195
śataka prakṛti kā bhī baṃdhaviccheda ho jāye to unake hisse kā dravya unakī sūla prakṛti ke antargata vijātīya prakṛtiyoṃ ko milatā hai | yadi una vijātīya prakṛtiyoṃ kā bhī baṃdha ruka jātā hai to usa mūla prakṛti ko dravya na milakara anya mūla prakṛtiyoṃ ko dravya mila jātā hai | jaise ki styānaddhitrika kā baṃdhaviccheda hone para unake hisse kā dravya unakī sajātīya prakṛti nidrā aura pratralā ko milatā hai aura nidrā va pracalā kā bhī baṃdhaviccheda hone para unakā draśya apanī hī mūla prakṛti ke antargata cakṣudarśanāvaraṇa ādi vijātīya prakṛtiyoṃ ko milatā hai| unakā bhī baṃdhaviccheda hone para gyārahaveṃ ādi gṛṇasthānoṃ meṃ saba dravya sātāvedanīya ko hī milatā hai | isī prakāra anya prakṛtiyoṃ ke bāre meṃ bhī samajhanā cāhie | sārāṃśa yaha hai ki kisī prakṛti kā baṃdha. viccheda hone para usakā bhāga samāna jātīya prakṛti ko mila jātā hai aura usa samāna jātīya prakṛti kā bhī baṃdhaviccheda hone para mūla prakṛti ke antargata sanakī vijātīya prakṛtiyoṃ kā milatā hai| yadi usa mūla prakṛti kā hī viccheda ho jāye to vidyamāna anya mūla prakṛtiyoṃ ko yaha dravya prāpta hone lagatā hai|

haiṃ, unameṃ se thoḍa़e dalika udaya samaya meṃ dākhila kara diye jāte hai, usase asaṃkhyātaguṇe dalika udaya samaya se ūpara ke dvitīya samaya meṃ dākhila kara diye jāte haiṃ, usase asaṃkhyātaguṇe dalika tīsare samaya meṃ, usase asaṃkhyātaguṇe dalika kramaśaḥ cauthe, pāṃcaveṃ ādi samayoṃ meṃ dākhila kara diye jāte haiṃ | isī krama se antarmuhūrta kāla ke aṃtima samaya taka asaṃkhyātaguṇe, asaṃkhyātaguṇe dalikoṃ kī sthāpanā kī jātī hai| yaha to huī prathama samaya meṃ gṛhīta dalikoṃ ke sthāpana karane kī vidhi | isī prakāra śeṣa dūsare, tīsare, cauthe ādi samayoṃ meṃ gṛhīta dalikoṃ ke nikṣepaṇa kī vidhi jānanā cāhiye | yaha kriyā antarmuhūrta kāla ke samayoṃ taka hī hotī rahatī hai|
śataka
sārāṃśa yaha hai ki guṇaśra eṇi kā kāla antarmuhūrta hai, ataḥ antarmuhūrta taka ūpara kī sthiti meṃ se karmadalikoṃ kā prati samaya grahaṇa kiyā jātā hai aura prati samaya jo karmadalika grahaṇa kiye jāte hai, unakā sthāpana asaṃkhyāta guṇita krama se udaya kṣaṇa se lekara antamuhūrta kāla ke antima samaya taka meṃ kara diyā jātā hai| jaise kalpanā se antarmuhūrta kā pramāṇa 16 samaya māna liyā jāye to guṇaśreṇi ke prathama samaya meṃ jo karmadalika grahaṇa kiye gaye unakā sthāpana pūrvokta prakāra se 16 samayoṃ meṃ kiyā jāyegā| dūsare samaya meṃ jo karmadalika grahaṇa kiye gaye, unakā sthāpana bākī ke 15 samayoṃ meṃ hī hogā, kyoṃki pahale kṣaṇa kā vedana ho cukā hai| tīsare samaya meṃ jo karmadalika grahaṇa kiye gaye unakā sthāpana śeṣa caudaha samayoṃ meṃ hī hogā | isī prakāra se cauthe, pā~caveṃ ādi samayoṃ ke krama ke bāre meṃ samajhanā cāhiye, kintu aisā nahīṃ samajhanā cāhiye ki pratyeka samaya meṃ grahīta dalikoṃ kā sthāpana solaha hī samayoṃ meṃ hotā hai aura isa taraha guṇaśra eṇi kā kāla ūpara kī ora baḍha़tā jātā hai | isa prakāra antarmuhūrta

samyaktva kī prāpti ke samaya jīva isa prakāra kī guṇaṇi racanā karatā hai | guṇaṇi udaya samaya se hotī hai aura ūpara-ūpara asaṃkhyāta guṇaṃ dalikasthāpita kiye jāte haiṃ | ataḥ guṇaṇi karane vālā jīva jyoṃ-jyoṃ ūpara kī ora caḍha़tā hai tyoṃ-tyoṃ prati samaya asaṃkhyātaguṇī, asaṃkhyātaguṇī nijarā karatā jātā hai | isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki jisa kāma se dalika sthāpita hote haiṃ usī krama se ve pratisamaya udaya meṃ āte haiṃ, ve asaṃkhyāta guṇita krama se sthāpita kiye jāte haiṃ aura usī krama se udaya meṃ āte haiṃ, jisase samyaktva meṃ asaṃkhyātaguṇo nirjarā hotī hai | ___ samyaktva kī prāpti ke bāda deśapirati aura sarvavirati kī prāpti ke liye jīva yathāpravṛtta aura apūrvakaraṇa hī karatā hai, tīsarā ani vṛttikaraṇa nahīṃ karatā aura apūrvakaraṇa meṃ yahāṃ guṇaśreṇi racanā bhī nahīṃ hotī hai aura apūrvakaraṇa kā kāla samāpta hone para niścita hī deśavirati yā sarvavirati kī prāpti ho jātī hai | jisase anivṛttikaraṇa kī āvaśyakatā nahīṃ rahatī hai|

śabdārtha--paliyāsaṃkhasamūha-palya kā asaṃkhyātavāṃ bhāga aura antarmuhūrta, sāsaṇaiyaraguṇa–sāsādana aura dūsare guṇasthānoṃ kā, aṃtaraṃ antara. hasaṃ -jadhanya, guru -utkṛṣṭa, minchīmithyātva meṃ, chasaṭṭhī-do chiyāsaṭha sāgaropama, iparaguṇe-dūsare guṇasthānoṃ meṃ, puggalabaṃto - kucha nyūna ardhapudgala parāvarta ||

śataka
guṇasthāna kahā jātā hai ādi| isī prakāra āge bhī samajhanā cāhiye | ataḥ ukta guṇaśra ṇiyoṃ kā saṃbaṃdha guṇasthānoṃ ke sātha hone ke kāraṇa gāthā meṃ guṇasthānoṃ kā jaghanya aura utkṛṣṭa antarāla batalāyā hai | koī jīva kisī guṇasthāna se cyuta hokara punaḥ jitane samaya ke bāda usa guṇasthāna ko prāpta karatā hai, vaha samaya usa guṇasthāna kā antarakāla kahalātā hai |
sarvaprathama guṇasthānoṃ kā jaghanya antarāla batalāte hue kahā haipaliyāsaṃkhasamuha sāsaṇaiyaraguṇa aṃtaraṃ hassaṃ sāsādanaṃ nāmaka dūsare suṇasthāna kā jaghanya antarakāla palya ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga aura śeṣa guṇasthānoṃ kā antara antamuhūrta haiṃ | jisako yahāṃ spaṣṭa karate haiṃ |

1 yathāpravṛtta ādi tīna karaṇoṃ ke binā hī kisī prakṛti ko anya prakṛti rūpa pariṇamāne ko uvalana kahate haiṃ|

paṃcama karmagrantha ubalanā karatā hai yānī donoṃ prakṛtiyoṃ ke dalikoṃ ko mithyātva mohanīya rūpa pariṇamātā rahatā hai |
isa prakāra udghalana karate-karate palya ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga kāla meṃ ukta donoṃ prakṛtiyoṃ kā abhāva ho jātā hai aura abhāva hone para vahī jīva punaḥ aupaśamiyā sampayatva ko prāśa kara samāpana guṇasthāna meṃ ā jātā hai| isīlie sāsādana guṇasthāna kā aṃtarāla kāla palya ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga mānā gayā hai | ___ sāsādana guṇasthāna kā jaghanya antara patya ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāma pramāṇa batalāne kā kāraṇa yaha hai ki koī jīva upaśama śreṇi se girakara sāsādana guṇasthāna meṃ āte haiṃ aura antamuhūrta ke bāda punaḥ upaśama śreṇi para caḍha़kara aura vahāṃ se girakara punaḥ sāsādana guṇasthāna meṃ āte haiṃ | isa dṛṣṭi se to sāsādana kā jaghanya aṃtara bahuta thoḍa़ā rahatā hai, kintu upaśama śreṇi se cyuta hokara jo sāsādana samyaktva kī prāpti hotī hai, vaha kevala manuṣyagati meṃ hī saṃbhava hai aura vahāṃ para bhī isa prakāra kī ghaṭanā bahuta kama hotī hai, jisase yahāṃ usakī vivakṣā nahīṃ kī hai kintu upaśama samyaktva se cyuta hokara jo sāsādana kī prāpti batalāī hai, vaha cāroṃ matiyoṃ meṃ saṃbhava hai | ataḥ usakī apekṣā se hī sāsādana kā jaghanya antara palya ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga batalāyā hai 1 yānī śroṇi kī apekṣā nahīṃ kintu upaśama samyaktva se cyuta hone kī apekṣā se sāsādana guṇasthāna kā jaghanya antara palya ke amakhyātaveṃ bhāga batalāyā hai |
sāsādana ke sivāya bākī ke guṇasthānoṃ meṃ se kṣīṇamoha, sayogikevalī aura ayogikevalī, ina tīna guṇasthānoṃ kā to aṃtara kāla nahīṃ hotā, kyoṃki ye guṇasthāna eka bāra prāpta hokara punaḥ prāpta nahīṃ hote haiṃ | śeṣa rahe guṇasthānoṃ meṃ se mithyādṛṣṭi, miśradṛṣṭi, avirata sabhyag

mātaka dṛṣṭi deśavirati, pramatta apramatta tathā upaśama śreṇi ke apūrvakaraṇa, anivṛttikaraṇa, sūkṣmasaṃparāya tathā upaśāntamoha guṇasthāna se cyuta hokara jīva antāna ke bāda hī puna: una maraṇādoṃ ko pāna kara letā hai | ataḥ unakā jaghanya antarakāla eka antamuhurta hī hotā hai | kyoṃki jaba koī jīva upaśama śreṇi para caḍha़kara gyārahaveṃ guṇasthāna taka pahu~catā hai aura vahāṃ se girakara kramaśaḥ utarate-utarate pahale mithyātva' guṇasthāna meṃ ā jātā hai aura usake bāda punaḥ eka antamu hūrta meṃ gyārahaveṃ guṇasthāna taka jā pahu~catā hai | kyoṃki eka bhava meṃ do bāra upaśama śrīṇi para caḍha़ne kā vidhāna hai |' usa samaya miśra guṇasthāna ke sivāya bākī ke guṇasthānoṃ meṃ se pratyeka kā jaghanya antarakāla antamuhūrta hotā hai|
