paṃcama krarmagranya
413
baṃdha karake teraha aura nau kā baṃdha kara sakane ke kāraṇa satraha ke baṃdhasthāna ke ho alpaptara baṃdha hote haiṃ | isa prakāra dāīsa ke tīna aura satraha ke do alpatara baṃdhoṃ meṃ se karmananya meṃ kevala ekā-eka hī alpatara baṃdha batalāyā hai | ataḥ śeṣa tīna rahate haiṃ jo karmagrantha se karmakāṃ meṃ adhika hai|
bhūyaskāra, alpatara aura avaktavya baṃdha ke dvitīya samaya meṃ bhī yadi utanī hī prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha holā hai jitanī prakṛtiyoṃ kā baṃdha pahale samaya meṃ huā thā to use avasthita baṃdha kahate haiṃ | ataḥ karmakāṃḍa meṃ myaskāra, alpatara aura ava
savya baṃdhoṃ kī saṃkhyā ke barābara hī avasthita baṃdhoṃ kī saṃkhyā batalāī hai | yadi dusare samaya meṃ hone vāle badha ke ūpara se bhūyaskāra, alpatara athavā avaktavya padoṃ ko alaga karake unakī vāstavika sthiti ko dekheṃ to mūla avasthita baṃdha utane hī ṭhahara sakate haiṃ jitane baṃdhasthāna hote haiṃ | jaise kisī jīva ne ikkīsa kā badha karake prathama samaya meṃ bāīsa kā bandha kiyā aura dūsare samaya meṃ bhī bāīsa kā hī badha kiyā to yahaḥ 5 samaya kā baṃdha bhūskāra badha hai aura dūsare samaya kā avasthita | jisa prakāra bhūyaskāra ādi baṃdhoṃ kā nirūpaṇa hai, yadi usī prakāra anna sthita baṃdha kā nirūpaṇa kiyā jāye to dāīsa kā baṃdha karake bāīsa kā baṃdha karanā, ikkīsa kā baṃdha karake ikkīsa kā baṃdha karanā, satraha kā baṃdha karake satraha kā baṃdha karanā, ādi avasthita baṃdha hī hai| isakā sārāṃśa yaha hai ki mūla avasthita baṃdha utane hī hote haiṃ jitane ki baṃdhasthāna hote haiṃ, isīliye karmagrantha meṃ dasa hī avasthita baṃdha mohanīya karma ke batalāye haiṃ| kiṃtu bhayaskāra, alpatara aura avaktavya baṃdha ke dvitīya samaya meṃ prāyaḥ avasthita bandha hotā hai, ataḥ ina upapada pūrvaka hone vāle avasthita baṃdha bhī utane hī hote hai jitane ki tīnoṃ baṃdhoṃ ke hote haiṃ | isī se karmakāṃḍa meṃ ukta tīnoṃ prakāra ke baṃdhoṃ ke barābara hī avasthita baṃdha kā parimāṇa batalāyā hai| avaktavya baṃna karmagranya aura go karmakāṃḍa meṃ samāna haiṃ |
go0 karmakāṃḍa meṃ viśeṣarūpa se bhī bhūyaskāra ādi ko gināyā hai, jinakī saṃkhyā nimna prakāra hai

-2 alpatara baṃdha māne gaye haiṃ | sātaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ eka bhī allatara baṃdha nahīṃ hotā hai, kyoṃki jaba jīva sātaveṃ se āṭhaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ jātā hai to vahāṃ bhī nau prakṛtiyoṃ kā hī baṃdha karatā hai, kama kā nahīṃ karatā hai| āṭhaveṃ meṃ no kā baṃdha karate nauveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ pāṃca kā baṃdha karane para 1x1=1 hī malpatara baṃdha hotā hai| nauveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ pāṃca kā baṃdha karake cāra kā baṃdha karane para eka, cāra kā baṃdha karake tīna kā baṃdha karane para eka, tīna kā baṃdha karake do kā baṃdha karane para eka aura do kā baṃdha karake eka kā baṃdha karane para eka, isa prakāra cāra alpatara baṃdha hote haiṃ | isa prakāra paitālīsa alpatara baṃdha samajhanā cāhie | avaktavya baṃdha isa prakāra hai--

paṃcama karmagrantha
maga kī vivakṣā ke viśeṣa se avaktabya baṃdha sūkṣma saṃparāya guṇasthāna se utarane meṃ eka hotā hai | arthāt dasaveṃ guṇasthāna me utara kara jaba nau guṇasthāna meṃ eka prakṛti kā baṃdha karatā hai taba eka avaktavya hotā hai aura dasaveṃ meṃ maraṇa karake devagati meṃ janma lekara jaba sabaha kā baṃdha karatā hai taba do avaktavya baṃdha hote haiṃ| isa prakāra tīna avaktavya baṃgha mānanā cāhie | arthāt dasaveṃ se uttara ke jaba naune meṃ ānā hai taba saṃjvalana lobha kā baṃdha karatā hai, ataḥ eka avaktavya baṃdha hā tapā usī dasaveṃ meṃ maraṇa kara deva asaṃyata huā taba do avaktavya baṃdha hote haiṃ, kyoṃki deva hokara 17 prakṛtiyoṃ ko do prakāra meṃ bāṃdhatā hai| isa taraha tīna avaktavya baṃdha hue|

isa gāthā meṃ jisa pratibhāga kā ullekha kiyā hai, usako gāthā 145 meṃ spaṣṭa kiyā hai | ekendriyādika jīvoṃ kī apekṣā se ukta prakṛtiyoṃ kī jaghanya
aura utkṛṣṭa sthiti batalāne ke lie apanī apanī pūrvokta utkṛṣṭa sthiti meṃ mithyātva kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti kā bhāga dane se prāpta landha ekendriya kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti hai aura usameṃ palya kā asaṃkhyātavāṃ bhāga nna karane se jaghanya sthiti hotī hai | ata: jaghanya sthiti baṃdha ko ekendriya jīva ke karane se śeṣa prakṛtiyoṃ kā jaghanya sthitibaṃdha karmakāṃḍa meṃ alaga se nahīṃ batalāyā hai|

18
pariśiṣṭa-2 karmaprakṛti meṃ śeṣa prakṛtiyoṃ kī jaghanya sthiti batalāne ke lie varga banā kara mithyātva kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti kā bhāga dene kā pahale saṃketa kiyā gayā hai
aura ekendriya jīva kī apekṣā se prakṛtiyoṃ kī sthiti kā parimāṇa batalāte hue āge likhā hai
esegidiyahare samvāsi ūṇasaṃjuo jaṭṭho | arthāta apane-a . utkṛṣṭa gita . .... ati ! kā bhāga dekara labdha meṃ se palya ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga ko kama karane se jo apanī-apanī jaghanya sthiti ātī hai, vahīṃ ekendriya yogya jaghanya sthiti kā pramāṇa jānanā cāhie | kama kiye gaye palya ke masaṃkhyāta bhāga ko usa jaghanya | spipti meṃ joḍa़ne para utkṛṣṭa sthiti kā pramāṇa hotā hai|
karmagrantha meṃ pacāsī prakṛtiyoṃ kī jaghanya sthiti kā vivecana paṃcarsagraha aura karma prakṛti donoṃ ke amināyānusāra kiyā hai| ina donoṃ vivecana meṃ yaha aṃtara hai ki paṃcasaṃgraha meṃ to apanī-apanī prakṛti kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti meṃ mithyāśva kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti kā bhāga dekara jaghanya sthiti batalāī hai aura karma prakṛti meṃ apane-apane gaṃ kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti meṃ mithyātva kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti kā bhāga dekara aura usake labdha meṃ se palpa kā asaṃkhyātayāṃ bhāga kama karake jaghanya spiti vatalāī hai|
go0 karmakāṃḍa prakṛtiyoṃ kī sthiti meṃ bhāga dene taka to paṃcasaṃgraha ke mata se sahamata hai lekina āge vaha karmaprakṛti ke mata se sahamata ho jātā hai| paṃcasaṃgraha kā mata hai ki prakṛtiyoṃ kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti meṃ bhāga dene para jo landha mātā hai, vaha to ekendriya kī apekṣā se jaghanya sthiti hotī hai aura usa meṃ palya kā asaṃkhyātavāṃ bhāga joḍa़ne se usakī utkṛṣṭa sthiti ho jātī hai| lekina jī. karmakāṃḍa aura karmaprakṛti ke matānusāra midhvātva kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti kā bhāga dene para jo labdha pātā hai vahī utkṛṣṭa sthiti hotī hai aura usameṃ palya kā asaṃkhyātavāṃ bhāga kāma dene para jaghanya sthiti hotī hai | paṃca- : saṃgraha meṃ to apane-apane varga kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti meṃ bhāga nahīṃ diyā jātā hai phintu apanī-apanī utkṛṣṭa sthiti meṃ mithyātva kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti se bhāga dene para prāpta samdha jaghanya sthiti kā parimāṇa hai|

paṃcama pharmagrandha
___ 416 isa prakāra se paṃcasagraha aura karma prakṛti ke mata meṃ aṃtara hai| āyukarma ke abādhākāla kā spaṣṭīkaraṇa
deva, nāraka, tiryaca, manuṣya māyu kī utkṛṣṭa sthiti batalāte samaya abādhāsāla pūrva koṭi kā tīsarā bhāga batalāyā hai | isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki pūrva phoṭi varṣa kī āyu vāle manuṣya aura tiyaṃ ca yathāyogya rīti se apanī āyu ke do bhāga bītane ke paścāt tīsare bhāga ke prārambha meṃ deva, nāraka kā setosa sāgaropama pramāṇa utkṛṣṭa āyu bāṃdha sakate haiṃ. isalie utkṛṣṭa sthiti ke sātha abādhā rūpa kāsa pūrva koṭi kā tīsarā bhāga lene kā saṃketa kivā hai | jaise anya sabhī karmoṃ ke sātha abādhākāla joḍakara sthiti kahī hai vaise āyukāma kī sthiti abādhākāla joḍa़kara nahīṃ batāī hai | kyoṃki usakā avādhākāla niścita nahīṃ hai | asaṃkhyāta varṣa kī āyu vāle manuṣya aura tiryaṃca evaṃ deva tathā māraka apano āyu ke chaha māha śeṣa rahane para paramava kī āyu bāṃdhate haiṃ aura śeṣa saṃkhyāta varṣa kī nirupakramī āyu vāle apanī āyu ke do bhāga bītane ke paścāta tīsare bhāga kī śuruāta meṃ paramana ko āyu kā baṃdha karate haiṃ aura sopakramī āyu vāle kula āyu ke do bhāga jāne ke paścāta tīsare bhāga ke prārambha meṃ bāṃdhate haiṃ | yadi usa samaya āyu kā badha na kareṃ to jitanī āyu śeṣa ho usake tīsare māga kī śurūāta meṃ bāṃdhate haiṃ | isakā āśaya yaha hai ki saṃpūrṇa āyu ke tīsare bhāga, nauveṃ bhāga, mattāīsaveṃ bhāga, isa prakāra jaba taka aṃtima antarmuhūrta āyu śeṣa ho taba paramara kī āyu kā baṃdha karate haiṃ| parabhava kī āyu kā baṃdha karane ke bāda jitanī āyu meṣa ho, vaha abādhākāla hai tathā abādhā jaghanya ho aura āyu kā baṃdha meṃ: jaghanya ho jaise anta muṃhataṃ kī āyu vālā antarmuhūrta prabhāga āyu bāṃdhe | abādhā jaghanya ho aura āyu kā baṃdha utkṛṣṭa ho jaise anna muhūrta kī āyu vālā tetīsa sāgara pramāṇa ta dula matsya kī taraha nāraka kā āyu bāṃdhe | utkṛṣṭa abādhā ho aura āyu kā jaghanya badha hā aṃsa pūrva koṭi varṣa kī māyu vālā apanī āyu ke tīsare bhāga ke prārambha meṃ antarmuhūrna āyu kā baṃdha kare tathā uskṛṣṭa avādhā ho aura āśru kā baṃdha bhī utkṛṣṭa ho jaise pūrva koṭi varṣa vālā tīsare bhāga kī śuruāta meṃ se tīsa sāgaropama pramāṇa deva,

paṃcama karmantha
paryāpti ke pūrṇa hone ke samaya se lekara āyu ke anta taka hone vāle yoga ko pariṇāma yogasthāna kahate haiṃ | pariṇāma yogasthāna utkṛṣṭa bhī hote haiṃ aura jaghanya bhī | labdhyaparyāptaka ke bhī apanī sthiti ke saba bhedoṃ meṃ donoṃ pariṇāma yogaśthāna sambhava haiṃ| so ye saba pariṇāma yogasthāna poṭamāna yoga samajhanā | kyoṃki ye ghaṭate bhī hai, baḍha़te bhī hai aura jaise ke taise bhī rahate haiṃ |
42 1
I
upapāda yogasthāna aura ekāntānuvṛddhi yogasthānoṃ ke pravartana kā kāla jaghanya mīra utkṛṣṭa eka samaya ho hai | kyoṃki upapādasthāna janma ke prathama samaya meṃ hī hotā hai aura ekāṃtānuvṛddhi sthāna bhī samaya-samaya pratibuddhi rūpa judā-judā hī hotā hai aura ina donoṃ se bhinna jo pariṇāma yogasthāna haiṃ. unake nirantara pravartane kā kāla do samaya se lekara āṭha samaya taka hai | āṭha samaya nirantara pravartate vāle yogasthāna sabase thoḍa़e haiṃ aura sāta ko ādi lekara cāra samaya taka pravartane vāle ūpara-nīce ke donoṃ jagaha sthāna asaṃkhyāta guṇe haiṃ kintu tīna samaya aura do samaya taka pravartane vāle yogasthāna eka jagahaūpara kī ora hī rahate haiṃ aura unakā pramāṇa krama se asaṃkhyāta asaṃkhyāta guṇā hai |
saba yogasthāna jagat śroṇi ke asaṃkhyāta bhāga pramāṇa haiṃ | inameṃ eka-eka sthāna ke 1. avibhāga praticcheda 2. varga, 3. vargaṇā, 4. svaka, 4. guṇahāni, ye pāṃca bheda hote haiṃ |
jisakā dūsarā bhāga na ho, aise śakti ke aṃśa ko avibhāga praticcheda kahate haiṃ | avibhāga praticcheda kā samūha varga varga kā samūha vargaṇā śrargaṇā kā samūha sparddhaka aura sparddhaka kā samūha guṇahāni kahalātā hai aura guṇahāni samūha ko sthāna kahate haiṃ |
ke
eka yogasthāna meṃ guṇahāni kī saṃkhyāe~ palya ke asaṃkhyāta bhāga prabhāṇa haiṃ aura eka guṇahāni meṃ sparddhaka jagatśreṇi ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga pramāṇa haiṃ | eka-eka sparddhaka meṃ vargaṇāoṃ kī saṃkhyā jagat ṇi ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga pramāṇa hai aura eka-eka vargaṇā meṃ asaṃkhyāta jagatpratara pramāṇa varga haiṃ aura eka eka varga meṃ asaṃkhyāta lokapramāṇa avibhāga praticcheda hote haiṃ |

śāmāvaraṇa---sarvaghātī dvandha meṃ āvalī ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga kā bhāga dekara bahubhāga ke pāṃca samāna bhāga karake pāṃca prakṛtiyoṃ ko eka eka bhāga denā cāhie| śeṣa eka bhāga meṃ āvalī ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga kā bhāga dekara bahabhāga matijñānāvaraṇa kī, śeṣa eka bhāga meṃ punaḥ māvalī ke asaṃkhyāta māga kā prāga dekara dūsarā savayāga śrutamānāvaraṇa ko poṣa bhāga meṃ puna: āvalī ke asaṃkhyāta bhāga kā bhāga dekara tīsarā bahumāga avadhijñānāvaraṇa ko, isī taraha cauthā bahubhāga manaparyāyajñānāvaraṇa ko aura śeṣa eka bhāga kevalajñānāvaraṇa ko denā cāhie | pahale ke bhāga meṃ apane apane bahubhāga ko milāne se matijñānāvaraṇa ādi kā sarvadhātī dravya hotā hai|
anantaveṃ bhāga ke sivāya śeṣa bahabhāga dravya deśaghātī hotā hai | yaha deśaghātī dradhya kevalajñānāvaraṇa ke sivāya śeṣa cāra deśaghātī prakṛtiyoṃ ko milatā hai| vibhāga kī rīti pūrva anusāra hai | arthāt deśaghātī drabya meṃ āvalī ke asaṃdhyātaveṃ bhāga kā bhāga dekara eka bhāga ko judā rakhakara śeṣa bahubhāga ke cāra samāna bhāga karake cāroṃ prakṛtiyoṃ ko eka eka bhāga denā cāhie| śeṣa eka bhāga meṃ āvalī ke asakhyāsaveṃ bhāga kā bhāga dekara bahubhāga nikālate hue kramaśaḥ vaha bahubhāga matijñānāvaraṇa, yūta jñānāvaraṇa, avadhijñānācaraṇa aura manaparyāyajñānāvaraṇa ko nambara vāra denā cāhie| apane-apane sarvadhātī aura deśaghāto dravya ko milāne se apane apane sarva dravya kā parimāṇa hotā hai |
darśanāvaraṇa-- sarvaghātī dravya meṃ āvalī ke asaṃkhyāta bhāma kā bhāga dekara eka bhāga ko alaga rakhakara śeṣa bahubhāga ke nau bhāma karake darśanāvaraṇa kī nau

gotra aura aṃtarāya pharma kā bhī pūrvavat hai | yānī nīca gotra kā kama aura ummana gotra kā usase adhika | dānānta rāya kā kama, lābhānta rāya kā usase adhika, bhogānta rāya kā usase adhika, upabhogāmtarāya kā usase adhika aura vīryā. ntarāma kā usase adhika bhāga hai |

pariśiṣṭa-3
guṇaśreṇī karane dvitīyāda aṃta paryanta samayoṃ meṃ samaya-samaya ke prati asaṃkhyātaguṇā krama liye dravya ko apakarṣaṇa karatā hai aura maṃcita arthāta purvokna prakāra udayāni ādi meṃ use nikṣepaṇa karatā hai | aise āyu ke dinā sāta phārmoṃ kā gṛṇī ṇi vidhāna samaya-samaya meṃ hotā hai !
vana kathana kā sārāṃśa yaha hai ki guṇane ṇi racanā jo prakṛtiyo udaya meṃ ā rahī haiṃ unameṃ bhī hotī hai aura jo udaya meṃ nahīṃ ā rahī hai unameṃ bhī hotī hai| antara kevala itanā hī hai ki udayāgata prakutiyoṃ ke dravya kā nikṣepaṇa to udayāvalī, guṇaśreṇī aura ūpara kī sthiti, ina tīnoṃ meṃ hī hotā hai, phintu jo prakṛtiyā~ udaya meṃ nahīṃ hotī haiṃ unake dravya kā sthāpana kevala guṇaṇi aura ūpara kī sthiti meṃ hī hotā hai, udayāyalī meṃ unakā sthāpana nahīṃ hotā hai| āśaya yaha hai ki vartamāna samaya se lekara eka āvalī taka ke samaya meṃ jo niṣeka udaya āne ke yogya haiṃ, unameṃ bo dravya diyā jātā hai, use jadayāvalī meṃ diyā gayā davya samajhanā cāhiye | udayāvalī ke ūpara guṇaśreṇi ke samayoṃ ke barābara jo niṣeka haiṃ, uname jo dṛṣya diyā jātā hai, use gaṇaṇi meṃ diyā gayā samajhanā cāhiye | guṇaśreṇi se ūpara ke aṃta ke kucha nikoṃ ko choḍa़kara śeṣa karmaniṣakoṃ meṃ jo pravya diyā jātā hai, use ūpara kī sthiti meṃ diyā gayā dṛśya samajhanā cāhiye| isako mithyātva ke udāharaṇa dvārā yoṃ samajhanā cāhiye
mithyātva ke dravya meṃ apakarṣaka bhāgāhāra kā bhāga dekara, eka māga vinā bahabhāga pramāṇa dravya to jyoṃ kā tyoṃ rahatā hai, śeṣa eka bhāga ko palya ke asaṃkhyāta bhāga kā bhāga dekara bahubhāga kā sthāpana para kī sthiti meṃ karatā hai| yoṣa eka bhāga meṃ asaṃkhyāta loka kā bhāga dekara guṇaśreṇi āyāma meṃ detā hai, śeṣa eka bhāga udayāvalī meṃ detā hai | isa prakāra guṇaśreṇi racanā ke liye guṇākāla ke aṃtima samaya paryanta asaṃkhyāta guṇe asaṃkhyātaguṇe dravya kā apakarpaṇa karatā hai aura pūrvokta vidhāna ke anusāra udayāvalī, guṇaṇiāyāma aura ūpara kī sthiti meṃ usa dravya kī sthāpanā karatā hai | isa prakāra āyu ke sivāya śeṣa sāta karmoṃ kā guṇaṇi vidhāna jānanā cāhiye |

paṃcama karmagrantha
451
uttarottara pariṇāmoṃ kī viśuddhi kī adhikatā hote jāne ke kāraṇa karmoṃ kī nirjarā asaṃspātaguṇo asaṃkhyātagṛṇī adhika-adhika hotī hai, arthāt jaise-jaise morakarma niḥśeṣa hotā jātā hai vaise-vaise nirjarā bhī baḍha़tī jātī hai aura
yakā dravya pramāṇa asaṃkhyātaguṇā, asaṃkhyātaguṇā adhikādhika hotā jātā hai| phalataḥ vaha jīva mokṣa ke adhika-dhaka nikaṭa pahuṃcatā jātā hai| jahā~ guṇākāra rūpa se guṇita nirjarā kā dravya adhikādhika pāyā jātā hai unako guṇa ṇi kahā jātā hai aura una sthānoṃ meṃ hone vālī nirjarā guṇauṇi nirjarā nahī jātī hai| ___ go0 jīvakāṃḍa gāra 66-67 meṃ ukta dṛṣṭi ko lakṣya meṃ rakhakara guṇa śreṇi kā varṇana kiyā hai| yaha varṇana karmaprakṛti, paṃcasaṃgraha aura karmagrantha se milatā-julatā hai| lekina itanā aṃtara hai ki karmagrantha ādi meṃ samyaktva, deśavira ti, sarvavirati, anantānubaṃdhī kā visaṃyojana, darśanamoha kā kṣapatrā, cāriyamoha kā upaśamaka, upaśāṃtamoha, lapaka, kṣīṇamoha, sayoga kevalī aura ayoga kevalī, ye gyārahaguṇaṇi sthāna batalāye hai | lekina go0 jīvakāṃḍa, tatvārthasūtra, sarvārthasiddhi, tatvārtharājavātika ādi granthoṃ meṃ sayogikevalī aura ayogikavalī ina donoṃ ko alaga-alaga na mānakara jina pada se donoṃ kā grahaṇa kara liyā hai|
‘go. jīvakārḍa kī mūla gāthāoṃ meṃ guṇaśreṇi nirā ke dasa sthāna gināye haiṃ, lekina ṭīkākāra ne gyāraha sthānoṃ kā ullekha karate huye spaṣṭa kiyā hai ki yā to samyaktvotpatti isa eka nāma se sātiśaya micyādṛṣṭi
aura asaṃyata samyāpṭi, isa taraha do medoṃ kā grahaṇa karake gyāraha sthānoṃ kī pūrti kī jā sakatī hai athavā aisā na karake samyaktvotpatti śabda se to eka hī sthāna lenā kintu antima jina śabda se svasthānasthita kevalī aura samṛdghānagata kevalī, ina do sthānoṃ kā grahaṇa kara lenā cāhiye aura svasthānakevalī kī apekṣā samūdayāta gata kevalī ke nirjarā dravya kā pramāṇa asaṃsthānagugā hotā hai|
isa prakāra gyāraha aura dasa guṇaśreṇi sthāna mānane meṃ vivakṣā bheda hai|

pariśiṣṭa
kṣapakaṇi ke vidhāna kā spaṣṭīkaraṇa
śrapakatheṇi meṃ bhaya hone vālī prakṛtiyoṃ ke nāma karmagrantha ke anurūpa āvaśyaka niyukti gā0 121-123 meṃ batalāye haiṃ | gora karmakāṃḍa meṃ śapakaśreṇi kā vidhāna isa prakāra hai
ṇirayatiriksasurāgasate ṇa hi vesasayalabavakhavagā | ayadaścatakkaṃ tu aṇaṃ aṇiyaṭṭīkaraṇacarimamhi || 335 jugavaṃ saṃjogisā puṇovi aṇiyaṭṭikaraṇabahumāgaṃ |
poliya kamaso miccha missaṃ samma khaveṣi phame || 366 arthāt-naraka, tithaṃca aura devāyu ke sasva hone para krama se deśavata, mahāśrata aura bhapaka theṇi nahīṃ hotī, yānī narakāsū kā satva rahate deśavata nahī hote, timaṃcāyu ke satva meṃ mahāvrata nahīṃ hote aura devāyu ke satva meṃ kṣapakaveṇi nahīṃ hotī hai | ata: paka thaṇi ke ārohaka manuṣya ke narakāyu, tiryacāyu aura devāyu kā sattva nahīṃ hotā hai tathā asaṃyata sabhyagdṛṣṭi, deśavirata, pramatta saṃyata athavā apramatta saṃbata manuṣya pahale kī taraha adhaḥkaraṇa, apūrvakaraṇa aura anivṛttikaraṇa nāmaka tīna karaṇa karatā hai | anivṛttikaraṇa ke antima samaya meṃ anaṃtānubandhī krodha, māna, māyā aura lobha kā eka sātha visaṃyojana karatā hai, unheṃ apratyākhyānāvaraṇa ādi bāraha kaṣāyoṃ aura nau nokaṣāya spa pariṇamātā hai aura usake bāda eka antarmuhūrta taka viśrāma karake darśanamoha kā kṣapaṇa karane ke liye punaḥ adhaḥkaraṇa, apūrvakaraṇa aura anivṛttikaraṇa karatā hai | anivṛttikaraṇa ke kāla meṃ se jaba eka bhāga kāla bākī raha jātā hai aura bahubhāga bīta jātā hai, taba kramaśaḥ mithyātva, mina aura samyaktva prakṛti kā kṣapaṇa karatā hai aura isa prakāra kṣāyika sampadṛṣṭi ho jātā hai |
isake bāda cāritramoha kā kṣapaṇa karane ke liye lapaka śreṇi 'para ārohaṇa karatā hai| sabase pahale sātaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ adhaḥkaraṇa karatā hai aura usake bāda āṭhaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ pahuṃca kara pahale kī hī taraha sthiti khaṃḍana, anubhāgakhaṃḍana ādi kārya karatā hai| usake bāda nauveṃ anivṛsikaraṇa guṇasthāna meṃ pahu~ca kara

﻿________________

paṃcama karmagrantha
solachemikā chakka canusekka pāvare ado ekka |
khoṇe sosasa'joge ghāyara tevata te ||337 usake nau bhāgoṃ meṃ meṃ pāṃca bhāgoṃ meṃ krama se solaha, āṭha, eka, eka, chaha prakṛtiyoṃ kā kṣaya hotā hai athavā sattā se vyucchinna hotī hai tathā śeṣa cāra bhāgoṃ meṃ eka-eka hī kī sattā vyucchinna hotī hai | anantara dasaveṃ sūkṣmasaṃparāya guṇamyāna meṃ eka prakṛti kī nyucchitti hotī hai | gyārahaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ yogyatā nahīṃ hone se kisī bhī prakṛti kā viccheda nahīṃ hotā hai aura usake bāda bārahaveṃ jīṇamoha guṇasthāna ke anta samaya meṃ solaha prakṛtiyoṃ kī sattā myucinchanna hotī hai| samogī kevalī guṇasthāna meṃ kisī bhī prakṛti kī vyūjitti nahīṃ hotī aura ayogī kevalī-caudahaveṃ guṇasthāna ke anta ke do samayoṃ meṃ se pahale samaya meṃ 72 tathā dūsare samaya meṃ 13 prakṛtiyoṃ kā viccheda hotā hai|
prakṛtiyoṃ ke viccheda hone kā spaṣṭīkaraṇa isa prakāra jānanā cāhiye ki naurya guṇasthāna ke nau bhāgoṃ meṃ se pahale bhāga meṃ nāmakarma kī 13 prakṛtiyāṃ | narakatika, tiryacaṭika, vikalatrika, ātapa, udyota, ekendriya, sādhāraṇa, sūkṣma, sthāvara tayā darśanāvaraṇa kī 3 prakṛtiyā~ --styānaddhitrika, kula 16 prakṛtiyāṃ kṣapaṇa hotī haiṃ | dūsare bhāga meṃ apratyākhyānāvaraṇa catuṣka, pratyākhyānāvaraṇa catuṣka kula āṭha prakṛtiyoṃ kā, tīsare bhāga meṃ napuṃsaka beda, cauthe bhāga meṃ strīveda, pāṃcaveṃ bhāga meṃ hāsyādi ṣaṭka tathā chaṭhe, sātaveṃ, āṭhaveṃ aura nau bhāga meṃ kramavāḥ puruṣaveda, saṃjvalana krodha, māna, māyā kā kṣapaṇa hotā hai| isa prakāra nauveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ 36 prakṛtiyāṃ byurichanna hotī haiṃ | dasaveṃ sūkṣmasaṃparāya guṇasthāna meṃ saṃjvalana lobha, bārahaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ jñānādharaṇa pāṃca, darśanāvaraṇa cāra, aṃsarāya pāṃca aura nidrā va pracalā, isa prakāra solaha prakṛtiyāṃ kṣaya hotī haiṃ, phira sayogakepalī hokara caudahā guṇasthāna prāpta hotā hai aura usake upāntya samaya meṃ nāma, gotra, vedanīya kī 72 prakṛ. tiyoṃ kā bhaya hotā hai aura anta samaya meṃ 13 prakṛsiyoṃ kā kṣaya ho jāne para mukta taśā prāpta ho jātī hai | jo kṣapaka śreṇi kā prāptavya hai |
ayoga kevalī guṇasthāna ke maṃtra samaya meṃ kinhīṃ-kinhīṃ prācāryoṃ kā mata

﻿________________

pariśiṣṭa-3
hai ki 13 prakṛtiyāṃ kṣaya hotī haiṃ aura kinhīṃ kā mata hai ki 12 prakṛtiyāṃ sā hotī haiṃ| 13 prakṛtiyoṃ kā kṣama mānane vāle apane mata ko isa prakāra spaṣṭa karate haiṃ ki tadbhavamokṣagāmī ke aṃtima samaya meṃ ānupūrvī sahita teraha prakṛtiyoṃ kī gattā utkṛṣṭa rūpa se rahatī hai aura japāya se tīrthakara prakṛti ke sivāya śeṣa bāraha prakuttiyoṃ kī satā rahatī hai| isakā kāraṇa yaha hai ki manuṣyagati ke sātha udā ko prāpta hone vālī mavipānī manuṣyāyu kṣetravipākī manuṣyānapūrvī, jīvadhipāko śeṣa nau prakṛtiyāṃ tathā sātā yā amātā meṃ se koī eka vedanīya', ucca gotra, ye teraha prakṛtiyāṃ samavamokṣagāmī jīva ke aṃtima samaya meṃ bhaya ko prāpta hotī haiṃ, dvicarama samaya meṃ naṣṭa nahīṃ hotī hai| ataḥ tadbhava mokṣagāmī ke aṃtima samaya meṃ utkṛṣṭa teraha prakṛtiyoṃ kī aura anya bāraha prakṛtiyoṃ kī mattā rahatī hai |
lekina caudahave guṇasthāna ke aṃtima samaya meṃ bāraha prakṛtiyoṃ kā kṣaya mānane vāloṃ kā kahanā hai ki manuṣyānupūrvī kā kṣaya vicarama samaya meṃ hī ho jātā hai, kyoṃki usake udaya kā kāma hai| jina prakṛtiyoṃ kā udaya hotā hai, unameṃ stibukasaṃkrama na hone se aṃta samaya meṃ apane-apane svarūpa se unake dalika pāye jāte hai jisase unakā carama samaya meṃ sattāviccheda honā yukta hai| kintu cāroṃ hī ānupūrvī kṣe ṛvipākī hone ke kāraṇa dusare mava ke liye mati karate samaya hī udaya meṃ ātī haiṃ ataḥ bhava meṃ jīva ko unakā udaya nahīṃ ho sakatā hai aura udaya na ho sakane se ayogi avasthā ke dvicarama samaya meṃ hī manuṣyānupūrvī phī sattā kā kṣaya ho jātā hai|
isa prakāra ke matāntara meṃ adhikatara ayogikavalī guṇasthāna ke upāntya samaya meṃ 72 aura aṃta samaya meṃ 13 prakṛtiyoṃ ke kṣaya ko pramukha mānā hai | paṃcama karmagranya kī ṭīkā meṃ 72 + 13 kā hī vidhāna kiyā hai aura go karmakāṃḍa gāta 341 meṃ bhī aisā hī likhā hai-'udayagavāra garāṇū terala carimahi vocchiṇṇā' arthāt udayagata 12 prakṛtiyāṃ aura eka manuṣyānṛpūnī, isa prakāra teraha prakutiyā~ ayogī kevalī ke aṃta ke samaya meṃ apanī sattā se chūṭatī hai|
saṃkṣepa meṃ kṣapaka śreṇi kā yaha vidhāna samajhanā cāhiye|
